# Changelog

## [1.97.1] - 2026-11-13
- **The unverified-signup dead-end is fixed (kiddymoms01@gmail.com, 2026-11-13).** Live audit: the profile existed (`email_verified:false`, created 05:01Z, an OTP was sent then but never used); the 07:14–07:28Z return visit got correct `check` answers (`emailRegistered:true, verified:false`), yet the final `POST /api/onboard` matched the email and took the v1.40.0 "already registered" branch — `WEB_ONBOARD_EXISTING` + `WEB_ONBOARD_DUPLICATE_NOTIFIED`, `already_verified:true`, **no code re-sent, OTP gate unreachable**. `inspectExistingSignup` now returns `'verified'` ONLY for a genuinely verified email match; an unverified match returns **`'unverified'`** (the `'email_exists'` state is retired).
- **New `reissueWebOnboardCode`.** Resumes the SAME account: reuses the open `web_onboard_otps` row (same token, attempts reset) or creates one, carries the **STORED phone** (so verification flips the right profile — a retyped number can never mint a second row), never touches `profiles` (no phone overwrite, no tier/trial change), and audits `WEB_ONBOARD_RESUMED`. `/api/onboard` routes unverified email matches through it, sends the code and returns `{ success:true, onboard_token, profile_created:false }` → the site advances to `#stepOtp`.
- **Frontend silent gate removed.** `main.js` no longer does `if (detailsContinue.disabled) return;` (v1.96.7): Enter-key submits during a pending/failed availability check cancelled with zero feedback. It now shows "Still checking your details — one moment." and re-runs the check, points a verified match at the WhatsApp CTA, or tells the user to check the details — no path returns without a DOM change; a `console.warn` records the blocked submit.
- **Honest refusals + diagnosability.** `legalSubmit` uses the server's `message`/`error` for business refusals (no more misleading "could not reach Saka" copy), and malformed `/api/onboard`/`/api/onboard/check` responses are logged with `console.error('[onboard] …')`.
- **Tests.** `test_web_onboarding` 64 → **72** (unverified email-match states + Test 6b: code/token issued, stored-phone carrier, profile untouched, resume audit, retry reuses the row and resets attempts, verified accounts refused); `test_duplicate_signup` 21 → **25** (the email-match expectation corrected to `verified`, the kiddymoms01 resume state, and route-source pins for `reissueWebOnboardCode` + the removed `email_exists` branch); `test_landing_static` 484 → **489** (Test 26: no silent gate return, visible checking copy + re-check, locked-gate copy, server-message preference, malformed-response logging).
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "a gate that means to guide returns silently"* — the submit handler now always renders a visible outcome (sibling: the legalSubmit refusal path, fixed to honest copy; the availability check's malformed branch, now logged). *(ii) "two endpoints disagree on one fact"* — the check endpoint already said "continue and we will resend"; the submit endpoint now does exactly that; the retired `email_exists` state has no producer left (grep-verified). *(iii) "a resume path rewrites identity"* — the OTP carries the stored phone and `profiles` is never written (pinned).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/webOnboarding.js` (`inspectExistingSignup`, new `reissueWebOnboardCode`), `index.js` (import + `/api/onboard` resume branch), `landing/assets/js/main.js` (submit gate, availability logging, legalSubmit refusal), `test_web_onboarding.js`, `test_duplicate_signup.js`, `test_landing_static.js`, `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.97.1), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§243 + history + version), `docs/` (regenerated), `CHANGELOG.md`.
- **Suites run.** test_web_onboarding (72), test_duplicate_signup (25), test_landing_static (489), test_agent_training, test_doc_sync, test_kb_docs, test_vehicle_documents, `npm run health` — green.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **0 open proposals** verified live — 8 unrelated PENDING friction rows left for their own review), 3/4, 10/11 (stateless suites), 26/29 (no trial/vehicle side effects; identity preserved), 31 (one clear next step, warm copy), 33/43, 47 (verified accounts are still never re-onboarded), 50 (the resume path degrades honestly on a missing OTP row), 51/52 (autopsy + before/after assertions), 54 (live DB evidence drove the fix), 55/56 (evolved the existing handshake; returning and new visitors alike), 2 (architecture + CHANGELOG + landing feed in the same commit). No rule edited. **Impact:** a returning unconfirmed signup now resumes, receives a fresh code and reaches the OTP screen; a blocked submit always explains itself; verified accounts still never re-onboard.

## [1.97.0] - 2026-11-13
- **The `/d/admin` console becomes a full User Management CRM (`/d/admin/profiles`).** The Master List now reads EVERY live profile (owners, drivers and shells) instead of the vehicle-owner filter: `buildAdminProfilesList` merges three reads (profiles `deleted_at IS NULL`, live vehicles, ACTIVE trials) and derives a deterministic status chip (Superadmin / Paid · tier / Past due / Trial · N d left / Trial ended / Free / Shell), masked phone + email, vehicle counts and search by name, email, phone tail and visible digits. The old `/d/admin` owners list stays untouched (it remains the fleet reference, test-pinned). Every view writes `ADMIN_DASHBOARD_VIEW` (`page: profiles|profile`).
- **Profile detail (`/d/admin/profiles/:id`).** One audited page: identity form (name, email, country, units), subscription block (engine entitlements view + Extend trial link + manual tier override + force-verify), fleet table (passbook links), the last 3 `support_tickets` (phone OR email), internal notes, the audit history, force logout, impersonation + email-as-Saka links, and the danger zone. Each optional block degrades independently (rule 50) with an honest `degraded:` banner.
- **Six new POST routes on the v1.91.0 guard stack** (flag gate → generic 404; `isCrossOriginWrite`; `requireRoleSession('admin')`; `ADMIN_WRITE_RATE` 5/5-min composite; server-side typed word; reason 3–300 chars; `logAdminAudit` with actor/targetId/before/after; 303 `?done=` banner):
  - `identity` (typed **UPDATE**) — whitelist ONLY `full_name/email/country/unit_system`; phone, tier, trial and asset identifiers are structurally unreachable. **A changed email defaults `email_verified=false` unless the admin ticks "Force Verify" in the same submit (operator instruction).** Duplicate emails are refused (pre-check + 23505 mapping); a country change stamps `country_source='user'` so `reconcile:country` never fights the operator.
  - `tier` (typed **TIER**) — the new engine-owned `applyManualTierOverride` validates through `isKnownTierKey` (a typo is REFUSED, never silently mapped to Free), flips the profile tier/status/interval, **appends a `subscriptions` ledger line with `source='manual'`** (no invisible edits), invalidates entitlements and marks user data changed. Additive schema: `subscriptions.source text` (`npm run migrate:subscription-source`, idempotent) with the un-migrated retry path.
  - `verify-email` (typed **VERIFY**) — sets `email_verified=true` (audited before/after).
  - `force-logout` (typed **LOGOUT**) — deletes every `dashboard_sessions` row by owner + `admin_impersonations` by target; refuses the admin's own session and records the cleared counts.
  - `wipe` (typed **WIPE**) — reuses `wipeProfileForSignup` + session/impersonation cleanup; refuses self and the superadmin; audited with per-table counts.
  - `note` — the note itself is the reason (3–500 chars), stored as an `ADMIN_PROFILE_NOTE` audit row and rendered back on the page.
  - `GET /d/admin/profiles/:id` is the detail page; `adminNav()` now carries **Profiles**.
- **Latent defect fixed (rule 51 sweep).** `subscriptions.js` called `logWriteFailure` in two catch paths without importing it — a ReferenceError that could mask the very billing failure it meant to record; the import ships with this release.
- **Tests.** `test_dashboard_admin` +62 (100 → **162**): flag-off 404 for all 8 new URLs; cross-origin 403 for all 6 CRM POSTs; Test 13 master list (all profiles incl. shell, search by email/phone, masked phones/emails, audited view, failed-trials degrade); Test 14 detail (identity form, ticket, impersonation/extend links, danger zone, 404, failed-tickets degrade); Test 15 identity (typed word, reason, email reset to unverified, Force Verify override, country_source, whitelist, duplicate email, before/after audit); Test 16 tier (bogus tier refused, lowercase word refused, profile flip, `source='manual'` ledger line, audit, superadmin refusal); Test 17 force logout (sessions deleted, counts audited, self refused); Test 18 wipe (typed word, profile erased, audit, superadmin refused); Test 19 notes + force-verify. `test_dashboard` (936), `test_agent_training` (247 — playbook + core prompt), `test_subscription_billing` (108), `test_trial_engine` (138), `test_profile_wipe` (17), `test_db_reset_scope` (128) — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "a write reads its own before-state after mutating it"* — verify-email and tier now capture the before-snapshot prior to the write (the mock's live row references exposed the class); identity already built its snapshot synchronously. *(ii) "an unknown key is silently defaulted"* — `normalizeTierKey` maps unknown tiers to Free, so the override uses `isKnownTierKey` first; `getTierDefinition` remains a display default by design. *(iii) "a schema carrier exists in only one place"* — `subscriptions.source` added to the schema SQL (CREATE + idempotent ALTER), the migration script and the boot ensure; the engine retries without the column on drift. *(iv) "a helper is used but never imported"* — `logWriteFailure` fixed; its only two call sites were in the same module.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/dashboardAdmin.js` (CRM constants, `buildAdminProfilesList`, `loadAdminProfileRow`, `buildAdminProfileDetail`, `applyAdminIdentityUpdate`, six section renderers, both page renderers, `ADMIN_ACTION_WORDS`, `renderConfirmActionPage` select support, exports), `src/services/subscriptions.js` (`applyManualTierOverride`, `isKnownTierKey` import, `logWriteFailure` import, default export), `src/db/subscriptionSchema.js` (`source`), `scripts/run_subscription_source_migration.js` (new), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (imports + 8 routes), `src/services/dashboard.js` (`adminNav`), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_dashboard_admin.js`, `test_agent_training.js`, `test_user_context_cache.js` (43-site ratchet), `package.json` (1.97.0 + `migrate:subscription-source`), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§242 + history + version), `docs/` (regenerated), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_dashboard_admin (162), test_dashboard (936), test_agent_training (247), test_subscription_billing (108), test_trial_engine (138), test_profile_wipe (17), test_db_reset_scope (128), test_user_context_cache (137 — 43-site ratchet), test_landing_static, test_doc_sync, test_kb_docs, test_vehicle_documents, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run health` — green.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **0 open proposals** verified live — 8 unrelated PENDING friction rows left for their own review), 3/4, 8 (VIN/identity locks untouched), 10/11 (stateless suites), 13 (playbook + core prompt synced; KB docs regenerated), 18/28 (engine-owned overrides; no invented prices), 31 (one next step, typed confirmations), 33/43 (one version commit; deploy on push), 50 (every block degrades + un-migrated ledger retry), 51/52 (autopsy + before/after assertions), 54 (live gate check), 55/56 (evolved the v1.91.0 console; no parallel surface), 2 (architecture + CHANGELOG + landing feed in the same commit). No rule edited. **Impact:** support can now see and manage every account from one audited console; a changed email is re-verified by default; manual plan changes leave a `manual` ledger line; the superadmin account remains untouchable.

## [1.96.9] - 2026-11-13
- **GTM now loads from the first-party host `gtm.sakafleet.com`.** The dynamically injected loader in `landing/assets/js/cookieConsent.js` (`loadGtm()`) now builds `https://gtm.sakafleet.com/gtm.js?id=GTM-TGR68X2X`, replacing `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js`. Consent is untouched: `applyConsent()` still calls `loadGtm()` only when `data.analytics === true`, the one-shot `gtmLoaded` guard and the `GTM-TGR68X2X` container id are unchanged, and the `try/catch` still makes analytics best-effort (a dead proxy can never block the page). No HTML change: this site has never shipped a GTM `<noscript>` iframe, and it must not — a noscript iframe cannot read the localStorage consent gate and would contact the GTM host for every no-JS visitor regardless of opt-in.
- **Live verification (rule 54).** `GET https://gtm.sakafleet.com/gtm.js?id=GTM-TGR68X2X` → **200**, `content-type: application/javascript; charset=UTF-8`, 642,911-byte payload carrying `GTM-TGR68X2X` (verified before any edit). HEAD returns 400 (proxy quirk) — irrelevant to `<script src>` GETs. The previous session correctly stopped at the earlier 404 state and did not ship.
- **Tests.** `test_landing_static` Test 9's domain pin now asserts `gtm.sakafleet.com`; new **Test 25** (+11: 473 → 484) pins the exact loader URL, the untouched `GTM-TGR68X2X` id (and the absence of the `GTM-TGR682X` typo), the `analytics === true` gate, a case-insensitive sweep proving **zero `googletagmanager.com` anywhere under `landing/`**, per-page absence on all six HTML files, and the no-noscript consent guarantee.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class "a hard-coded third-party host duplicated across delivery paths"*: loader (fixed), HTML script/noscript paths (absent by design — per-page + noscript assertions), `main.js` dataLayer pushes (no host — fine), dashboard pages (no GTM — deliberately left), prose/docs (no URLs — the directory sweep proves it). One targeted assertion per sibling.
- **Frames touched.** `landing/assets/js/cookieConsent.js` (header + `loadGtm` URL), `test_landing_static.js` (Test 9 + Test 25), stamped landing assets (`?v=1.96.9`), `landing/data/changelog.json`, `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.96.9), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§241 + history + version), `docs/` (regenerated version header), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_landing_static (484), test_vehicle_documents (83), test_doc_sync (30), test_kb_docs (43), `npm run docs:check`, `npm run health` — green.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **0 open proposals** verified live — 8 unrelated PENDING friction rows left for their own review), 3/4 (targeted reads, batched edits), 10/11 (stateless suites, no test rows), 13 (no intent/flow/field/prompt change — no skill sync triggered), 31 (consent model unchanged), 33/43 (one version commit; deploy on push), 51/52 (sibling sweep + targeted assertions), 54 (live GET + payload verification before the edit; the earlier 404 stop was correct), 55/56 (one-line evolution of the existing loader; all pages re-stamped), 2 (architecture + CHANGELOG + landing feed in the same commit). No rule edited. **Impact:** consented analytics now load from a first-party host, reducing blocker loss; no change to consent behaviour or the data collected.

## [1.96.8] - 2026-11-13
- **The mobile cookie banner is now visible, tappable and above every overlay.** A phone visitor could not submit the website signup: `legalSubmit` (`landing/assets/js/main.js`) correctly gated account creation on `window.__sakaCookies.chosen()`, but the banner it re-opens sat at `z-index: 90` while the onboarding modal backdrop (`.modal-backdrop`) is a fixed, full-viewport overlay at `z-index: 100` — the bar rendered UNDER the dimmed backdrop (a ghost, never clickable), so the form kept asking for a cookie choice with nothing to tap. `bottom: 14px` also had no `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` (the bar hid behind the iOS home indicator / collapsed Safari toolbar) and the one-row `flex-wrap: nowrap` layout squeezed the copy to nothing on 375px phones. `.cookie-banner` is now `z-index: 9999` (above modal 100, settings panel 110, nav 60, drawer 55, launch banner 1) with `bottom: calc(14px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))`.
- **Guided, not alerted.** The gate no longer fires a native `alert()` (which hid the very banner it pointed at): it opens the banner, closes the mobile keyboard (`document.activeElement.blur()`) and shows an inline `#cookieGateErr` line in the legal step naming the bar at the bottom of the screen. `cookieConsent.showBanner()` adds an `attention` class; `@keyframes cookie-pulse` gives three gentle lime beats (removed on any choice, disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`).
- **Phone layout + safe areas.** A new `@media (max-width: 640px)` block wraps the banner (copy and full-width action row on separate rows, 44px tap targets, text unclamped), and both `.modal-backdrop` and `.cookie-settings-backdrop` gain `padding-bottom: calc(20px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))`.
- **Tests.** `test_landing_static` Test 24 (+18: 455 → 473) pins the z-index hierarchy (banner 9999 > nav 60, drawer 55, modal 100, settings 110, launch banner 1 — one assertion per sibling), the safe-area `calc()`, the single base `display:none` (no media query may hide the banner), the phone wrap/full-width/44px rules, the inline gate + keyboard blur (and the dead-end alert's absence), and the pulse with its reduced-motion fallback.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Class "consent UI layered beneath a full-viewport overlay"*: `.modal-backdrop` (safe-area padding added; deliberately below the banner), `.cookie-settings-backdrop` (safe-area padding added; banner hidden by JS while it opens), `.nav`/`.nav-menu`/`.launch-banner` (top-anchored — no safe-area need; z-order pinned by test), dashboard overlays (no cookie banner — deliberately left). *Class "bottom-fixed UI without the iOS safe area"*: both backdrops fixed, banner fixed; one targeted assertion per sibling.
- **Frames touched.** `landing/assets/css/styles.css` (`.cookie-banner`, `.cookie-banner.attention`, `@keyframes cookie-pulse`, reduced-motion media, 640px block, `.modal-backdrop`, `.cookie-settings-backdrop`), `landing/assets/js/cookieConsent.js` (`showBanner`/`hideBanner` attention), `landing/assets/js/main.js` (legalSubmit gate), `landing/index.html` (`#cookieGateErr`), stamped landing assets (`?v=1.96.8`), `landing/data/changelog.json`, `test_landing_static.js` (Test 24), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.96.8), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§240 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_landing_static (473), test_vehicle_documents (83), test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **0 open proposals** verified live — 7 unrelated PENDING friction rows left for their own review), 3/4 (targeted reads, batched edits), 10/11 (stateless suites, no test rows created), 31 (warm, one next step, 44px tap targets), 33/43 (one version commit; deploy on push), 51/52 (sibling sweep + before/after assertions), 54 (live read-only operator check), 55/56 (evolved the existing banner/consent assets; every page re-stamped), 2/13 (architecture + CHANGELOG + landing feed in the same commit). No rule edited. **Impact:** a phone visitor can now see and tap the cookie choice above the signup form, safely above the iOS home indicator, and is told exactly where to look.

## [1.96.7] - 2026-11-12
- **The web signup dead-end is gone (the ghost signup of 2026-11-12 08:57Z).** The ops audit proved one visitor ran four `POST /api/onboard/check` calls in 16 seconds and never submitted; `checkSignupAvailability` logs nothing, so the typed values were unrecoverable — and the UI dead-ended BOTH outcomes: an UNVERIFIED existing account (which the submit path is built to re-serve with a fresh code) kept Continue disabled forever, and a VERIFIED account got a text note with no way forward. `main.js` now allows Continue for unverified matches (the submit path re-sends the verification code) and a verified match swaps the dead note for a real **WhatsApp CTA button** (`registeredWhatsAppCta` → `wa.me/19404407252`). Verified accounts still never re-onboard (rule 47).
- **PII-safe check telemetry.** `/api/onboard/check` now audits `WEB_ONBOARD_CHECK_REGISTERED` with the per-field result + `verified` only — never the typed value — so the next ghost signup is diagnosable while the anti-enumeration contract (20/min, per-field answers) is untouched.
- **Plate escape hatch (stalled user +254728134729, 2026-11-10).** The transcript showed the exact loss: "Nissan tiida hatchback" → the plate question → silence, two nudges, never returned (a fresh web signup with 0 vehicles). `quizEngine.isPlateSkipText()` recognises "no plate yet / don't have it / later / logbook not with me / not sure …" at the plate step, parks the captured answers as a `registration-draft` in the UNIFIED pending slot (24 h TTL, ephemeral, **no schema change**), and replies with exactly ONE next step. The first real plate resumes the flow via `resumeRegistrationDraft()`; a fresh log line yields (never swallowed); *cancel* discards the draft. No vehicle row and no trial exist until a real plate arrives (rules 9/26 intact).
- **Single-question nudge law.** Both live nudges asked for the plate AND offered Restart/Cancel in one message (Sentinel's own `MULTI_QUESTION_PROMPT` flag). The law is now data: `FLOW_GUIDANCE.vehicle_registration` names the "no plate yet" escape, a new `registration-draft` guidance line exists (rule 60), the flow playbook states the escape + resume behaviour, `DEFAULT_SAKA_CORE_PROMPT` carries `PLATE STEP LAW (v1.96.7)`, and the composer carries `PLATE_DRAFT_NOTE`.
- **Tests.** `test_quiz_flow` (+11: 250 — skip phrases, real-plate immunity, park, resume, fresh-log yield, generator/VIN sibling pins), `test_pending_state` (+7: 444 — registry count 14, fixture, fresh-log/cancel/quiz-gate pins, differential exemption documented), `test_agent_training` (+4: 245), `test_landing_static` (+4 net: 455), `test_duplicate_signup` (21) — all green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class A — a client-side gate that blocks a path the server supports*: siblings audited — verified match (fixed with the CTA), unverified match (fixed), network failure (already fails open, pinned), Enter-key submit (guard updated in the same lane), country-code change (re-checks, pinned). *Defect class B — a required question with no escape*: siblings audited — serial step on non-road assets (deliberately left: its resume lane would key on a serial parser, a separate tested lane; pinned so the plate escape never leaks there), VIN step (deliberately left: the logbook-photo path is the offered fallback; `no vin yet` never triggers the plate escape — pinned), optional fields (already skip-able). One targeted assertion per sibling.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/quizEngine.js` (`isPlateSkipText`, `PLATE_DRAFT_PARKED_REPLY`, plate copy + validator, `parkRegistrationDraft`, `resumeRegistrationDraft`, plate branch in `handleQuizTurn`), `src/services/pendingState.js` (`TTL_24H`, `registration-draft` kind, resolver case), `src/routes/whatsapp/quiz.js` (imports + resume lane), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js` (guidance + playbook law), `sakaAi.js` (core prompt law), `src/services/conversationEngine.js` (`PLATE_DRAFT_NOTE`), `landing/index.html` (CTA), `landing/assets/js/main.js` (gate, CTA, copy, verified flag), `index.js` (check telemetry), `test_quiz_flow.js`, `test_pending_state.js`, `test_agent_training.js`, `test_landing_static.js`, `package.json` (1.96.7), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§239 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_quiz_flow (250), test_pending_state (444), test_agent_training (245), test_landing_static (455), test_duplicate_signup (21), test_doc_sync, test_web_onboarding, test_first_message_flow, `npm run health` — green.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **0 open proposals** verified live, 6 unrelated PENDING friction rows left for their own review), 3/4 (targeted reads, batched calls), 10/11 (stateless suites; no test rows created), 13/31/60 (prompt + guidance + composer synced; one question per message; every flow has a guidance line), 26/29 (no trial/vehicle until a real plate; account-bound), 33/43 (one version commit; deploy alerts automatic), 50 (graceful degradation; no schema change), 51/52 (sibling sweep + before/after assertions), 54 (live read-only evidence drove both fixes), 55/56 (evolved existing flows; existing and new users alike). No rule edited. **Impact:** website signups no longer dead-end on a known number/email; a plate-less user can save a draft and resume by sending the plate; plate nudges are single-question.

## [1.96.6] - 2026-11-12
- **Enhanced Conversions on website signup.** The OTP-verified `successful_signup` dataLayer push (`main.js`; v1.49.3 event, name unchanged) now carries first-party data in the SAME event: `user_data: { email, phone_number }` — the GTM pattern (the container's GA4 `sign_up` / Google Ads tags read a data layer variable named `user_data`; a standalone `gtag('set','user_data')` is impossible because the site ships no raw gtag.js — GTM-TGR68X2X is the single consent-gated hub since v1.49.0). Values ride unhashed per Google's documented option; the Google tag hashes with SHA-256 in the browser before transmission.
- **Formats are guaranteed, not assumed.** Two pure normalisers re-check the form values at push time: `enhancedEmail()` trims + lowercases (drops anything that is not an address); `enhancedPhone()` strips spaces/dashes/parens and accepts ONLY `^\+[1-9]\d{7,14}$` — E.164 exactly (`+254712345678`). A national-format number (`0712345678`) or one without its leading `+` is omitted, never guessed; the event still fires when neither value exists.
- **Consent scope v3 + disclosure (rule 56).** Passing hashed email/phone to Google for ad measurement widens what "Analytics & marketing" means, so `cookieConsent.js` VERSION 2 → 3 forces a fresh opt-in; GTM still loads only after `analytics === true`, and the loader now queues `{ event: 'consent_analytics_granted', analytics: true }` for Consent Mode (defaults granted are accurate — the container never exists without consent; no GTM = no transmission). The settings sentence on ALL six pages, `privacy.html` §11 (new Enhanced Conversions paragraph naming SHA-256 + E.164) and `llms.txt` disclose it; `landing/data/changelog.json` gains a plain-language entry.
- **Tests.** `test_landing_static` Test 23 (+24: 429 → 453) lifts both normalisers out of `main.js` and runs them in a `node:vm` sandbox — behavioural, not source-matching: `"  Grace.W@Example.COM  "` → `grace.w@example.com`; `"+254 712-345-678"` → `+254712345678` (asserted against `/^\+[1-9]\d{7,14}$/`); `"0712345678"` / `"254712345678"` → omitted. Plus source assertions for the combined push, consent v3 + the consent event, and the disclosure on every page/file.
- **Frames touched.** `landing/assets/js/main.js` (`enhancedEmail`, `enhancedPhone`, `trackSignup`), `landing/assets/js/cookieConsent.js` (VERSION 3, consent event, header), `landing/{index,privacy,terms,status,data-deletion,404}.html` (analytics settings copy), `landing/privacy.html` §11, `landing/llms.txt`, `landing/data/changelog.json`, stamped landing assets (`?v=1.96.6`), `test_landing_static.js`, `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.96.6), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§238 + history + version), this file.

## [1.96.5] - 2026-11-11
- **A receipt photo is never a logbook again (+254734852529, KAS628T).** The live audit (rule 54) proved the exact turn: the fuel quiz was open at "How much did you spend?", the Astrol e-TIMS receipt photo arrived, the OCR read every line correctly — but the document classifier said **logbook** because the receipt's bare control-unit serial `KRACU0300578490` tripped the VIN heuristic before the fuel branch was reached. So no `receipts` row was ever written, the confirmed KES 3,000 / 13.47 L expense saved with `receipt_id = NULL`, and `/d/records` showed "Receipt: —". Worse: the photo was vaulted as a **logbook document and superseded the genuine logbook file** in the vault (six re-sends = six junk rows). **Fixes:** a strong-fiscal guard now sits ABOVE the logbook branch in `classifyOcrDocument` (`START/END OF LEGAL RECEIPT`, `FISCAL RECEIPT`, `TAX INVOICE`, `TOTAL TAX`, `TAXABLE`, `BUYER PIN` + fuel evidence → `receipt_fuel`); the Document Lens demotes a logbook base with the same markers (the v1.87.5 sibling-guard pattern); a shared `stripFiscalSerials()` strips labelled PINs, `CU:` serials and bare `KRACU…` invoice numbers before any VIN scan (so `vin: P051141264M` can never be minted); `resolveReceiptVault` refuses to vault a fiscal receipt as a compliance document (incoming guard — a genuine logbook can never be superseded by a receipt again) and never builds a logbook prefill from one.
- **A receipt sent mid-quiz now links to that very draft.** The v1.81.0 prefill seam only carried the receipt id into a NEW quiz (`!session.quiz`), so the live photo — sent while the amount question was open — never reached the draft. New pure `injectReceiptLinkIntoDraft()` banks the id in an ALREADY OPEN `log_expense` draft (engine-only, never asked/rendered/AI-injectable, `restart` still drops it, the write path still re-verifies ownership). A registration/compliance draft is deliberately never hijacked (decision D5, operator-approved — hijacking would destroy user progress).
- **Two money/currency siblings caught while proving the fix.** (A) The lens read the tax token `E-8%` as an amount (**8** instead of 3,000) — `amountFrom` now rejects percentage tokens, never trusts a tax-component line's next value, and scans the fiscal-stripped text with date/time exclusions. (B) `detectCurrencyFromText` matched the alphabetic symbol `RM` (MYR) inside "SCU **INFORMATION**" — alphabetic symbols now require their own word (`R$`-style punctuation symbols keep the historical substring behaviour). The live receipt now reads KES 3,000, vendor ASTROL PETROLEUM COMPANY, date 2026-11-10.
- **Live reconciliation (rule 56).** David's six misclassified logbook rows (`a5053731`, `14fa83df`, `ca458a9e`, `0eef23d2`, `985dea35`, `12df7fc3`) deleted with their `documents` bucket objects; the genuine logbook `bca7003f` restored as current; the 11-11 receipt re-vaulted from Meta media `987949260501656` and linked to `expense_logs.863aed9a…` — the records page now renders **View** and the vehicle page shows only the real files.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class: "a weaker heuristic branch runs before a stronger labelled marker"* — the exact v1.87.5 class, now swept for receipts: classifier precedence (fixed), Lens demotion (fixed — the licence demotion's missing sibling), `looksLikeVin`/`parseLogbook` VIN heuristics (hardened with the shared strip), document-vault intake (fixed — receipts can never enter `vehicle_documents`), mid-quiz prefill (fixed), compliance link at save (audited — mechanically correct, the pollution entered at intake), quick-log lane (deliberately left — text-only by construction), CSV import (no media path), dashboard render (correct — `—` was truthful). *Class: "a number/symbol read from the wrong place"* — tax-token amount (fixed), fraction-in-word currency (fixed), PIN-as-VIN (fixed).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/ocrService.js` (`classifyOcrDocument`, new `stripFiscalSerials`, `looksLikeVin`), `src/services/documentLens.js` (`classifyDocumentFamily`, `parseLogbook`, `amountFrom`), `src/services/ocrNormalizer.js` (`detectCurrencyFromText`), `src/routes/whatsapp/media.js` (imports, `resolveReceiptVault` fiscal guard + logbook prefill guard), `src/routes/whatsapp/quiz.js` (new `injectReceiptLinkIntoDraft`, `arbitrateQuizTurn`), `test_ocr_flow.js` (+14), `test_quiz_flow.js` (+5), `test_dashboard.js` (+2), `test_ocr_normalizer.js` (+2), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.96.5), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§237 + history + version), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_ocr_flow (164), test_quiz_flow (240), test_dashboard (936), test_ocr_normalizer (40), test_vehicle_documents (83), `npm run docs:check`, `npm run health` — green.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **0 open proposals**), 44/54 (live DB + transcript audit and an offline reproduction against the live tree before any edit), 51/52 (sibling sweep + before/after assertions), 55 (evolved the existing classifier/lens/vault/prefill — nothing rebuilt), 56 (live rows reconciled + verified), 36 (generic fiscal markers only; no country parsing leaked), 50 (every guard degrades, never throws), 33/43 (single-version commit, deploy on push), 13/2 (docs + architecture + landing feed in the same commit). ## [1.96.3] - 2026-11-10
- **Operator template tools patch — three defects found on the first LIVE test-fire (22:03 & 22:15) and the 09:37 button taps.** Only the operator surfaces change; no customer-facing flow, prompt or schema is touched.
- **D1 — `admin tpl test all` re-fired the first five kinds forever.** `runTemplateTestFire` always started at `TEMPLATE_TEST_KINDS[0]`, so every run sent `service_reminder, expiry_reminder, trial_ending, trial_grace, dunning` and stopped at the 5-send window; `re_engagement` and the remaining 13 kinds were unreachable through `all`. **Fix:** a module-level `all` cursor keyed to the OPERATOR phone (1-hour TTL, per Cloud Run instance like the rate window) that records every kind that actually returned a wamid. The next run slices the registry from the first unfired kind; a completed 18/18 sweep wraps to the top; refused kinds (`unset_knob`, blocked, rejected) never advance it and stay retryable. The reply reports `Session progress: N/18` and says the next run resumes at the first remaining kind. No new syntax — repeating the same command walks the whole registry.
- **D2 — the admin gate swallowed mapped button taps.** A tap resolved to `fuel` / `accept` / `dashboard` still entered `handleAdminIntercepts`; from the superadmin phone `parseSuperadminCommand` returned `UNKNOWN`, the Admin AI Agent picked no tool, and the hub replied "🤔 I'm not sure which admin action that is." **Fix:** `messages.js` passes `isInteractiveTap: Boolean(messageObj?.interactive || messageObj?.button)` and `handleAdminIntercepts` returns `null` first — the care console AND the superadmin hub step aside for ANY tap shape (recognized or A1-diagnosed), so the resolved phrase reaches the normal Saka lanes exactly as it does for a customer. Typed text still enters both gates (pinned).
- **D3 — `admin tpl buttons` was blind to the target phone.** The audit hardcoded the admin's own number, so taps from the device the test was fired at were invisible. **Fix:** `admin tpl buttons [target_phone]` (deterministic parse; a junk token is refused downstream, never sent to the AI classifier). `runTemplateButtonAudit` normalises with the SAME `normalizeWhatsAppTargetPhone`, queries THAT phone's `chat_history`/`error_logs` rows, returns `invalid_target` with zero reads for a malformed number, and the digest header names the audited number masked (`Auditing: +2547****2529`). The `ADMIN_TEMPLATE_BUTTONS` audit row gains `audited_phone` + `status`.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "an operator-triggered loop forgets its place across invocations"* → sibling `all` pacing (fixed with the cursor); the 5/10-min rate window is time-keyed by design and was left unchanged. *(ii) "a gate that means to catch typed text answers an explicit choice"* → siblings: the care-console leg and the admin-hub fallback inside the same intercept (both fixed by one early return), the A1 unrecognized-tap diagnostic (now bypasses too — it never gets a hub reply), typed admin commands (deliberately unchanged, pinned). *(iii) "a read scoped to the caller instead of the subject"* → `admin tpl test [target_phone]` (already target-correct since v1.96.1); `admin tpl buttons [target_phone]` (fixed); `admin wipe <phone>` audited and left (its phone is already the subject).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/templateTestFire.js` (cursor consts/map/helpers, `runTemplateTestFire`, `formatTemplateTestReply`, `runTemplateButtonAudit`, `formatTemplateButtonDigest`, exports), `src/services/superadmin.js` (`parseSuperadminCommand`, `ADMIN_TEMPLATE_BUTTONS` dispatch, help registry), `src/routes/whatsapp/messages.js` (intercept call), `src/routes/whatsapp/admin.js` (`handleAdminIntercepts`), `test_template_testfire.js` (70 → 88), `test_interactive_reply.js` (70 → 75), `test_superadmin.js` (+5 parse/help assertions), `docs/TEMPLATES.md` (§6), `package.json` (1.96.3), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§234.2 + history + version), this file.

## [1.96.2] - 2026-11-10
- **Live chat audit fix release — +254734852529 (David Njonjo).** A deep read of the full conversation (322 messages + transcripts), the ledger, receipts, transfers, reminders and the 12 friction rows found **five live defects**. Four older ones were verified already fixed at HEAD and were deliberately NOT re-fixed (rules 42/44): the launch-award copy (v1.87.0 — this exact account was that fix's evidence), nickname editing (v1.89.2), the receipt link + KE fiscal amount (v1.87.4) and the transfer cancel write (v1.87.5). The driver compliance card was proved correct live — it lists **both** the licence and the PSV badge.
- **D1 — a receipt photo is NEVER answered as a ledger query (highest user impact).** Live 2026-11-10: five photo-only re-sends of the ASTROL Runda receipt were answered *"Nothing under fuel + repairs expenses for 10 Nov 2026 yet — send the next one and I'll keep it here."* The records lane claimed the OCR dump before the receipt lane could speak (the receipt had no vault row, so `receiptQuizStartFor` returned null). **Fixes:** `parseRecordQuestion` now returns `document-evidence` for a legal-receipt/invoice/registration-certificate body (rule 36/37) so evidence can never become a query; `receiptQuizStartFor` now opens the confirmation quiz for **every FRESH receipt**, vaulted or not, and keeps only a **DUPLICATE** out (the v1.87.4 no-second-draft law, now expressed with an explicit `isDuplicateReceipt` fact). `messages.js` passes that fact.
- **D2 — "Add fuel record" is a LOG command, never a read.** Live 2026-11-10: *"Add fuel record"* was answered *"Fuel all time: Ksh 3,000 (1 record) · 13.51 L"* while the sibling *"record fuel"* correctly opened the log quiz. New `LOG_COMMAND_RE` in `parseRecordQuestion` (`add|log|save|record|enter|capture|create` + what to log → `log-command`), and `applyPostAiIntentFixes` routes the reason to `LOG_EXPENSE` (earnings words to `LOG_EARNINGS`).
- **D3 — a non-ledger delete can never execute a pending ledger delete (highest severity, rule 37).** Live 2026-11-09: the user opened *"Delete last entry"* (a fuel preview), then typed *"No. Delete last compliance"*. The stale delete prompt survived, the model invented a *"Delete the last compliance entry?"* card, and the next *"Delete"* **removed the fuel row** (the user restored it). New pure `detectNonLedgerTarget()` + a `blockedTarget` turn: a compliance/reminder target **drops** the stale prompt and is answered deterministically (compliance dates are facts — correct them with the right date; reminders are not ledger rows); receipt/document/vehicle targets simply unblock their own lanes. A bare `DELETE` and a numbered `delete 2` still work (pinned).

- **D4/D5 — money is engine-owned and law-abiding.** Two live amount errors: *"Paid 300 parking … on 7th, 8th and 9th of November 2026"* became **KES 315** (300 + 7 + 8 — bare ordinals survived the date mask and "and" read as additive), and *"Price of diesel 222.7kes/liter"* became the **spend** with **1 L** (the nozzle number) as the volume. **Fixes:** `moneyLexicon` masks bare ordinals (`7th`) and skips `st|nd|rd|th` tails; `parseMoneyStory` now outranks the AI in the quick-log lane and an AI amount that is only a unit price is rejected (`isUnitPriceOnly`); `extractVehicleAttributes` keeps a per-litre figure as `unit_price`, never `amount`; the quiz rejects AI-extracted litres on a document dump unless the text carries real litre evidence (`13.47 X 222.70` yes, `NOZZLE: 1` no).
- **D6 — an open draft never claims a save.** Live 2026-11-10: *"Saved the fuel expense. ✓ How many litres did you put in?"* while nothing was written. New pure `stripPrematureSaveClaim()` applied to every non-completed quiz reply; completed flows keep their verified "Logged ✓" card.
- **D7 — a complaint is never a fresh draft.** *"Kindly ensure this never happens again. Never miss to link a receipt"* was answered *"What kind of expense are we logging…"*. The complaint detector gains the narrow "never happens again / never miss to link / you saved it wrong" family, and the post-AI fix resets the intent so no expense quiz starts.
- **D8 — "Cancel passbook transfer" can never START a handover.** Live 2026-11-09: it opened the buyer-number question, then hit "a transfer is already running" while the real transfer stayed in flight (fixed on the dashboard in v1.87.5, never on WhatsApp). New deterministic read lane `transfer_cancel_hint` answers honestly (names the vehicle + the dashboard cancel, or says nothing is pending) and **yields** to a live transfer flow / buyer-OTP sink.
- **Live reconciliation (rules 54/56).** `receipts.f4d9b896…amount` corrected **11,823 → 3,000** (the ASTROL P.O. Box was read as the total) through the new idempotent `scripts/reconcile_receipt_amounts.js` (`--scan` / `--receipt --amount --reason [--apply]`), audited `RECEIPT_AMOUNT_CORRECTED`; the scan now reports **0 linked mismatches**. All **9 PENDING friction rows** were closed through `markFrictionDeployed` with per-row resolutions (each names the release that fixed it, or the detector artifact it was).

- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "evidence text re-enters an intent lane"* → records lane (fixed with the document guard), duplicate/fresh receipt decision (fixed), complaint lane (fixed), transfer-cancel lane (fixed), fuel-volume extraction (fixed). *(ii) "a confirmation whose executor does something else"* → the ledger pending guard (fixed) and the pause/composer card source (the deterministic guard now answers first). *(iii) "a number read from the wrong place"* → quick-log amount (fixed), narrative money story (fixed), extractor unit price (fixed), receipt litre evidence (fixed). Deliberately left: the AI reader may still extract a *typed* volume without a unit ("filled 40") — the guard only applies to document dumps, so forgiving typed input keeps working.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/{moneyLexicon,recordAnswers,entryAnswers,readCommands,quizEngine}.js`, `src/routes/whatsapp/{quiz,messages,commands,conversation,helpers}.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `scripts/reconcile_receipt_amounts.js` (new), `package.json` (1.96.2 + `reconcile:receipt-amounts`), `test_record_answers.js` (+3), `test_natural_intake.js` (+3), `test_entry_ledger.js` (+7), `test_read_registry.js` (+7), `test_quiz_flow.js` (+7), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§235 + history + version), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), this file.

## [1.96.1] - 2026-11-10
- **`admin tpl test` gains an optional target phone.** `admin tpl test [kind|all] [target_phone]` now fires the REAL template to a regular device (e.g. `admin tpl test re_engagement 0734852529`); without a target it still goes to `SUPERADMIN_PHONE`.
- **Normalisation + refusals.** New pure helpers in `metaMessenger.js`: `normalizeWhatsAppTargetPhone` (digits only; a leading `00` is an international prefix; a leading `0` with exactly 10 digits resolves to Kenya `+254…`; 9–15 digits or `null`) and `maskWhatsAppTargetPhone` (`+2547****2529`). An invalid target is a deterministic `invalid_target` refusal — zero sends, one audit row, a clear reply with an example.
- **Security/cost.** The 5-sends/10-min sliding window is now keyed to the OPERATOR's phone (`adminPhone`), never the target, so switching devices can never widen the cap; every `ADMIN_TEMPLATE_TEST` row records the target masked to its last 4 digits; the reply header names the masked target. The delivery blocklist still applies to the actual recipient.
- **Deterministic parsing.** `parseSuperadminCommand` uses a two-token match: `admin tpl test` (bare) still prints usage, `admin tpl test <kind> <target>` carries both, a phone without a kind stays usage, and a malformed target never falls to the AI classifier. The `side-effect-free` import pin (probe imports only `proactiveTemplates.js` + `metaMessenger.js`) is unchanged.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "an identifier is trusted from a free-text admin command"* → the target is normalised in one pure helper, refused when implausible, and masked in every audit row; *(ii) "a limit follows the artifact instead of the actor"* → the window key moved to the operator (pinned); *(iii) "a strict admin regex pushes malformed input to the model"* → the parser now claims every `admin tpl test …` shape deterministically (pinned).

## [1.96.0] - 2026-11-10
- **Operator template tools — fire a real template, then audit every reply button.** Two superadmin-only, deterministic, zero-token commands. Neither changes any customer flow, template kind, env knob or schema.
- **`admin tpl test [kind|all]`.** Sends a REAL approved Meta template to `SUPERADMIN_PHONE` (sender and recipient are the superadmin) so the operator can see it arrive. `TEMPLATE_TEST_FIXTURES` (new, 18/18 pinned) supplies one sample variable set per kind; `TEMPLATE_SUGGESTED_NAMES` names the expected template when a knob is unset — the code never invents a name. `all` paces the registry, reports per-kind outcome and stops at the window with "cooling down — run again in N minutes". Reply copy is honest: `sent — wamid X`, `rejected by Meta — no wamid`, `delivery blocked (no wamid)`, `credentials missing (no wamid)`, and the delivery-status line points at Cloud Logging / the Meta console (never a DB trail). Sender = recipient = the superadmin; 30-minute session gate inherited by dispatch position; one `ADMIN_TEMPLATE_TEST` audit row per attempt; `SUPERADMIN_PHONE` unset refuses gracefully with nothing sent.
- **Probe wraps the ONE transport.** New `sendWhatsAppTemplateProbe` calls the SAME internal `postWhatsAppMessage`, which gains an optional `capture` sink (parsed 2xx body → wamid). `sendWhatsAppText` / `sendWhatsAppTemplate` keep their 2-arg calls and byte-identical boolean contracts (pinned). The blocklist (`isDeliveryBlocked`) runs BEFORE any network call; a blocked recipient sends nothing and still writes the audit row with the block reason. `templateTestFire.js` imports ONLY `proactiveTemplates.js` + `metaMessenger.js` (source-pinned) — it cannot stamp a nudge, a deploy marker or a queue row.
- **Rate limit.** ≤5 sends / 10 minutes per phone via a module-level sliding-window `Map` (mirroring `processedWebhooksCache`). Per-instance on purpose: the command is operator-triggered only, the per-phone serial queue means one operator fires one command at a time, and a second Cloud Run instance can only add one extra audited test-fire to the superadmin's own number.
- **`admin tpl buttons`.** Deterministic `captured → declared → mapped` digest: declared from the registry (3 button-bearing kinds) plus the new frozen `TEMPLATE_BUTTON_TITLES` (operator-approved titles; the auth_otp URL button declares none), mapped from `BUTTON_TITLE_PHRASES` (7), and captured from `error_logs` route `/webhook/whatsapp/interactive` (unrecognized shapes; the phone is folded into `error_message` because `error_logs` has no phone column) plus `chat_history` rows whose `message` EXACTLY equals a mapped target phrase (no substrings). Every tap without a named claimant is flagged. Audited `ADMIN_TEMPLATE_BUTTONS`.
- **Structural change (boundary preservation).** `BUTTON_TITLE_PHRASES` + `BUTTON_PAYLOAD_PHRASES` moved from the route leaf `src/routes/whatsapp/interactive.js` to the pure service `src/services/interactiveReply.js` (the §190 boundary forbids a service importing a route module; the audit is a service consumer). The leaf re-exports the SAME frozen objects byte-identically, so every existing importer and test pin passes unchanged.
- **Phrase-reachability law (pinned).** One assertion per `BUTTON_TITLE_PHRASES` entry against its real claimant: `reminders`/`reminder`/`remind me` → `readCommands` lane `reminders_list`; `set reminder` → `quizEngine.detectQuizStart` `set_reminder`; `log expense` → `log_expense`; `dashboard` → the deterministic dashboard sign-in lane. **The `fuel` value has NO deterministic claimant** (it reaches the `log_expense` quiz only through the AI intent path) — pinned as a documented design gap; per operator decision (a) it is flagged, not fixed, in this release. The payload-phrase siblings were swept too (reminders / help / keep all / subscription).
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "one transport grows a second return contract"* → the capture sink lives on the single internal POST; both boolean senders pinned to 2-arg calls with boolean returns. *(ii) "an inbound button resolves to a phrase no live lane claims"* → all 7 title phrases + 4 payload phrases pinned to named claimers; the one gap named and pinned. *(iii) "an admin probe stamps production state"* → the probe module's imports are source-pinned to the registry + transport, and it never calls a boolean sender.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/proactiveTemplates.js` (fixtures, suggested names, button titles), `src/services/metaMessenger.js` (capture sink + `sendWhatsAppTemplateProbe`), `src/services/templateTestFire.js` (new), `src/services/interactiveReply.js` (registries moved in), `src/routes/whatsapp/interactive.js` (re-export), `src/services/superadmin.js` (parse, `ADMIN_INTENTS`, help registry, dispatch + audits), `test_proactive_templates.js` (84 → 93), `test_template_testfire.js` (new, 59), `test_interactive_reply.js` (59 → 70), `test_superadmin.js` (+8 pure assertions), `scripts/health_audit.js` (stateless suite registered), `docs/TEMPLATES.md` (§6 operator runbook), `docs/` regenerated, `package.json` (1.96.0), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§234 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_proactive_templates (93), test_template_testfire (59), test_interactive_reply (70), test_superadmin (integration, green), test_sentinel_policy, test_notifications, test_customer_care, test_doc_sync, test_env_audit, `npm run docs:check`, `npm run health` — green.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (consult-first; live check: **0 open proposals**), 2, 3/4, 10/11, 12/13/33, 18 (no `.github/`), 31, 43, 44, 50, 51/52, 54, 55, 58. No rule edited. **Decision impact:** two new superadmin commands; no customer-facing behaviour changes. Operator decision **(a)**: the `fuel` gap is documented and pinned, not fixed.

Decisions: **D1** drawer at ≤1024px (strip above); **D2** impersonation banner outside the drawer (z-index 60 > 50, DOM order pinned); **D3** lane switch = session field update; **D4** tips at T+12h/T+48h in the trial sweep; **D5** re-engage on `chat_history` 14+ days; **D6** `saka_reengage` Utility; **D7** `saka_feature_tip` Utility; **D8** both knobs OFF (off-state byte-identical); **D9** one feature/user, max 2 sends, skip paid-live; **D10** playbook line only.
- **Task 1 — the drawer.** `renderPageShell` renders a 48px `.nav-burger` (ARIA) + a `role="dialog"` `.nav-menu` drawer + one inline IIFE that clones the SAME `.nav` anchors (no duplicated markup), publishes `--bar-h`, closes on Escape/backdrop/link/resize>1024 and removes the burger on the login doors. `DASHBOARD_CSS` gains the drawer + `@media (max-width:1024px)` block; reduced-motion renders the finished state. The impersonation banner (unchanged) always renders BEFORE the drawer (test-pinned).
- **Task 2 — the dual-role toggle.** New `POST /d/lane/switch`: flag gate → Origin → 5/5-min limit → session → **impersonation refused** ("Exit impersonation to switch lanes") → `resolveDashboardRole` (both lanes must exist) → the SAME sign-in gates (owner: live trial/paid; driver: live assignment) → updates `dashboard_sessions.session_role` on the caller's own row → 303 to `/d` or `/d/driver`. `requireRoleSession` attaches `request.sakaLane`; a second `onSend` hook injects the Owner · Driver strip only for genuinely dual-role sessions and never while impersonating. `loadAssignedVehicles` + validated `?vehicle=` on the three driver routes (403 outside the assignments) feed a Vehicle picker when >1 assignment. No schema change; the 403 matrix is untouched.
- **Task 3 — the drip-feed.** `trial_sessions.feature_nudge_sent_at / feature_nudge_followup_sent_at / feature_nudge_key` (`npm run migrate:trial-feature-nudge`, boot DDL + provisioning + scope pins). New OFF-by-default stage in `runTrialLifecycleSweep`: ONE tip at T+12h after activation for the FIRST unexplored feature (dashboard / expense / voice note / report / compliance, resolved from canonical ledgers), ONE follow-up at T+48h only while that feature stays unexplored, all-explored or engaged → stamped without sending, per-sweep cap 10, paid-live skipped, un-migrated columns send nothing (rule 50). New `feature_tip` template kind (`META_TPL_FEATURE_TIP`, {{1}} feature, {{2}} link) + TEMPLATES.md entry 18.
- **Task 4 — the re-engage template.** `processSilentReengagement` now reads the LAST `chat_history` row (14+ days quiet; the 13-15 `chat_transcripts` band retired). New `REENGAGE_TEMPLATE_ENABLED` (default off): ON → kind `re_engagement` + first name with a guaranteed `there` fallback (template `saka_reengage`, now Utility with the D6 body); OFF → kind/vars `null`, copy byte-identical to v1.16.3. Dedupe stamp, cap 10, superadmin skip and active-vehicle requirement unchanged.
- **Rule 13/60 sync.** `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK` gains the dual-role toggle + vehicle-picker line; `test_agent_training` +1 (241). `.env.example` documents all five new knobs; `envAudit` registers them; `docs/TEMPLATES.md` carries entries 6 (updated) + 18 (new) with the Meta submission/rollback runbook.

## [1.94.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 31 — website overhaul: one menu, one billing switch, one brand door.** Six landing items plus the dashboard logo target, one commit. **No schema change, no new dependency, no flow/prompt change** (so no `docs:gen`). Rule 42/63 gate: **0 open remediation proposals**. Operator decisions: **D1** drawer at 1280px; **D2** 30px logo + square mark at ≤480px; **D3** header CTA folds into the drawer at ≤960px (Log In stays); **D4** duplicate ghost Pricing removed; **D5** lowercase "get"; **D6** impersonation brand stays `/d`; **D7** monthly default, `saka_pricing_interval`; **D8** only the live slot counter remains.
- **Task 1 — the approved launch copy.** The banner now reads exactly *"🎉 Launch offer — Sign up and activate trial to get an extended 6-month trial, zero payment."* plus the live `#launchSlots` pill; the `launchSlotsTotal`/`launchAwardDays` spans are gone (award-day analytics still ride the API body), and the opt-in label + dynamic hero note speak the same offer (6-month when the award is ≥180 days).
- **Task 2 — the banner on phones, for real.** The banner already stacked at ≤640px and stayed inside the sticky header; the actual defect was the drawer's hardcoded 104px top padding, which hid its first items behind a taller banner header. `nav.js` now publishes the real header height (`syncHeaderOffset` → `--nav-h`, re-synced on resize and on the `saka:nav-sync` event `main.js` fires when the banner appears/disappears); `.nav-menu` uses `calc(var(--nav-h, 68px) + 36px)`. The banner wraps (`overflow-wrap: anywhere`) and `.nav` gains `translateZ(0)` for iOS sticky.
- **Task 3 — one menu, one logo.** Burger/drawer breakpoint moved **960 → 1280px** (CSS + resize close); the five anchors show only above 1280. One **30px** logo height at every width (the 34/28/22 chain is retired); at **≤480px** the square `logo-mark.png` replaces the 9.9:1 lockup (the only physical way 375px fits the logo, Log In and the burger) and the same markup ships on the legal/status pages. The duplicate ghost "Pricing" CTA is removed; `.nav-cta .btn-primary` hides at ≤960 (the drawer carries it) while `.nav-login` stays visible at every width (v1.86.0 decision). The footer gains a Support `mailto:` link — the nav stays five items (FAQ already there).
- **Task 4 — monthly ⇄ annual with one switch.** A two-option radio-pill group above `.pricing` (Monthly default, Annual · save 30%, `aria-live="polite"`) drives **`renderPricing(interval)`**: monthly → `$9.99 / per month`; annual → `$83.92 / per year · save 30%`; Free stays `$0 / forever`; the add-on note appends "(add-ons are monthly)". The choice persists in `localStorage['saka_pricing_interval']` (blocked storage ignored) and local-currency pricing renders through the same function. `data-usd-monthly`/`data-usd-annual`/`data-addon-usd` are unchanged (tier-registry pins keep passing); annual is exactly 70% of 12×monthly; JSON-LD stays monthly USD.
- **Task 5 — "no card" → "no payment".** Meta description, OG description, FAQ JSON-LD, hero note, pricing sub, FAQ, CTA sub, modal legal summary, `main.js`, `terms.html` and `llms.txt`. **Untouched:** the `twitter:card` meta NAME, privacy's factual "full card number" processor sentence, and the historical `landing/data/changelog.json`. A guard test scans every landing HTML/JS/txt for `/no card|no-card|credit card/i` and requires zero hits.
- **Task 6 — the logo always goes somewhere sensible.** `renderPageShell` gained `{ brandHref = '/d' }`; `/d/login` and `/d/admin/login` pass `https://sakafleet.com`, and the two signed-out 401 branches in `requireRoleSession` do too. Signed-in pages keep `/d`; **impersonation keeps `/d`** (the as-cookie resolves to the target owner's overview — D6), with the banner and Exit unchanged.
- **Frames touched.** `landing/{index,privacy,terms,status,data-deletion}.html`, `landing/llms.txt`, `landing/assets/css/styles.css`, `landing/assets/js/main.js`, `landing/assets/js/nav.js`, `landing/data/changelog.json`, `src/services/dashboard.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `test_landing_static.js` (+33: 429), `test_dashboard.js` (+4: 900), `test_dashboard_admin.js` (+1: 93), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.94.0), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§232 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_landing_static (429), test_dashboard (900), test_dashboard_admin (93), test_vehicle_documents, test_doc_sync, test_env_audit, test_kb_docs, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "a breakpoint change leaves a second, stale breakpoint behind"* → the redundant ≤640 `.nav-links` hide and the old 960 logo heights were removed, and the resize close now matches 1280; *(ii) "a sticky header's height is assumed, never measured"* → measured via `--nav-h` and re-synced on the banner's reveal/dismiss; *(iii) "a copy sweep hits machine names"* → `twitter:card` and the privacy card-number sentence are guarded by an explicit scan; *(iv) "a second pricing renderer drifts from local currency"* → ONE `renderPricing()` used by both the toggle and the FX pass; *(v) "a shared shell hardcodes a link that only makes sense signed in"* → `brandHref` with explicit signed-out callers and a pinned impersonation target.

No rule edited. **Decision impact:** one predictable header at every width, an honest billing switch, the approved launch offer, and a logo that always leads somewhere useful — signed-out users reach the public site instead of a 401.

## [1.93.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 30 — backend & flow polish: four items, one release.** Executed smallest-to-largest (D5): the expense-kind guard, the pre-vehicle document stash, the registration logbook invitation, then the phone PDF variant. No new tables, no new environment knobs, no protected paths. Rule 42 gate: live audit found **0** open `remediation_proposals`; rule 54 live check found 2 vehicles / 4 expense rows / **0** kind mismatches (no backfill needed).
- **29a (P2-11) — a free-text expense that cannot exist on the asset is REFUSED (D2).** `expenseTypeChoices()` always scoped the MENU by `EXPENSE_TYPES[].kinds`, but free text bypassed it ("tyres" on a generator saved). New pure `validateExpenseTypeForAssetKind(expenseType, assetKind)` in `expenseCatalog.js` → `{ ok, type, kind, suggestion?, message? }`; `nearestKindType()` answers with a VALID alternative (curated type×kind pairs → same-category sibling on the kind menu → the kind's first menu type) and `expenseKindNoun()` supplies the plural. Applied at THREE write lanes (rule 51): `quizEngine` `log_expense` (validated before the duplicate guard; on refusal only the type is dropped and the draft stays alive with its amount), `commands.js handleLogExpenseTurn`, and `csvImport.validateImportRows` (new `kind_mismatch` + copy). Warm copy names the fix: "Generators don't have tyres — did you mean Consumables? Reply *Consumables* or pick another type." `Other` remains the honest escape. Blocking, never a warning: a kind-impossible row would pollute report categories permanently.
- **29b (v1.87.6) — the pre-vehicle stash.** A logbook/insurance/inspection/road-licence document sent BEFORE the asset existed used to be discarded (`media.js` TODO: `targetVehicle` null). New `src/services/documentStash.js`: `buildDocumentStash()` (JSON-safe metadata + transient buffer + 24 h expiry), `isDocumentStashExpired()`, `replayDocumentStash()` through the existing `vaultVehicleDocument` (media-id dedupe; Free keeps parsed data only). `media.js` returns the stash; `messages.js` stores it on the session; `sessionStore.stripTransientSessionFields()` removes ONLY the buffer before every `chat_sessions` write (single call site in `hooks.js`); registration completion (now carrying `saved.vehicleId`) files it under the new asset and appends the engine line. A cold instance re-downloads the media from Meta once; if that fails the card asks for a re-send — nothing is invented.
- **29c — the logbook invitation at registration completion.** `flowKnowledge.LOGBOOK_UPLOAD_COPY` + pure `resolveLogbookUploadCopy()`: a stash outcome wins, a prefilled `document_id` is never re-asked, and the storage promise appears ONLY when the vault stores files — the Free variant says "Photo storage is a paid feature." `quizEngine` appends the line after the completion card (the launch-award precedent, zero AI tokens). The playbook + core prompt carry the same copy (rule 13/60).
- **29d (26G) — mobile (A5) PDFs (D1/D4).** `SAKA_PDF_GEOMETRY_MOBILE` (419×595 A5, 36 pt margins, 347 pt content, hard 10 pt floor) + `resolvePdfGeometry()`/`normalizePdfVariant()` (unknown → A4); `SAKA_PDF_GEOMETRY` keeps its A4 numbers plus `minFontFloor: 0`. Every internal draw helper takes a geometry argument `G` (default = the frozen A4 set) — never a module global — and `fontSizeFor()` applies the floor. The phone layout uses a compact ledger (Date · Type · Description · Total, + Vehicle on fleet-wide; every row still prints), 30/32 pt rows, a 2-column KPI grid and geometry-relative charts/legend/footer. `buildProReportPdf({ variant })`; `buildRecordsPdf({ variant })`; `renderReportPdfBuffer(report, people, { variant })`; `deliverReportPdf({ variant })`. Routes: `pdfVariantFor(request)` honours `?variant=mobile|a4` and otherwise defaults phones (iPhone/Android UA) to the A5 layout on `GET /d/records.pdf` + `GET /d/reports.pdf`; the download filename is unchanged (D1). WhatsApp-shipped links default to mobile (`buildInlineReportLink`); email PDFs stay A4 (monthly sweep + earnings worker unchanged). The records page offers **PDF (A4)** and **PDF (phone)**; the reports builder has both submit buttons (`formaction`).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/expenseCatalog.js`, `src/services/quizEngine.js`, `src/routes/whatsapp/commands.js`, `src/services/csvImport.js`, `src/services/dashboard.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `src/services/documentStash.js` (new), `src/routes/whatsapp/media.js`, `messages.js`, `hooks.js`, `src/services/sessionStore.js`, `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `src/services/pdfEngine.js`, `src/services/reportDelivery.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_expense_catalog.js` (+7: 98), `test_quiz_flow.js` (+13: 228), `test_first_message_flow.js` (+2), `test_csv_import.js` (+4: 78), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (+8: 83), `test_pdf_engine.js` (+12: 104), `test_dashboard.js` (+13: 896), `test_report_builders.js` (+3: 91), `test_agent_training.js` (+2: 240), `package.json` (1.93.0), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§231 + history + version), this file.

## [1.92.1] - 2026-11-09
- **The digest template's button — found before activation (rule 44).** The operator opened `saka_overdue_digest` in Meta Business Manager (still pending approval) and shared a screenshot: the template carries a **button** ("Reminders"). The v1.92.0 registry declared `overdue_digest` as body-only, so the first send after `META_TPL_OVERDUE_DIGEST` was set would have hit the v1.87.3 failure class — Meta `131008: Button at index 0 ... requires a parameter` — outside the 24-hour window, where the text fallback cannot deliver. **Nothing was activated:** the env var stays unset and no live send was attempted.
- **Confirmed: Quick Reply ("Reminders", Custom).** The operator re-checked Business Manager. A Quick Reply returns the button text as a user message and takes **NO parameters**, so `overdue_digest` now declares `buttons: [{ subType: 'quick_reply', index: 0 }]`.
- **The payload is parameter-free (no 131008).** New `templateButtonState()` (pure; `templateButtonsForKind()` delegates) emits a Quick Reply as `{ type: 'button', sub_type: 'quick_reply', index: '0' }` with **no `parameters` key**; URL buttons keep the v1.87.3 contract (a declared variable; missing → text fallback, never a broken button). The wire payload is body `count|worst item|worst days` then the button; body-only kinds (`deploy_live`, the other 15) keep the unchanged one-component payload (pinned).
- **Inbound: the button opens the reminders list.** `parseInteractiveReply` already resolves `interactive.button_reply`; new `BUTTON_TITLE_PHRASES` maps an UNREGISTERED id's TITLE — "Reminders" / "Reminder" / "Remind me" — to the typed phrase **`reminders`**, owned by the deterministic `reminders_list` lane. The D1 law holds: "Remind me" never starts the reminder-creation quiz from this button; the alias sits after registered ids and quick-edit resolution, so it cannot hijack an existing lane.
- **No new environment knob** — the confirmation needs none; `.env.example` documents the parameter-free button and `docs/TEMPLATES.md` entry 17 carries the approved contract.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/proactiveTemplates.js`, `src/routes/whatsapp/interactive.js`, `docs/TEMPLATES.md`, `.env.example`, `test_proactive_templates.js` (+9: 74), `test_interactive_reply.js` (+4: 53), `package.json` (1.92.1), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§230b + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_proactive_templates (74), test_interactive_reply (53), test_read_registry (231), test_notifications (22), test_error_logging (22 — ratchet holds), test_env_audit (59), test_vehicle_documents, test_doc_sync, test_kb_docs, test_landing_static, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *"A template's component shape drifts from what Meta actually approved"* → every registry kind re-pinned (auth_otp URL parameterised; overdue_digest quick_reply; 15 body-only); the inbound reply shapes swept (button_reply / list_reply / legacy button / quick-edit / locked VIN/date) with the new title alias ordered last.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; 0 open proposals), 2, 3/4, 10/11, 12/33, 13, 18, 31, 44 (audited before activation), 50, 51/52, 54 (operator-verified Meta fact; no live send), 55, 58. No rule edited. **Decision impact:** none for users until the operator sets `META_TPL_OVERDUE_DIGEST`; after that the digest delivers outside the 24-hour window with a working "Reminders" button.

## [1.92.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 29 — the passbook earnings bug (real, fixed).** A completed passbook transfer re-anchored the vehicle but left `private_earnings` untouched, so the SELLER lost sight of their own money history and the BUYER inherited earnings they never generated (records, vehicle page, portfolio, reports, deep-dive, context snapshot, weekly auto-audit). Now the seller's earnings are archived at the handover and stay theirs.
- **D3 (locked) — archive BEFORE the COMPLETED flip.** `completePassbookTransfer` runs `archiveVehicleEarnings()` (ONE update, `WHERE archived_at IS NULL`, idempotent) immediately after the capacity gate and BEFORE the vehicle/COMPLETED writes. An archive error aborts with *"Could not finalize the transfer. Nothing was changed. Please try again."* and audits `ADMIN_TRANSFER_ARCHIVE_FAILED`; a later failure rolls the archive back (`restoreArchivedEarnings`) — invariant **COMPLETED ⇒ archived**. Success audits `ADMIN_TRANSFER_EARNINGS_ARCHIVED` with its count; a phone-only seller is audited `ADMIN_TRANSFER_EARNINGS_REPORT_SKIPPED`.
- **Buyer-blind on every surface (rule 51 sweep).** New `src/services/earningsScope.js` (`selectLiveEarnings`) filters `archived_at IS NULL` with a rule-50 degrade when the column is missing; applied to `queryOwnerRecords` (rows + count), `fleetReports`, `ownerPortfolio`, `vehicleDeepDive`, `userContextSnapshot`, `reportMetrics`, `driverReports` (×2) and the weekly auto-audit. Superadmin revenue totals deliberately keep counting archived rows (D6 — evidence ledger).
- **D2 (locked) — history view + final report, both.** `GET /d/records?history=1` (and `.csv`/`.pdf`) swaps in `queryArchivedEarnings()` scoped through `passbook_transfers.from_owner_id` (never live ownership); rows carry an **Archived** chip, no receipt link, and the `All records · Transferred (history)` toggle rides every pager/export link. The seller receives `sendFinalEarningsReportEmail` ~10 minutes after the handover: a real PDF attachment (the SAME records renderer, `Final Earnings Report` heading) **plus** the history link in the body. `earnings_report_queue` + a 30-second worker drain it (3 attempts max, honest `last_error`, nothing-to-send rows closed, sent rows pruned after 30 days). The transfer completion message now says the history has been archived and a final report is on its way.
- **Schema (additive).** `private_earnings.archived_at` + `archived_by_transfer_id` (+ partial index) and `public.earnings_report_queue`; `public.overdue_digest_log` for the digest below. Carriers: `npm run migrate:earnings-archive` + `npm run migrate:overdue-digest` (idempotent) + boot ensures + staging DDL + `RESET_TABLES`/TRUNCATE/targeted-phone cascade + `testCleanup` + `profileWipe` + `test_db_reset_scope` Test 10. **Applied live** (project `wnvbhpykboonygaznhhl`) and probed.
- **D1 (locked) — hybrid overdue compliance digest.** The FIRST overdue item of an episode keeps its one immediate alert; every later item is stamped `notified_overdue` but NOT messaged, and `evaluateOverdueDigest()` (the 4th `runReminderSweep` pass) sends ONE consolidated digest at T+3 and ONE at T+7 — **three messages maximum per episode regardless of N**, replacing today's one-message-per-item storm. The anchor is the first item's due date and never moves while anything stays overdue; renewing everything resets the episode (a genuinely new item later starts a fresh clock); a sweep with zero active date reminders clears every episode row. Driver-linked items reach the owner; `canDeliver` boundaries hold; milestones are stamped only after an attempt.
- **D7 — template.** `overdue_digest` (`META_TPL_OVERDUE_DIGEST`, Utility, en, count / worst item / worst days) registered in `PROACTIVE_TEMPLATE_KINDS` (17 kinds), `docs/TEMPLATES.md` entry 17 + suggested name `saka_overdue_digest`, `envAudit` and `.env.example`. Knob unset = byte-identical free-form fallback; the operator submits the template in Business Manager in parallel.
- **Rule 13/60 sync.** `flowKnowledge.transfer_seller` (writes + rules), the dashboard playbook EARNINGS ARCHIVE paragraph, and the core prompt's `8b. PASSBOOK EARNINGS & OVERDUE DIGEST (v1.92.0)` block; `test_agent_training` +6 (238); `docs/` regenerated.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "a state transition leaves a derived read surface stale"* → all 9 vehicle-scoped earnings readers swept (superadmin totals deliberately left, D6). *(ii) "a completion path writes before it validates"* → archive first + rollback on every later failure; the paused-upgrade path never archives. *(iii) "a notification cap enforced per item instead of per owner"* → same-sweep `firstOverdueSent` set + persisted episode row; threshold reminders deliberately untouched. *(iv) "a milestone marker that burns on a failed send"* → milestones stamped only after the attempt.

## [1.91.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 28A — superadmin write actions, shipped on top of the 28B hardening.** Six tools on `/d/admin`, each behind the same guard stack (flag gate → Origin check on every POST → admin session → 5/5-min composite IP+session rate limit → server-side typed confirmation → mandatory reason → `admin_audit_logs` row with reason + targetId + before/after → 303 `?done=<verb>` banner):
  - **Impersonate (READ-ONLY, D1).** A separate `saka_dash_as` cookie (`Path=/d; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Max-Age=1800; Secure`) and a new `admin_impersonations` table (sha256 of the token only). `requireRoleSession` opens the TARGET owner lane only while the caller holds a live ADMIN session and the row belongs to that admin; owner-lane POSTs answer *"Exit impersonation to make changes"*. `GET /d/admin/exit-as` clears ONLY the as-cookie and deletes the row — the admin's `saka_dash` session is never touched (proven by test). Expiry is enforced by the cookie, the row's `expires_at` on every resolution, and a 15-minute prune. A sticky yellow banner (owner name + Nairobi HH:MM + Exit) is injected by a scoped `onSend` hook.
  - **Reset.** Typed `RESET` + reason; reuses `wipeProfileForSignup` and ALSO deletes `dashboard_sessions` by owner_id plus any `admin_impersonations` pointing at or driven by the profile; the superadmin's own profile is refused; audited with per-table counts.
  - **Extend trial.** Typed `EXTEND` + reason + days 1–365; calls `extendAccountTrial({days})` **without** `maxTotalDays`, so the 45-day clamp (and the launch-award exception) stay engine-owned; a capped result is reported honestly, and an account with no active trial gets the engine's own error.
  - **Passbook view.** Read-only plate/VIN/token (masked, `<details>` reveal) + transfer history with masked buyer details; 404 for a missing or soft-deleted vehicle; audited.
  - **Facilitate transfer.** The SAME `transfers.js` engine via injectable seams: step 1 initiate, step 2 buyer details (the release PIN is emailed **ONLY to the owner** — D2 — and the admin relays it via WhatsApp), step 3 release PIN, cancel with an honest refusal path. Steps 4–5 (buyer ACCEPT + email code) stay on WhatsApp; the page shows live status + refresh. Every step audited `ADMIN_ACTION_TRANSFER_*`.
  - **Email as Saka.** Draft → escaped preview → typed `SEND` + reason; From `Saka Fleet <reports@reports.sakafleet.com>`; refuses an account with no email; a refused send answers 502 and is audited `ok:false` (never a fake success).
- **Schema (additive, rule 56).** `public.admin_impersonations` with `token_hash` UNIQUE, both profile ids, `expires_at`, `created_at`; indexes on both ids; RLS enabled. Carriers: `npm run migrate:admin-impersonations` (idempotent) + boot ensure + staging DDL + `RESET_TABLES`/TRUNCATE/targeted-phone cascade + `testCleanup` + `test_db_reset_scope`. **Applied live** (project `wnvbhpykboonygaznhhl`) and verified via the Management API: PK `admin_impersonations_pkey`, UNIQUE `admin_impersonations_token_hash_key`, `idx_admin_impersonations_target`, `idx_admin_impersonations_admin`, `relrowsecurity = true`.
- **`wipeProfileForSignup` ends the account's sessions.** It now deletes `dashboard_sessions` by owner_id and `admin_impersonations` by target/admin, so no cookie outlives the profile (`test_profile_wipe` 17).
- **Rule 13/60 sync.** `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK` gains the SUPERADMIN WEB TOOLS line and scopes the "have NOT shipped" list to OWNERS; the core prompt names the six tools and the typed-confirm/audit law; `docs/CARE_WORKER_GUIDE.md` gains section 2b; `test_agent_training` +6 (232).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/dashboard.js` (impersonation constants/DDL/ensure/mint/resolve/clear/prune + the cookie trio, `adminNav` export, owners/fleet action links + notice), `src/services/dashboardAdmin.js` (new), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (impersonation branch, banner `onSend`, 15 routes, injectable `adminEmailSender`/`adminTransferEngine`), `src/services/profileWipe.js`, `src/services/rateLimitIdentity.js` (`adminActionKeyGenerator`), `index.js` (boot ensure + prune), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `scripts/run_admin_impersonations_migration.js` (new), `scripts/{reset-db,testCleanup,provision_staging_schema,health_audit}.js`, `docs/CARE_WORKER_GUIDE.md`, `test_dashboard_admin.js` (new, 92), `test_profile_wipe.js` (+2: 17), `test_db_reset_scope.js` (+13: 107), `test_agent_training.js` (+6: 232), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.91.0 + `migrate:admin-impersonations`), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§229 + history + version), this file.
