## [1.90.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 28B — transport & error hardening (security P0, shipped BEFORE the Phase 28A admin write actions so those endpoints are born hardened).** Five fixes from the Phase 24 security audit, each with before/after assertions and a live pre-check:
  - **CSV formula injection.** `csvCell` escaped quotes/newlines only, so a cell starting with `= + - @ TAB CR` became a live Excel/LibreOffice formula. The cell is now apostrophe-prefixed AFTER RFC-4180 quoting; numeric-shaped values (`-500`, `1,200.50`) are deliberately exempt so arithmetic survives, and `csvNumber` output (unsigned) is never touched.
  - **Error-handler leak.** The global handler echoed `error.message` (constraint text, column names, provider errors). It now returns ONE generic line while `fastify.log` and `error_logs` keep the full error; `error.statusCode` and `error.name` are preserved. Extracted to `src/services/errorHandler.js` so the suite can prove both halves.
  - **HSTS + 308.** `Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains` on every HTTPS response, and a 308 for requests the proxy marks plain-HTTP. The three status probes (`/healthz`, `/api/health`, `/internal/status`) are exempt — Google's edge intercepts `/healthz` on the custom domain and platform health checks must never be redirected. CHECK 2 verified live before choosing `includeSubDomains`: sakafleet.com 301, www 301, app 302, reports 301 — all HTTP→HTTPS.
  - **`/d/verify` CSRF.** CHECK 4 enumerated all 12 `/d` POSTs: 11 already carried `isCrossOriginWrite`; `/d/verify` was the only gap. The guard now runs BEFORE any OTP work, so a cross-site post cannot burn an attempt or mint a session.
  - **Rate-limit identity.** With no `trustProxy`, `request.ip` is the shared Google Frontend address, so every per-route bucket was effectively global. Fastify now runs with `trustProxy: true` and the limiter keys on the RIGHTMOST `x-forwarded-for` hop (`src/services/rateLimitIdentity.js`) — the one address the GFE appends and a caller can never forge; no header falls back to the socket address.
- **CHECK 1 (error.message consumers).** Swept `src/` and the suites: the only client-facing raw message was the handler itself; `index.js`'s `/api/sentinel` catch is secret-gated, and the service-layer `message:` fields are internal result objects. No test asserted the old handler copy.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/dashboard.js` (`csvCell` exported + guard, default export), `src/services/errorHandler.js` (new), `src/services/securityHeaders.js` (new), `src/services/rateLimitIdentity.js` (new), `index.js` (trustProxy, keyGenerator, security hook, handler install after the Supabase client), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (`/d/verify` guard), `test_security.js` (+54 assertions: Tests 6–9), `test_dashboard.js` (+3), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.90.0), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§228 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_security, test_dashboard, test_doc_sync, test_kb_docs, test_landing_static, test_vehicle_documents, test_env_audit, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defects swept by class: *(i) "a serializer quotes but never neutralises executable prefixes"* → every CSV writer audited: `csvCell` is the only cell serializer and both `serializeRecordsCsv` + `serializeAllDataCsv` route through it (fixed once, proven both ways); the PDF/text paths emit no formulas. *(ii) "an error path echoes internal text to the caller"* → the one global handler (fixed); `/api/sentinel` (secret-gated, deliberately left); the dashboard routes render their own honest copy (safe). *(iii) "a transport guarantee assumed to exist at the edge"* → HSTS was absent everywhere (fixed); the probes were explicitly exempted rather than assumed safe. *(iv) "a POST lane added after the CSRF pattern shipped"* → all 12 `/d` POSTs re-enumerated (`/d/verify` fixed; the other 11 verified). *(v) "a limiter keyed on an address the platform shares"* → the single root registration (fixed) and every per-route config re-checked (they inherit the keyGenerator).
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **0 open proposals** — live check; no duplicate work), 2 (this section), 3/4 (targeted reads, batched edits), 10 (all new assertions stateless), 11 (targeted suites only), 12/33 (one commit: version + history + CHANGELOG + landing feed + regenerated docs; `fix(v1.90.0)` style), 13 (architecture + regenerated docs; no flow/intent changed, so no prompt edit), 18 (no protected path touched), 21 (no new env knob — HSTS is static), 31 (the client error line is warm, never technical), 43/54 (live checks: HTTP→HTTPS on all four subdomains, `/api/health` 200, gcloud service + project verified), 50 (logging unchanged; a failure is still recorded), 51/52 (five-class sweep + before/after assertions), 55 (evolved the existing serializer/handler/hook seams — zero new tables), 58 (this report). No rule edited. **Decision impact:** clients now receive a generic error message; plain-HTTP requests are redirected except the probes; rate-limit buckets are per real client. No user-facing flow changed.

## [1.89.2] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 27D — the nickname is actually editable now (the v1.89.0 gap).** Live walkthrough on KAS628T: the owner typed **"Edit KAS 628t nickname"** at 16:07 EAT and asked five more times over three minutes; every **"Nickname"** reply was answered *"that didn't come through as a field name"*, and `vehicles.nickname` stayed **NULL** (verified against the live row, `updated_at` untouched by any nickname write).
- **Root cause (rule 51 sibling sweep).** v1.89.0 added the column, the registration question, nickname-first resolution and the display label — but never added nickname to either **edit** surface:
  - `quizEngine.js` → `EDITABLE_VEHICLE_FIELDS` (the edit_vehicle field menu) listed 15 fields and **not nickname**, so the choice parser rejected the word outright;
  - `commands.js` → `handleEditVehicleTurn` kept its own `fieldAliasMap` / `attrKeys` / explicit-field regex, all without nickname.
  The DB layer was already correct (`sanitizeVehicleUpdates` whitelists `nickname`; `updateVehicleDetails` writes it), so the chain broke only at the two registries that decide *what the user may ask for*.
- **Fix — quiz lane.** `EDITABLE_VEHICLE_FIELDS` gains `'Nickname': 'nickname'` with everyday aliases (`nick name`, `aka`, `a.k.a.`, `asset name`, `pet name`); the new-value field sanitises through `sanitizeVehicleNickname` (≤40, letters/numbers), refuses longer input with guidance, and **`remove` / `clear` / `none` / `skip` clears it to NULL** (the engine reads `null` as "not an answer", so the clear rides an explicit sentinel that the executor maps to NULL — the card shows *"Remove nickname"*, never the sentinel).
- **Fix — one-line command.** New pure `parseVehicleEditRequest()` (exported) reads the sentence itself: **"edit KAS 628T nickname to Teddy"** → field + value, **"edit KAS 628T nickname"** → field only, **"rename KAS 628T to The Workhorse"** → nickname, while a bare "edit vehicle" still asks and other fields (`"change fuel type to Diesel"`) keep working. Plates and existing nicknames are masked before field matching, so an asset called *"The Workhorse"* or a plate containing a field word can never be misread. `quiz.js` prefills it on **both** start paths (mid-quiz switch + normal start); `applyExtraction` never overwrites a prefilled answer (deterministic wins over AI).
- **Fix — deterministic lane + menu.** `handleEditVehicleTurn` gains nickname in its alias map, attribute keys, explicit `<field> to <value>` parser and its "what can I edit" card; `interactiveReply` accepts `edit_vehicle:nickname` (VIN stays locked); `FLOW_GUIDANCE.edit_vehicle` now names the nickname and says **passbook token** (the old copy wrongly said the plate was locked — it is editable).
- **Display.** The success card uses the **saved** row (`res.vehicle`) through `vehicleDisplayLabel()` and reports *"no nickname"* on a clear; the edit card header shows `Nickname (PLATE)`. Dashboard/ledger display was already correct (v1.89.0) and is covered by `test_dashboard` (883).
- **Rule 13/60 sync.** `flowKnowledge.edit_vehicle` (trigger + `nickname` extracted + editable/clear rule), `ASSET NICKNAMES … EDITING (v1.89.2)` in the core prompt, the `EDIT_VEHICLE` classifier instruction (`nickname` is a valid `edit_field`), `FLOW_GUIDANCE.edit_vehicle`; `docs/` regenerated.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/quizEngine.js` (registry, `parseVehicleNicknameEdit`, `editFieldChoices`, `parseVehicleEditRequest`, flow parse/validate/summary, executor), `src/routes/whatsapp/quiz.js` (prefill wiring), `src/routes/whatsapp/commands.js` (alias map, attr keys, explicit parser, sanitise/clear, edit card), `src/services/interactiveReply.js`, `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_quiz_flow.js` (+20: 215), `test_edit_delete.js` (+4 lane assertions), `test_v1571.js` (+2: 22), `test_interactive_reply.js` (+1: 49), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.89.2), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§227 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_quiz_flow (215), test_edit_delete, test_v1571 (22), test_interactive_reply (49), test_narrative_draft (128), test_entry_ledger, test_dashboard (883), test_agent_training (226), test_doc_sync (30), test_kb_docs, test_landing_static, test_env_audit (59), test_vehicle_documents (75), `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defect class *"a shipped field is added to one registry but not to the edit surfaces that decide what a user may ask for"* → every edit surface swept: quiz `EDITABLE_VEHICLE_FIELDS` (fixed), `commands.js` `handleEditVehicleTurn` alias map/attrs/regex (fixed), `interactiveReply.QUICK_EDIT_ACTIONS` (fixed), `vehicles.sanitizeVehicleUpdates` + `updateVehicleDetails` (already correct ✓), dashboard/ledger resolution (v1.89.0 ✓). The same class is now pinned by an assertion in each lane so a future field cannot ship half-wired.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/42/45/63 (rules + architecture first; **no open proposals**; 2 live PENDING friction rows found and reconciled), 2 (this section), 3/4 (targeted reads, batched edits), 7 (no country-specific parsing touched), 8 (VIN/passbook stay locked — asserted in both lanes), 10 (all new suites stateless or cleaned), 12/13/33 (one commit: version + history + CHANGELOG + landing + regenerated docs), 31/60 (plain-language guidance, one next step, no blame), 50 (a missing nickname column still degrades — the edit path uses the existing pre-migration retry), 51/52 (sibling sweep + before/after assertions), 54 (live row + transcript + revision verified), 55 (evolved the existing edit surfaces — zero new tables), 58 (this report). ## [1.89.1] - 2026-11-09
- **Release-gate hygiene — `npm run health` stops going red on a Windows checkout.** The v1.89.0 release was pushed at 13:13 EAT and deployed (revision `saka-fleet-00383-p67`, `/api/health` 200, `deploy.last_deployed_revision` written), but the local Windows clone came back from the same moment with a corrupt git index and zero-filled refs, so the release gates had to be re-proven from scratch (rule 52). Re-running them surfaced one real defect that has nothing to do with the app: the knowledge-doc gate.
- **Root cause.** `scripts/gen_kb_docs.js` compared the generated document to the file on disk **byte-for-byte** (`onDisk !== content`), and `test_kb_docs.js` did the same. This clone runs with `core.autocrlf=true`, so git hands the very same generated text back as CRLF — the content is identical, the bytes are not, and `test_kb_docs` (one of the suites inside the mandatory pre-push `npm run health`) failed with *"PRODUCT_QA.md matches the live registries (run npm run docs:gen on drift)"*. `npm run docs:gen` "fixed" it by rewriting the files with LF, which git then reports as **no diff at all** — proving there was never any content drift. The same false red would return after every checkout/pull that touches those two docs.
- **Fix (evolve, don't rebuild — rule 55).** One exported helper, `normalizeDocNewlines(text)` in `scripts/gen_kb_docs.js`, collapses `\r\n` to `\n`; `checkDocs()` compares normalised text on both sides and `writeDocs()` always writes LF. `checkDocs` is now exported so the suite can assert the CLI gate and the test agree. Nothing about what counts as real drift changed — a genuine registry change still fails the gate.
- **Tests (behaviour pinned).** `test_kb_docs.js` normalises the on-disk read, adds `checkDocs(docs).length === 0` (the CLI gate and the suite are one source of truth), and a new **Test 2b** that builds a CRLF copy of each real generated doc, proves it differs byte-for-byte, and proves it normalises back to the exact generated text — plus LF-untouched and CRLF-collapse assertions. `test_vehicle_documents.js` version pin updated.
- **Frames touched.** `scripts/gen_kb_docs.js` (`normalizeDocNewlines`, `writeDocs`, `checkDocs` now exported), `test_kb_docs.js` (+7 assertions incl. Test 2b), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `package.json` (1.89.1), `landing/data/changelog.json`, `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§226 + history + version), this file.

## [1.89.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 27C — country-resolved STT locales + asset nicknames.** The last two Phase 27 tasks, both built on the existing seams (rule 55 — zero new tables beyond the two agreed columns).
- **STT locales (rule 7).** `globalization.js` gains the frozen `COUNTRY_STT_LOCALES` map — 21 countries: KE `sw-KE` + `en-KE`/`en-US`; TZ/UG Swahili; RW `rw-RW` + French; FR/DE/ES/BE own languages; every other REGIONS/EXTRA country derives its primary from its regional locale; GLOBAL stays `en-US` + `sw-KE`. A country can now be retuned WITHOUT a deploy: `country_kb.stt_locale` (new column, `npm run migrate:stt-locale`, boot DDL mirror in `index.js`) is read by `loadCountrySttLocale` (5-min in-process cache, `STT_LOCALE_CACHE_MS`) and wins over the bundled map; `media.js` passes it into `transcribeAudioBuffer`. `npm run reconcile:stt-locales` (dry-run by default, `--apply` writes) backfills one row per country — **live: 21 rows inserted, 21 `STT_LOCALE_RECONCILED` audits, a re-run inserts 0**. **Leak guard:** `getCountryBucket` now merges only `vehicle_compliance`/`driver_compliance` (or category-less legacy) overrides, so an `stt_locale` row can never surface as a compliance rule — pinned.
- **Asset nicknames (D2/D3/D4).** `vehicles.nickname` (nullable, `npm run migrate:vehicle-nickname`, boot DDL mirror) holds an optional human label — **≤40 characters, sanitised through `sanitizeEntryLabel`**. Registration asks "Got a nickname for it?" right after make/model; the question is optional and `skip` stores null, so onboarding never blocks. Every reference resolves **nickname FIRST, then plate, then ordinal, then make/model**: `matchNarrativeVehicle` (a new pass 0 with whole-token boundaries and ≥3 chars; two assets sharing a nickname answer `ambiguous-nickname`), `resolveLedgerVehicleScope` (never falls through to the active vehicle on an ambiguous nickname), the quiz "which vehicle" picker, the trial-end keep picker, and `findVehicleByCandidate` (edit/delete). `extractVehicleAttributes` captures an explicit marker only (`nickname:`/`aka`/`called`/`named`, trailing sentence words trimmed) — never guessed from prose. One `vehicleDisplayLabel()` helper renders **"The Workhorse (KAS 628T)"** in quick-log/quiz confirmations, the vehicle edit card and the dashboard; `sanitizeVehicleUpdates` whitelists `nickname` (VIN + passbook token stay locked — rule 8). An un-migrated schema retries the registration insert without the column (rule 50).

## [1.88.1] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 27A hotfix — the STT sample rate was still wrong.** v1.88.0 made Google accept the request (`sampleRateHertz` present) but declared **16000** for every note. Live telemetry after the deploy told a new story: the three notes at 08:47–08:48 UTC reached Google (3.9–4.6 s) and came back as **"ah" (confidence 0.316)** and **two × "No speech detected"** — not an instant rejection. Root cause proven with the new `scripts/probe_audio.js` against the *actual* Meta downloads: every WhatsApp note's Ogg container declares **`OpusHead input_sample_rate = 48000`**; declaring 16000 makes Google decode the audio 3× too slow, so speech becomes "ah" or silence. Replaying the same three notes with 7 configs: **16000 → "ah"/silence; 48000 → "repair … will it cost 300 kenya shillings" (0.782), "itategemea shillings today for euro var front right wing pancha" (0.842), "pancha repair rihanna kenya shillings from the right will" (0.681); 24000/12000 → silence; 8000 → nonsense; no rate → the documented 400; encoding omitted → 400.**
- **Fix (evolve, don't rebuild — rule 55).** `sttService` gains pure `readOggOpusSampleRate(buffer)` (locates `OpusHead`, reads the uint32 LE input rate, accepts only Google's five supported rates) and `resolveSpeechSampleRate(raw, buffer)`: an explicit `STT_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ` override wins (snapped to the nearest supported rate), otherwise **the container's own OpusHead rate**, otherwise **48000**. `transcribeAudioBuffer` passes the buffer to the resolver for OGG_OPUS; other encodings keep auto-detection. `STT_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ` is now an override knob only (default 48000; `auto`/unset reads the container).
- **Intent note (STEP 4 of the deep-dive).** The 0:08 note transcribed to the single word "ah"; that text was injected as the turn and the model answered from the previous documents context ("Both files are safe under K628T"). It was **not** a silent default — it was a garbled transcript doing its job. The rate fix removes the garbling; richer language coverage (Swahili/English code-switching) is Release C's `COUNTRY_STT_LOCALES` work.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/sttService.js` (`readOggOpusSampleRate`, `resolveSpeechSampleRate`, `SUPPORTED_OPUS_RATES`, OGG_OPUS config wiring), `scripts/probe_audio.js` (new operator probe: Meta download → 7 configs → real service call), `test_stt_service.js` (+6: 33 → 39), `.env.example` (override semantics), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `package.json` (1.88.1), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§223 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_stt_service (39 — +6), test_quiz_flow (187), test_agent_training (221), test_no_canned_replies (15), test_env_audit (59), test_doc_sync (30), test_vehicle_documents (75), test_landing_static, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defect class *"a fixed constant stands in for a value the payload itself carries"* → every media config builder swept: OGG_OPUS rate (fixed — now read from the container), Vision OCR (no rate concept ✓), `STT_ESTIMATED_BITRATE` (a duration estimate only — deliberately left a constant), PDF/media MIME selection (reads `download.mimeType` ✓), WhatsApp media size cap (constant by design ✓). The probe script is committed so a future note can be replayed in one command.

## [1.88.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 27A — voice notes actually transcribe + a warm Free dashboard door.** Three fixes, one live-proven and two copy/answer quality.
- **BUG — a live voice note was turned away.** Live proof (2026-11-09 07:29:51Z, revision `saka-fleet-00378-tw9`): `ocr_requests` provider=`google_speech` status=`failed` attempts=1 latency 1274 ms and `error_logs` `/webhook/whatsapp/voice` unresolved — **`Invalid recognition 'config': Opus sample rate (0) not in supported rates 8000, 12000, 16000, 24000, 48000`**. Root cause: `sttService` sent `encoding='OGG_OPUS'` WITHOUT `sampleRateHertz` (the old comment claimed the container carries it); Google reads rate 0 and rejects the request, so every WhatsApp voice note degraded to the fallback line. This also proves `STT_ENABLED` was never off (unset live = ON) and the v1.79.0 `403 API_KEY_SERVICE_BLOCKED` is gone (the error is an authenticated config validation). **Fix:** new `resolveSpeechSampleRate()` (knob `STT_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ`, default **16000**, clamp **8000-48000**, resolved per call) and `config.sampleRateHertz` for OGG_OPUS only; unknown encodings still omit both fields for auto-detect. The fallback line is now honest — **"🎙️ I couldn't catch that voice note — please type it or send a shorter one."** — with the unchanged `voice_note_fallback` status + `[voice note]` marker, and audio bytes are still never stored.
- **The Free dashboard answer is a warm upsell, never a cold refusal.** The `dashboard` lane always handed every caller the sign-in link, so a Free account met the OTP-time `not_entitled` refusal afterwards (the operator's cold "paid feature" line was AI-shaped by the old "point to *pricing*" playbook). The processor now adds the per-turn `entitlements` snapshot to `readCtx` and the lane branches deterministically (zero tokens): Free gets the warm card — live dashboard is a paid feature, the same data as a PDF (*report*), plans from *subscription* at the engine's own **$9.99/mo** (`TIER_MATRIX.solo_pro.monthlyUSD`); trial/paid/superadmin keep the static sign-in link + 3-line tutorial; a missing snapshot keeps the historical link (the page stays the gate); flag-off/admin-door unchanged. Rule 13/60 sync: `FLOW_GUIDANCE.dashboard`, `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK` (v1.88.0) and the core prompt all forbid the cold refusal.
- **Owner records with an assigned vehicle — proven, no code change.** `queryOwnerRecords` scopes `vehicle_id IN (owner's live vehicles)`; `vehicles.assigned_driver_id` is a separate FK that never enters the scope. `test_dashboard` now seeds v-b as assigned to driver 'd-1' and asserts the owner list carries the assignment, the plate maps, and a vehicle-filtered owner query still returns the driver-logged row.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/sttService.js` (rate resolver + OGG_OPUS config), `src/routes/whatsapp/media.js` (fallback copy), `src/routes/whatsapp/messages.js` (readCtx + entitlements), `src/services/readCommands.js` (`DASHBOARD_FREE_UPSELL` + plan branch), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js` (guidance + playbook), `sakaAi.js` (core prompt), `src/services/envAudit.js` (+`STT_SAMPLE_RATE_HZ`), `.env.example`, `test_stt_service.js` (+7: 26 → 33), `test_read_registry.js` (+10: 221 → 231), `test_dashboard.js` (+3: 876 → 879), `test_agent_training.js` (v1.88.0 pins + 2 assertions), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), `package.json` (1.88.0), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§222 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_stt_service (33), test_read_registry (231), test_quiz_flow (187), test_dashboard (879), test_agent_training (221), test_no_canned_replies (15 — ratchet still 4), test_env_audit (59), test_doc_sync (30), `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) "a provider config omits a field the API requires, documented as safe"* → Vision (no rate concept ✓), Speech STT (fixed), Lens (no provider call ✓). *(ii) "a deterministic lane ignores the caller's plan and lets a later gate refuse"* → dashboard lane (fixed); report lane already gates (`reportDelivery` ✓), `documents_list` already tier-gated (✓), web pages server-gated (✓). *(iii) "owner-scoped reads trust an assignment/person FK"* → `queryOwnerRecords` (vehicle scope ✓, now pinned for ASSIGNED), `loadOwnerVehicles` (owner_id ✓), WhatsApp records lane (per-caller `driver_id` by design — left), compliance board (owner vehicles ✓).

## [1.87.5] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 28 — driver-licence precedence + an honest transfer cancel.** Two walkthrough bugs, one latent (reproduced offline) and one live-proven.
- **BUG 1 — a driver's licence was filed as a vehicle logbook.** Root cause: `classifyOcrDocument` (`ocrService.js`) checked the LOGBOOK branch (INCLUDING `looksLikeVin`) **before** the LICENCE branch, and `looksLikeVin` accepted any **pure-digit 11–17 char token** — an NTSA licence's "SERIAL NO: 043202308218505" tripped it, so the photo became `logbook`; `classifyDocumentFamily` had no demotion, and the vault then stored it vehicle-scoped. Reproduced offline: `classifyOcrDocument(DL text) === 'logbook'` before the fix. **Fixes:** licence markers now win over logbook markers (insurance keeps first place — the v1.32.1 law); `looksLikeVin` requires **letters AND digits**; the Lens gains a defence-in-depth demotion (`logbook` + strong licence markers → `drivers_licence`); bonus crash fix — `parseLogbook`'s bare-VIN fallback regex has no capture group, so `vinMatch[1].toUpperCase()` threw and the lens silently lost every hint (now `vinMatch[1] ?? vinMatch[0]`). **Routing is unchanged by decision (D3):** a correctly-classified `drivers_licence` is already non-vaultable and goes to the existing driver capture lane (`driver_compliances`); **no new tables, no schema change**.
- **BUG 2 — "cancel" faked success and the handover stayed in-flight.** Root cause: `POST /d/transfer/cancel` wrote `{ status: 'CANCELLED', updated_at: … }`, but `passbook_transfers` has **no `updated_at` column**; PostgREST returned the error in the result object and the route ignored it, still redirecting `?cancelled=1` (live proof: row `78cda450…` stayed `PENDING_OWNER_RELEASE_OTP`, blocking new handovers). **Fixes:** the write names only real columns, the error is checked and answered honestly (500 "could not be cancelled just now", logged to `error_logs` — never a fake success); the machine's initiation guard now covers **every** pending status (it was missing `PENDING_BUYER_UPGRADE`).
- **Live reconciliation (rule 56).** David's stuck handover `78cda450…` set to `CANCELLED` (audited `TRANSFER_CANCELLED_RECONCILED` — the operator had already asked for the cancel), so the walkthrough can start a fresh transfer immediately; Cline's audit artifact (`error_logs` "Invalid time value" from a read-only probe, 06:51:41) deleted. No misclassified licence row existed in production at ship time (`vehicle_documents` = the 2 legitimate files); if one appears, the reconciliation path is delete-row + object and let the driver capture re-file it.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/ocrService.js` (`classifyOcrDocument` order, `looksLikeVin`), `src/services/documentLens.js` (`classifyDocumentFamily` demotion, `parseLogbook` fallback), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (cancel write + honest error), `src/services/transfers.js` (guard list), `test_ocr_flow.js` (+8), `test_dashboard.js` (+4), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§221 + history + version), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/` (regenerated), `package.json` (1.87.5), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_ocr_flow (153, +8), test_dashboard (876, +4), test_vehicle_documents (75), test_quiz_flow, test_ocr_normalizer, test_agent_training, test_doc_sync, test_kb_docs, test_landing_static, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *(i) Defect class "a weaker heuristic branch runs before a stronger labelled marker"* → whole document-family precedence swept: insurance (already first ✓), inspection/road_licence/purchase_invoice/service_receipt (Lens overrides demote a logbook base ✓), drivers_licence (fixed — the one unprotected family), receipts/odometer (not logbook-adjacent). *(ii) Defect class "a VIN heuristic accepts non-VIN tokens"* → `looksLikeVin` (fixed) and `parseLogbook`'s inline fallback (no-crash fix); KRA PIN stripping unchanged. *(iii) Defect class "a write names a column the live table never had, and the error is swallowed"* → every `passbook_transfers` writer swept: dashboard cancel (fixed), WhatsApp cancel (`pending.js` — status only ✓), delete-vehicle cancel (`vehicles.js` — status only ✓), start/verify (`transfers.js` — no phantom columns ✓); `error_logs` honesty added on the one that could fail silently. *(iv) Defect class "a pending-state guard lists only some pending states"* → the initiation guard (fixed) vs the dashboard in-flight list (`TRANSFER_OPEN_STATUSES` already had all five ✓).

## [1.87.4] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 27 — the document & receipt vault walkthrough fixes.** Two live walkthrough bugs, found and proven with live Supabase/Storage/Cloud Run queries (rule 54). **Bug 1:** a WhatsApp-stored compliance document never appeared under **Stored files** on `/d/vehicles/:id`. Root cause: `buildVehiclePage` called `loadDocuments({ supabase, vehicleId, … })` with ONE object while the real `listVehicleDocuments(client, options)` takes the client first — no scope reached the query, so it answered `[]` since v1.84.0, and the suite's injected mock ignored its arguments. **Bug 2:** a fuel receipt never appeared in `/d/records` with a View link. Root cause: the receipt auto-quiz was gated on the AI classifier returning exactly `LOG_EXPENSE` for a bare photo (it does not), so the vaulted `receipt_id` never rode the draft (live transcript: Saka asked the user to type the details, the typed reply opened a fresh quiz, the expense saved with `receipt_id = NULL`); the legacy same-amount fallback also failed because the receipt row's amount was the merchant's **P.O. Box 11,823** — the KE profile could not read the e-TIMS fiscal `3.000.00` grouping and the Document Lens fallback picked the box number (vendor came out as the legal-receipt marker line).
- **Fixes (evolve, don't rebuild — rule 55).** (A) `buildVehiclePage` now calls `loadDocuments(supabase, { vehicleId, … })` for both the current list and the replaced-file history, plus a defensive one-object unwrap in `listVehicleDocuments` (`{ supabase }` bag detected by the missing `.from`), so the convention can never silently empty again. (B) New pure `receiptQuizStartFor()` in `quiz.js`: a freshly VAULTED receipt ALWAYS opens the expense quiz whatever the classifier said (the v1.49.3 law — receipts confirm before any write); duplicates/unvaulted keep the legacy rule so a re-send can never open a second draft; `messages.js` passes `hasReceiptPrefill`. (C) `parseKeFiscalAmount()` resolves dot-thousands + dot-decimal fiscal money ("3.000.00" → 3000, "2.777.78" → 2777.78) and is registered as `KE_OCR_PROFILE.amount.parse`; the Document Lens delegates to the country profile, inspects the WHOLE line for P.O. Box/PIN/invoice/serial context, never accepts a bare 4–6 digit integer when a labelled TOTAL exists, and guards the vendor with `LEGAL_RECEIPT_MARKER_RE`. The GLOBAL path is proven unchanged (rule 36). (E) `loadOwnerTransfers` stops selecting the non-existent `passbook_transfers.created_at` (live `error_logs` 2026-11-09) — the owner's transfer history renders again.
- **Live reconciliation (rule 56 — reaches the existing user).** David's walkthrough rows were repaired and audited: `expense_logs.b2e79f10…` linked to `receipts.f4d9b896…` (`RECEIPT_LINK_BACKFILLED`) so the records page shows the receipt image now; the insurance photo was re-downloaded from Meta (267,996 bytes) and re-vaulted as `vehicle_documents.a3b4db13…` linked to its compliance row (`VEHICLE_DOCUMENT_REVAULTED`); the FIRST logbook was deliberately left (re-vaulting it would supersede the newer current file — documented in the audit row). Verified live after the writes: vehicle page 2 stored files, compliance Files count 2, records row `receiptStatus:'linked'`.
- **Deferred (decision D2).** Pre-vehicle uploads (insurance sent during registration, before the vehicle exists) are still discarded by `resolveReceiptVault`; a TODO with the exact stash-and-replay contract now lives in `media.js` for **v1.87.5**.

## [1.87.3] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 26B hotfix, found by a live send after activation.** With the three knobs set on Cloud Run, a real `saka_otp` send to the test number returned **`131008` ("buttons: Button at index 0 of type Url requires a parameter")** — the approved Authentication template carries a URL button whose parameter is the code, and our body-only payload omitted it. `saka_driver_invite` and `saka_transfer_invite` sent cleanly (wamids captured), so the hotfix is scoped to the button contract, not the transport.
- **The fix is data-driven (rule 55).** `PROACTIVE_TEMPLATE_KINDS.auth_otp` declares `buttons: [{ subType: 'url', index: 0, variable: 'code' }]`; new pure `templateButtonsForKind()` builds the ordered button components (and returns `null` when the code is missing, degrading the whole send to text rather than firing a broken button); `resolveProactiveMode` returns `buttons`; `sendWhatsAppTemplate` sends the body component first and the buttons after it; `sendWhatsAppText` and `sentinel.sendWhatsAppTemplateMessage` pass them through. Button-less kinds keep the body-only payload byte-identically.
- **Proof.** `test_proactive_templates` pins the button contract AND the exact wire payload (body `654321` + `{type:'button', sub_type:'url', index:'0', parameters:[{type:'text', text:'654321'}]}`), 64 assertions (+6). The live re-send is the operator-visible proof (see the run report).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/proactiveTemplates.js` (buttons contract + `templateButtonsForKind` + resolver), `src/services/metaMessenger.js` (components build + passthrough), `src/workers/sentinel.js` (wrapper passthrough), `docs/TEMPLATES.md` (template 14 button spec + live error), `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§219 + history + version), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `package.json` (1.87.3), `test_proactive_templates.js` (+6), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_proactive_templates (64, +6), test_sentinel_policy (30), test_error_logging (22), test_dashboard, test_env_audit, test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defect class *"a template's full component shape is not modelled in the registry"* → siblings audited: every registered kind (the other 15 are body-only and now prove `templateButtonsForKind() === []`), the deploy LIVE template (no buttons, sends live), the welcome template (not routed through this registry), and every future Authentication template (the button contract is now a declared registry field, so a missing parameter degrades to text instead of a Meta 400). Fixed: the registry + transport. Left with reasons: the legacy `web_signup_welcome` template is sent by its own path and has no buttons.

## [1.87.2] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 26C — Resend quota exhaustion can no longer be silent.** Resend answers a daily-quota exhaustion with HTTP 429 (observed live in the v1.85.0 release run), and until now every affected send failed invisibly: dashboard OTP emails, driver-assignment OTPs, transfer release PINs, trial reminders and Amina replies simply stopped. `mailer.js` gains ONE wrapper — `sendResendEmail()` — that every one of the 14 existing senders now routes through (the `{ data, error }` contract is unchanged). It detects the quota family (`statusCode/status === 429`, a `rate_limit` name or a `quota|rate limit|too many requests` message) on both returned errors and thrown ones, records `system_config.resend_quota_warned_at = YYYY-MM-DD` **once per UTC day**, writes an `admin_audit_logs` row (`RESEND_QUOTA_ALERT`) and pings the superadmin on WhatsApp once ("⚠️ Resend daily quota exhausted… paused until midnight UTC"). There is deliberately no email fallback — the email channel is exactly what is exhausted (documented, not hidden).
- **Deterministic helpers + command center.** `isResendQuotaError()` and `isResendQuotaWarnedAt()` are pure and pinned; the marker is registered in the system_config registry as `mailer.resend_quota_warned_at` with the documented legacy alias `resend_quota_warned_at` (dual-written, so the operator finds it under the briefed name). `buildCommandCenterMetrics` exposes `email { quotaWarnedAt, quotaWarnedToday }`, the deterministic digest gains an `EMAIL_QUOTA` alert, and `/d/admin/command` renders a **red banner** while today's warning is active — a previous-day marker renders nothing (the quota resets at midnight UTC).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/mailer.js` (wrapper, detection, record, 14 call sites), `src/services/systemConfigKeys.js` (+1 fixed key + constant), `src/services/commandCenter.js` (email section + alert), `src/services/dashboard.js` (banner), `scripts/health_audit.js` (suite registry), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§218 + history + version), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `package.json` (1.87.2), `test_resend_quota.js` (NEW, 22), `test_dashboard.js` (+2), `test_system_config_keys.js` (+1), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (version pin), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_resend_quota (NEW, 22), test_dashboard (870, +2), test_system_config_keys (41, +1), test_command_center (45), test_error_logging (22), test_doc_sync (30), test_kb_docs (36), `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defect class *"a provider failure that never reaches a human"* → siblings audited: Resend sends (all 14 now wrapped), Meta template/text sends (delivery guard + failed-status receipts already reach `error_logs`), Paystack webhook failures (`handlePaystackEvent` writes its own audit/error rows), AI provider failures (`ai_usage_logs.attempts` + breakers), Supabase write failures (`logWriteFailure`). Fixed: the Resend family (one wrapper, no per-function drift). Left with reasons: the AI and Paystack paths already carry their own telemetry.

## [1.87.1] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 26B — business-initiated codes now beat the 24-hour window.** Meta silently drops free-form messages sent more than 24h after the user's last message, so a returning user's dashboard sign-in code could never arrive (`/d/login` was minting a plain text). `proactiveTemplates` gains three kinds — **`auth_otp`** (`META_TPL_AUTH_OTP`, Meta **Authentication** category, `{{1}}` = the 6-digit code), **`driver_invite`** (`META_TPL_DRIVER_INVITE`, `{{1}}` = plate) and **`transfer_invite`** (`META_TPL_TRANSFER_INVITE`, `{{1}}` = plate) — and the senders pass `{ kind, templateVars }`: `requestDashboardOtp` (dashboard sign-in), `verifyOwnerAssignmentOtp` (driver ACCEPT invite) and `verifyOwnerReleaseOtpAndNotifyBuyer` (buyer ACCEPT invite). The audit also corrected an assumption: the passbook buyer's 6-digit code is EMAILED, so only the invitation needs a template.
- **One transport (rule 55).** `metaMessenger.js` now owns BOTH shapes — text and template — behind one `postWhatsAppMessage` guard/catch; `sentinel.sendWhatsAppTemplateMessage` and `src/routes/whatsapp/replies.js sendWhatsAppReply` delegate to it, so the delivery blocklist, recipient cleaning and never-throws contract can never drift. A template send that Meta rejects falls through to the text path (the 24h fallback contract is unchanged).
- **Unset = today (byte-identical).** All three knobs are empty in Cloud Run; every existing test proves the free-form fallback is unchanged. Operator action (after Meta approval): submit `saka_otp`, `saka_driver_invite`, `saka_transfer_invite`, then set the three `META_TPL_*` vars.
- **Rule 13 sync (same commit).** `docs/TEMPLATES.md` gains templates 14-16 with exact bodies, categories and variable specs; `flowKnowledge` dashboard playbook + `sakaAi` WEB DASHBOARD rule state the code arrives even after a long silence (never mention templates or Meta); `envAudit` + `.env.example` register the three knobs; knowledge docs regenerated.

## [1.87.0] - 2026-11-09
- **Phase 26A — the live "30-day trial" bug, fixed at both ends.** A sign-up that won the 6-month launch award was shown "30-day trial" copy. The live audit proved the engine was right (slot 1, coupon `LAUNCH183-1`, `trial_sessions.ends_at = 2027-05-11`) and the COPY was wrong in two places: the pre-confirm card repeated a pre-v1.16.0 "30-day trial ledger entry is created once per VIN for life" sentence, and the post-save card printed *"🎁 Free trial: 183 days, until 9 Dec 2026"* — the 183-day label with the pre-award 30-day end date (the welcome `endsAt` was captured before `grantLaunchAwardForTrial` extended the trial). New pure `resolveTrialCompletionFacts()` in `launchAwards.js` is the ONE decision: a fresh extension → the grant's end date; a repeat grant → the live `trial_sessions.ends_at`; an unreadable live end → the truthful day count and NO date; no grant (or a failed extension) → the welcome-trial facts and no award line. `quizEngine` composes the completion card from it and only appends `formatLaunchAwardLine` when the fact holds. The confirm card now reads "Your account gets one welcome trial — it starts with your first vehicle." (rule 29: trials are account-bound), and the delete-confirm bullet drops the stale "trial ledger" wording.
- **The activation nudge speaks the trial's real length.** `activationNudgeText(ownerName, tierLabel, trial)` computes the day label from `started_at`/`ends_at` instead of hardcoding "Your 30-day trial is running" — a 183-day winner now reads 183-day, a standard account still reads 30-day.
- **Rule 13 sync (same commit).** `productQa` pricing facts name the 6-month launch award (docs regenerated), `careKnowledge` subscription KB, `flowKnowledge` `subscription_upgrade.writes`, the `sakaAi` UPGRADE_SUBSCRIPTION rule, `subscriptionSchema` comments, and architecture §2.3/§18.3 (`vin_registry` = VIN provenance ledger; the asset-bound trial retired in v1.16.0).

## [1.86.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 25 — the final feature phase: nine items, two real bugs.** Every root cause was reproduced against the live tree before it was fixed. Locked decisions: **D1** the Kenya PSV badge now scopes TRUCK/HGV (Traffic Act Cap 403: a PSV carries passengers OR goods for hire), **D2** a renewed document soft-supersedes the previous one (evidence kept), **D3** the command center rides the existing 08:00 briefing (full on Mondays; no second scheduler), **D4** `GET /d/logout` becomes a 303 with a banner. ONE commit.
- **Trucks and buses now reach the compliance gate (bug 1).** Both compliance resolvers filtered on legacy tokens (`PSV/BUS/MATATU/TRUCK`) while every road asset stores `asset_type` = ICE/EV/HYBRID — the canonical identity is `asset_kind`. So a Kenyan truck/bus never matched the PSV badge (menu, gate AND quiz), and the KE speed-limiter certificate stayed hidden on trucks. New ONE bridge in `countryBucket`: `COMPLIANCE_ASSET_TOKENS` + `complianceAssetTokens()` + `complianceAssetMatches()`; both resolvers accept `assetKind` and match through it (`includeAll` and legacy `{assetType}` callers unchanged, a private car is still never PSV-class), and the kind is passed at all 13 call sites. `complianceCatalog.KE.psv_badge` gains `TRUCK`,`HGV`; `COMPLIANCE_CATALOG_VERSION` → 2026.10.2.
- **PDFs speak the asset's meter (bug 2).** The ledger header hardcoded "Odometer" (and the vehicle snapshot + distance KPI assumed one). `reportLedger.buildLedgerGroups({ meterLabel, meterId })` now carries the meter, `fleetReports.ledgerMeterFor()` decides it per scope (single asset → its meter; all-hours → **Engine hours**; all-road → **Odometer**; mixed → the honest **Reading**), `pdfEngine.buildLedgerColumns()` renames the column, `reportDelivery` switches the KPI to **Operating hours** for machines, and the missed v1.85.0 sibling (the admin fleet table) now prints `meterLabel`/`meterValue`.
- **A sign-in door that knows where you are.** `buildLoginPrefixMap()` inverts `COUNTRY_TIMEZONES` → country → prefix from `REGIONS`/`EXTRA_REGIONS` and adds the real browser zones for multi-zone markets; `/d/login` pre-fills the visitor's calling code (a suggestion, never a server-hardcoded +254). A live session is 303'd to its role home, and `GET /d/logout` now lands on `/d/login?logged_out=1` with a banner (the dead-end page is retired).
- **Every surface centered and phone-first.** Dashboard: `--page-max:1040px` shared by the header (`.bar-inner`) and content, centered KPI cards, a scrolling table card with a soft fade, a one-line swipe nav on phones, centered mobile headings, no image overflow. PDF: 8pt type floor (8.2 body, 8.0 mono, 23pt rows), long KPI values shrink instead of clipping, and the centering identity is pinned by a test. `DASHBOARD_ASSET_VERSION` → 1.86.0.
- **Landing doors.** A header **Log In** button (visible at every width, also inside the mobile drawer) and a quiet footer **King** link to the superadmin entrance; the content sweep now allows the app host ONLY for those two approved doors.
- **NEW superadmin command center.** `src/services/commandCenter.js` is the one BI builder: R1 active-paid/MRR base, R2 status buckets, R3 trials, R4 shells/verification, R5 zero-asset owners, R6 support desk, R7 AI telemetry (`getAiUsageSummary` + the new `getAiUsageByOwner`, background spend never divided per user). MRR is list prices from the tier matrix; AI cost is an estimate; every section is fail-soft and phone-masked. `/d/admin/command` renders it (audited `ADMIN_DASHBOARD_VIEW {page:'command'}`), `admin command|bi|business|kpi` returns a ≤20-line deterministic digest, and the 08:00 briefing now carries the full digest on Mondays and a 4-line pulse otherwise.
- **Money bug fixed in the superadmin digest.** `handleAdminReportCommand` printed a hardcoded KES over ledgers written in many currencies; it now groups by currency (a row without a currency column reads as USD) and names the grouping.
- **Renewals replace instead of piling up.** `vehicle_documents` gains `superseded_at`/`superseded_by` (+ a partial current-file index) via `npm run migrate:document-replacement` (idempotent, also applied by the boot ensure). A same-type re-upload supersedes the previous row and clears its compliance link while KEEPING the row + file (rule 48); the current list is the default, `findPendingDocument` never links a replaced file, the vehicle page offers **Show replaced (N)** (`?docs=all`), `/d/compliance` counts current files only, and the WhatsApp list says "replaces N earlier file(s)".
- **Creative handover labels.** New `ASSET_ROLES` (car/truck/bus → Driver, motorcycle → **Rider**, machinery/equipment/generator → **Operator**, boat → **Captain**) with `assetRoleLabel/Plural/Possessive`. `quizEngine.field()` gains `labelFor()` resolved by ONE helper used by the question and the confirmation, so the assign flow asks for the "Operator's WhatsApp number" while the extraction key stays `driver_phone`; `/d/transfer` names the reading from the fleet mix and re-labels on the vehicle picker.
- **Rule 13 sync.** `flowKnowledge` assign/unassign playbooks now state the role vocabulary and the kind-scoped PSV rule; `docs/` regenerated; no new chat intent or flow was added.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Class: hardcoded label / wrong identity column on a kind-aware surface* → PDF ledger, PDF snapshot, PDF KPI, admin fleet table, records CSV/import, transfer form, quiz handover, all 13 compliance call sites and the superadmin money block were swept; fixed vs deliberately-left documented in §215.5, with one targeted assertion per sibling.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/{countryBucket,complianceCatalog,vehicleCompliance,quizEngine,driverAssignments,complianceAnswers,reportLedger,fleetReports,reportDelivery,pdfEngine,dashboard,vehicleDocuments,commandCenter(new),aiUsageLog,superadmin,flowKnowledge}.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `src/routes/whatsapp/{messages,pending,commands}.js`, `scripts/run_vehicle_documents_migration.js` (+`migrate:document-replacement`), `scripts/mockSupabase.js`, `landing/{index.html,assets/css/styles.css,assets/js/nav.js}`, `package.json` (1.86.0), `test_driver_assignment_gate` (63), `test_vehicle_compliance` (71), `test_country_bucket` (86), `test_variable_catalogs` (100), `test_pdf_engine` (92), `test_fleet_reports` (57), `test_report_builders` (88), `test_dashboard` (867), `test_quiz_flow` (176), `test_vehicle_documents` (74), `test_command_center` (new, 45), `test_superadmin`, `test_landing_static`, `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§215 + history + version), this file.

## [1.85.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 24 — the final audit, then one fix release.** Stage 1 was a READ-ONLY audit of three domains (8 asset kinds × 6 flows, 6 lifecycles, 14 country buckets × 7 concerns) against the live tree; every gap below was reproduced before it was fixed. No P0 was found (no money leak, no data loss, no security hole). Locked decisions: P1 fixes 1-5 + P2 6/7/9/10/12 shipped; CA stays behind an explicit `country Canada` choice (+1 is US), BE gets a real +32 prefix; ONE commit.
- **Meters now follow the asset (P1-1).** `fleetReports.vehicleDisplay()` branches on `primaryMeterForAssetKind()` and returns `{meterLabel, meterId, meterValue, meterUnit}`; `vehicleSummary()` carries it into `renderVehiclesPage`/`renderVehiclePage`, so heavy equipment, machinery, generators and watercraft show **Engine hours** and never "Odometer 0 km" (road assets keep the odometer + imperial conversion).
- **`/d/analytics` is kind-aware (P1-4).** `vehicleAnalytics` gains `parseHoursFromNotes` (the `Engine hours: N h` note), `hoursInWindow`, `buildCostPerHourTrend` and `buildServiceIntervalRows` (active HOURS reminders with live readings); `buildTyreWearRows()` filters hours-led assets out at the source. `buildAnalyticsPage` flags `hasRoad`/`hasHours` (reminders best-effort) and `renderAnalyticsPage` renders tyre wear + cost per km for road assets and service intervals + cost per operating hour for machines.
- **Passbook fee in the buyer's currency (P1-2) + non-road identity (P2-6).** `formatTransferFeeLine()` renders "USD 3.00 (~NGN 4,500)" from the buyer's E.164 country (a hardcoded KES is impossible now); road transfers show VIN, equipment shows Serial (never "VIN: N/A"); the welcome email's identity + meter follow the asset (subject `Asset Passbook Issued`).
- **Driver handover meter per kind (P1-5).** `handoverMeterFields({ mode, ctx })` asks engine hours for hours-led assets on assign/unassign; `createDriverAssignment({ handoverHours })` stores `metadata.hours_at_assignment` (odometer column null) and the unassign completion writes `operating_hours`, never `current_mileage`. `/d/transfer` asks for a "Handover reading" and routes hours through `applyOperatingHours`.
- **Canada + Belgium (P1-3).** BE (+32, EUR, nl-BE, Europe/Brussels) joins REGIONS with its own vehicle compliance catalog (2026.18.0); CA becomes resolvable through `EXTRA_REGIONS` + `resolveCountryInput('Canada')` (CAD/en-CA/America/Toronto) and `routes/dashboard.countryForProfile()` now prefers the stored `profiles.country_code`; CAD seed rate, landing dataset + homepage bootstrap map extended.
- **Country-correct driver documents (P2-7).** `driverComplianceCatalog` gains `commercial_licence`, `driver_card`, `heavy_vehicle_permit`, `hrt_licence`, `white_card` with per-country menus (GB/US/CA/AE/IN/AU/BE), mapped into licence/permit categories.
- **Road rules stay on roads (P2-9).** COUNTRY_KB DEFAULT + KE insurance/inspection are scoped to road kinds; hours-led assets get an optional `equipment_insurance` hint.
- **Records/import labels per kind (P2-12).** `recordsMeterLabel`/`attachMeterReadings` + a dynamic CSV header (Odometer / Engine hours / Reading); the import preview label follows the fleet and hours-led imports write `Engine hours: N h` into the notes, keep the odometer null and advance `operating_hours`.
- **Delete-account clears the session snapshot (P2-10).** `delete_account` also deletes `chat_sessions`.
- **Rule 13 sync.** `flowKnowledge` assign/unassign/transfer playbooks and the dashboard playbook now state the kind-aware meters, the buyer-currency fee and CA/BE; no new intent or flow was added.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Hardcoded meter label* → every dashboard/PDF/CSV surface swept (dashboard fixed; raw ledger columns deliberately left, documented). *Hardcoded currency* → transfers fixed; trial cards, subscription page and monthly/weekly summaries verified already-local. *Country missing from REGIONS* → all 14 KB bundles + driver catalogs compared; BE added, CA handled as an extra region.

## [1.84.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 23 — document storage (D1/D2/D3 locked).** Compliance documents were OCR'd for their fields and then the FILE was discarded, so an owner had no logbook/insurance PDF to show at a police stop or to a buyer. The new `src/services/vehicleDocuments.js` keeps the ORIGINAL (photo or PDF) in a **private `documents` bucket** at `<vehicle_id>/<uuid>.<ext>` plus a `vehicle_documents` row: `id`, `vehicle_id` FK `ON DELETE CASCADE`, `document_type` (`logbook | insurance | inspection | ntsa_cert | pinkslip | road_license | other`), `storage_path` (NULL on Free), `original_filename`, `parsed_data jsonb`, `compliance_id` FK → `vehicle_compliances(id)` `ON DELETE SET NULL` (guarded constraint `vehicle_documents_compliance_fk`), **`media_id text UNIQUE`** (a Meta retry or re-sent document is never stored twice — D1), `uploaded_by`, `uploaded_at`. Paid tiers + welcome trial store the file; **Free keeps the parsed row only** (`storage_path = null`, receipt-vault contract — D2). Driver documents stay OUT (vehicle-only ledger, §40.2 — D3). Migration `npm run migrate:vehicle-documents` (idempotent) + boot ensure next to receipts; knob `DOCUMENTS_BUCKET` (default `documents`) and `DOCUMENT_LINK_WINDOW_HOURS` (24) in `.env.example`. **Operator prerequisite: create the `documents` bucket AS PRIVATE** (same shape as `receipts`).
- **The vault rides the proven seams.** `resolveReceiptVault()` (media.js) gains the compliance-document branch for the four vehicle families — never receipts, never driver licences — and returns `documentPrefill = { document_id, document_type }` through the SAME engine-only prefill seam as `receipt_id` (v1.81.0). The bytes already exist for images AND PDFs, so there is no second download; `vaultVehicleDocument` never throws, removes the object if the row insert fails, and degrades to a file-less row on Free.
- **The file links to what it proves (rule 51 sweep, four sites).** All four `submitVehicleCompliance` callers now call `linkSavedComplianceDocument` after a successful save: the paragraph path (`messages.js`), the capture turn (`pending.js` — `documentId` threaded through `persistCapturedCompliance`), the typed correction (`complianceAnswers.applyComplianceCorrection` gained `documentId`) and the registration seeds (`quizEngine.js`, `a.document_id` from the prefill). The explicit id wins; the newest UNLINKED file matching vehicle+type inside 24 h is the deterministic fallback; no match links nothing.
- **Dashboard.** `/d/vehicles/:id` gains a **Stored files** section (📄 label + filename + upload date, `View`, Delete with a confirm dialog; a Free row reads "parsed only (Free plan keeps the figures, not the file)"); `/d/compliance` gains a **Files** column with the per-vehicle count; **`GET /d/documents/:id`** mirrors the receipt route exactly (owner session → fleet ownership → 300-second signed redirect, `no-store`; foreign/missing/no-file = **404**, and no signed URL is ever minted for a foreign row); **`POST /d/documents/:id/delete`** is Origin-guarded (same defence as `/d/settings`), ownership-checked, audits `VEHICLE_DOCUMENT_DELETED` and 303s back to the vehicle page.
- **WhatsApp lanes.** `documents` / `my documents` / `vehicle documents` / `stored documents` / `document vault` → a new `documents_list` read lane (📄 newest first, plate + date, honest Free chip), registered after `receipts_list` with `skipWhenLocked` + `skipWhenPendingCapture` so a pending capture still owns its turn; `documents list`, `required documents`, `what documents can I send?` and `list all documents with expiry date` keep their lanes. `delete document N` (1-5, literal `DELETE` to confirm) mirrors `delete receipt N`: the confirm prompt shows the numbered list, the delete removes the file and **keeps the compliance dates**, and the audit is `VEHICLE_DOCUMENT_DELETED`.
- **Lifecycle carriers (rules 51/56).** `vehicle_documents` joined `RESET_TABLES` + the phone-wipe SQL + the global TRUNCATE, `testCleanup.js` (by vehicle AND uploader) and `wipeProfileForSignup` (rows + bucket objects, reported as `document_storage_objects`). `mockSupabase` gained `storage.remove` (recorded in `state.removals`) and PostgREST `in.(a,b)` OR-group parsing — additive.
- **Rule 13/60 sync.** `FLOW_GUIDANCE.document_vault`, the `STORED DOCUMENTS (v1.84.0)` core-prompt block, the composer's stored-documents law, and the training suite (219, +4). `DASHBOARD_ASSET_VERSION` deliberately stays `1.83.0` (no CSS/asset change; the pinned Test 53 stamp keeps its meaning).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/vehicleDocuments.js` (new, 429), `scripts/run_vehicle_documents_migration.js` (new), `src/routes/whatsapp/{media,messages,pending,quiz,commands}.js`, `src/services/{readCommands,complianceAnswers,quizEngine,dashboard,profileWipe,flowKnowledge,conversationEngine}.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `index.js`, `scripts/{reset-db,testCleanup,mockSupabase}.js`, `.env.example`, `package.json` (1.84.0 + `migrate:vehicle-documents`), `test_vehicle_documents.js` (new, 60), `test_dashboard.js` (816, +20), `test_ocr_flow.js` (138, +6), `test_read_registry.js` (224, +16), `test_profile_wipe.js` (15, +1), `test_db_reset_scope.js` (99, +1), `test_agent_training.js` (219, +4), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped assets, `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§213 + history + version), this file.

## [1.83.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 22A — premium dashboard branding (D1).** The dashboard now wears the Saka brand: self-hosted **Sora** (600/700 headings) + **Manrope** (400/500/700 body) served by a new **`GET /d/assets/:file`** route whose STRICT filename allowlist builds the filesystem path from a table (traversal impossible by construction; `font/woff2`/`image/png`/`image/svg+xml`, `Cache-Control: immutable`, `nosniff`, 240/5-min). `DASHBOARD_CSS` swaps the orange accent for the Monolith lime (`--accent:#B8EF5C`, text-safe `--accent-dark:#65A30D`) and one `--glow` ring that lights focused inputs, hovered/focused buttons and active/focus-within cards; the header renders the real `logo-lockup.png` (177x18, intrinsic 9.86:1, `alt="Saka Fleet"`) plus favicons. Phone-first pass: a 640 px breakpoint wraps the header, raises buttons to 48 px (secondary 44 px), sets 16 px controls (no iOS zoom), two-column KPIs, and honours `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- **Phase 22B — PDF polish (D2, revised by a live probe).** `SAKA_PDF_THEME` brand is the text-safe lime `#65A30D` with decorative `#B8EF5C` kept for fills only (teal retires as default), and `SAKA_PDF_GEOMETRY` exports the A4 + 48 pt margin contract. **Sora via WOFF2 was tried and rejected:** fontkit's WOFF2 subsetting rendered the first document then threw `Offset is outside the bounds of the DataView` on the second in-process build (a filtered records PDF 500'd). `registerReportFonts` now tries a vendored **Sora TTF/OTF** first and otherwise keeps the vendored **Barlow SemiBold**; WOFF2 is never registered for PDFs (pinned by the suite).
- **Phase 22C — passbook transfer page (D3).** `GET /d/transfer` + `POST /d/transfer/{start,verify,cancel}` run the SAME `transfers.js` machine for steps 1-3 on the web: pick a vehicle, enter the buyer's WhatsApp number + email + an optional handover odometer (written as a labelled `odometer_readings` row), then type the 6-digit release PIN Saka emails the OWNER. **The buyer's ACCEPT + buyer email OTP stay on WhatsApp** (steps 4-5 untouched). Buyer phones/emails render masked, `release_otp`/`buyer_otp` are never selected, in-flight/status chips are plain language, cancel is a status transition. A phone-only owner sees "Add an email first" and the server refuses too (never a stranded transfer); verify/cancel are ownership-checked and a foreign id answers 404.
- **Phase 22D — subscription page (D4 + D5).** `GET /d/subscription` shows plan, status, trial end + days left, period end, capacity bar (add-ons included), report quota and owner-scoped billing history (`subscriptions` ledger + invoice events re-checked against `payload.metadata.user_id`, degrading to the ledger). Upgrades use `checkoutForTier` and Fleet Command slots use `checkoutForAddon` — both via new injectable seams, both 303 to Paystack and refuse non-HTTPS URLs. `initializePaystackCheckout` gained an optional **`callbackUrl`** (default behaviour byte-identical for every existing caller) so the dashboard returns to `/d/subscription`; the page prints the reference with "Paystack confirms payments in the background" and never fakes success. **Add Vehicle = `https://wa.me/19404407252?text=add%20car`** (registration stays in the quiz); at capacity the link becomes the engine-priced upgrade and/or add-on forms. Nav gains Transfer + Subscription.
- **Rule 13/60 sync.** `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK` reheaded v1.83.0 with both new pages (owner steps 1-3, buyer consent on WhatsApp, PIN never shown, engine prices only, no card/refund actions); `FLOW_GUIDANCE` gains `dashboard_transfer` + `dashboard_subscription`; `DEFAULT_SAKA_CORE_PROMPT` names both pages; `docs:gen` regenerated the knowledge docs.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/{dashboard,pdfEngine,paystack,subscriptions,flowKnowledge}.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_dashboard.js` (796, +100), `test_pdf_engine.js` (79, +11), `test_subscription_billing.js` (108, +4), `test_agent_training.js` (215, +6), `package.json` (1.83.0), `landing/data/changelog.json` + stamped landing assets, `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§212 + §47.2 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_dashboard (796), test_pdf_engine (79), test_subscription_billing (108), test_agent_training (215), test_landing_static, test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Classes swept: a "supported" font format that only fails on second use (woff2 banned for PDFs + assertion); a heading rendering while its form is withheld (`ownerEmailAvailable` gate); escaped-vs-raw copy in assertions (`&#39;` pins); cross-owner id replay on verify/cancel (404); an engine URL used as a redirect (HTTPS-only + 502); fixtures that silently hit FX over the network (+1 number → USD); identifier collisions in the suite (`p22FleetMock`). Deliberately left: `transfers.js` OTP storage/compare, buyer-side lanes, `handlePaystackEvent`, pricing registry.

## [1.82.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 21A — rules 62 + 63 (additive).** Rule 62: default to full operator capability — use every available connection/tool/integration and execute pending operator actions autonomously. Rule 63: query pending remediations/proposals before every operation, audit the change, then offer/execute. No existing rule edited (rule 58 additive policy).
- **Phase 21B — the +254734852529 ghost, fixed at the source.** Live probes found the shell profile still LIVE (`deleted_at NULL`, no email, no name, created by a first-contact "Dashboard" message at 10:20:07Z) plus 4 `chat_history`, 4 `chat_transcripts`, 2 `processed_webhooks`, 1 `chat_sessions` row and 18 `conversation_analytics` synthesis blobs mentioning the number — **the earlier wipe never ran** (`admin_audit_logs` holds no `ADMIN_PROFILE_WIPED` row, only CONV_EVAL_HEALED confirmation prompts). New `scripts/purge_ghost_profile.js` (dry-run default, `--apply`) hard-deletes the shell + chat rows by phone variants, **redacts** the number inside the synthesis history (never deletes unrelated analytics), audits `GHOST_PROFILE_PURGED` with before/removed/after counts and re-counts every table — run verified 0 residue. `handleProfilesReportCommand` now counts **Registered** = `email IS NOT NULL` OR the superadmin's row, names **Unverified Shells** separately (and `details.named_no_email` for named no-email driver rows), and filters 24h/7d signups + Recent sign-ups to registered only; a profiles read failure answers one honest line. Live after: 2 registered / 0 shells. `test_superadmin` pins all four definitions.
- **Phase 21C — WhatsApp-native dashboard sign-in (D1–D5).** Static door `https://app.sakafleet.com/d/login` (+ `/d/admin/login`): the user enters their WhatsApp number and the 6-digit code arrives **in WhatsApp** (10 minutes); a successful verify sets a **strict 15-minute** `saka_dash` cookie (`HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/d; Max-Age=900; Secure`) and lands each role on its own home (`/d`, `/d/driver`, `/d/admin`). `requestDashboardAccess` → **`requestDashboardOtp`** (phone variants, ghost guard, admin-door-first check, owner trial/paid gate — the email-verification gate is retired, driver live-assignment gate, 60 s cooldown, one WhatsApp text, send-failure deletes the row, audit `DASHBOARD_OTP_SENT`). `verifyDashboardOtp` keeps every mechanic (sha256 at rest, timing-safe compare, 5-attempt lock, single-use burn, role re-check) and returns the role. **D4:** `/d/login` never mints an admin session — the superadmin must use `/d/admin/login`; `/d/admin*` still answers 404 to everyone else. **D5:** `sendDashboardMagicLinkEmail` kept but deprecated and uncalled. The `dashboard` / `records` lane ships the static link + a 3-line tutorial (no DB write, zero tokens), `dashboard admin` ships the admin door, and the Phase 16 report lane appends the same tutorial to its instant-PDF answer. New `src/services/metaMessenger.js` (`sendWhatsAppText`, extracted from `sendProactiveWhatsApp`, sentinel delegates) so the worker and the OTP cannot drift. `renderLoginPage` added; `renderOtpPage` speaks WhatsApp; `accountLabel()` masks the phone when email is null; `GET /d/:token` still serves pre-1.82 links. **Rule 13/60 sync:** `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK`, `FLOW_GUIDANCE.dashboard` and the core prompt all state the new flow.
- **Frames touched.** `.clinerules`, `scripts/purge_ghost_profile.js` (new), `src/services/{superadmin,metaMessenger(new),dashboard,readCommands,flowKnowledge}.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `src/routes/whatsapp/commands.js`, `src/workers/sentinel.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_dashboard.js` (696), `test_read_registry.js` (206), `test_superadmin.js` (+8), `test_agent_training.js` (209), `test_report_lane.js` (34, +2), `package.json` (1.82.0), `landing/data/changelog.json`, `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§211 + history + version), this file.
