## [1.81.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 20 — the durable receipt→expense link (PROP-2026-11-08-01).** A receipt and the expense its confirmation quiz wrote were linked only by a heuristic (same vehicle + same Nairobi day + same amount, unique-only) because no FK existed. `expense_logs.receipt_id` (`npm run migrate:receipt-links`, idempotent, `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS … REFERENCES receipts(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` + index) is now the durable link. **`ON DELETE SET NULL` is mandatory**: `delete receipt N` (rule 48) removes the evidence row and must never orphan or block the ledger.
- **Where the id was lost and how it rides now.** `resolveReceiptVault()` discarded `recordReceipt()`'s returned row; it now returns `receiptPrefill = { receipt_id }` (receipt branches only — never logbooks/insurance). The id travels the engine-only `prefill` seam proven by `logbookPrefill` (`messages.js` → `arbitrateQuizTurn` → `quiz.js` → `startQuiz` answers), so it is never asked, never rendered and **never AI-injectable**; `restart` drops it. The write path verifies it first (`resolveReceiptLink`: UUID shape → one receipts read → owner/driver profile OR the expense's own vehicle; else null, never throws — rule 50), writes `receipt_id: linkedReceiptId`, and the existing label-missing retry gains a second degrade that drops `receipt_id` too when the column is un-migrated.
- **The records table links exactly (D1 fallback kept).** `attachReceiptMatches()` resolves every `raw.receipt_id` with ONE `.in('id', …)` query → `receiptStatus: 'linked'`; rows written before the backfill keep the unchanged unique-match heuristic (`unique|ambiguous|none`), and a vanished id degrades to it. `renderRecordsPage` shows View for `linked` and `unique` alike.
- **The backfill is dry-run by default (D4).** `npm run backfill:receipt-links` probes the column (pre-migration runs are told to migrate, never "0 rows"), loads every linked receipt id (1:1 enforcement), snapshots unlinked rows, matches with the SAME `matchReceiptForExpense()` + `nairobiDateOf()`, links unique-only unused receipts, and audits `RECEIPT_LINK_BACKFILLED`. `-- --apply` writes. Idempotent: only `receipt_id IS NULL` rows are read.
- **Phase 20 — CSV bulk import at `/d/import`.** Upload or paste a CSV (`Date, Vehicle Plate, Type, Amount, Odometer, Notes`); the engine parses it with a hand-rolled RFC-4180-lite parser (BOM, CRLF, quoted commas/newlines, `""` escapes, header aliases, byte/row caps — **zero new dependencies, no multipart**), validates every row through the SAME sources the chat uses (owner's live vehicles for plates, the expenseCatalog for types, `dateParser` for country-aware dates stored at Nairobi noon, positive amounts ≤ 100 M, optional whole-number odometer, notes ≤ 500, in-file plus 3-hour DB duplicates where a different odometer reading is a separate fill) with **two DB reads total** for the whole file, shows a preview with invalid rows highlighted in engine copy, and only then bulk-inserts the valid rows in chunks of 200.
- **The import respects the engine's laws.** `driver_id` is the acting owner profile, rows carry no invented receipt link, the legacy `expenses` mirror honours its kill switch, each vehicle's odometer advances to the highest imported reading only, **`markUserDataChanged` fires ONCE** (`dashboard.csvImport`) and the run is audited `CSV_IMPORT`. **Rule 26 is pinned by a source assertion: the module imports no activation API — an import can never start or extend a trial**, and it never touches the report quota. The two-phase confirm re-parses and re-validates the submitted text (a tampered hidden field can only fail), refuses cross-origin writes, rate-limits both POSTs and answers an honest 502 with the exact saved count when a chunk is refused. Knobs: `CSV_IMPORT_MAX_ROWS` (500), `CSV_IMPORT_MAX_BYTES` (1 MB); nav gains **Import**.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/csvImport.js` (new), `src/services/quizEngine.js`, `src/services/dashboard.js`, `src/routes/whatsapp/{media,messages,quiz}.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `src/services/{envAudit,flowKnowledge}.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `scripts/{run_receipt_links_migration,backfill_receipt_links}.js` (new), `scripts/{provision_staging_schema,health_audit}.js`, `.env.example`, `test_csv_import.js` (new, 70), `test_quiz_flow.js` (165), `test_ocr_flow.js` (132), `test_dashboard.js` (672), `test_agent_training.js` (208), `package.json` (1.81.0 + 2 scripts), `landing/data/changelog.json`, `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§210 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_csv_import (new, 70), test_quiz_flow (165), test_ocr_flow (132), test_dashboard (672), test_agent_training (208), test_entry_ledger, test_record_answers, test_expense_catalog, test_user_context_cache, test_error_logging, test_doc_sync, test_no_canned_replies, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Whole class swept: (i) a foreign receipt linking to an expense → ownership guard + pinned foreign scenario; (ii) a deleted receipt orphaning an expense → `ON DELETE SET NULL`; (iii) a re-uploaded CSV double-logging → in-file key + 3-hour guard + refuse-not-truncate caps; (iv) an import triggering trial activation → no activation API imported (source pin); (v) cost-per-km drift → N/A (Phase 19), import writes only the ledger and the odometer. Deliberately left: the WhatsApp quick-log lane never receives receipt context by construction, so it needs no link code.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/45, 3/4/5, 7, 8, 10/11, 12/33, 13/31/60, 26/28, 37/48, 42/44, 50, 51/52, 54, 55, 56, 58. No rule was edited or added.

## [1.80.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 19 — Amina, the autonomous care agent.** Support email at support@reports.sakafleet.com is no longer just a ticket queue: `processCareEmail` (`src/services/careAi.js`) investigates every inbound email before deciding anything. She reads the customer's profile and vehicles, the last 5 `error_logs` that mention them, the last 10 WhatsApp messages they exchanged with Saka and the Paystack billing mirror through four engine-executed tools (`get_profile_context`, `get_recent_errors`, `get_recent_chat`, `get_payment_status`), in up to 3 `care_investigation` rounds. If her confidence clears `CARE_AI_CONFIDENCE_FLOOR` (0.7) she replies herself by email and marks the ticket **RESOLVED**; otherwise she **ESCALATES** with a named investigation ("What I investigated … Where I am stuck …"), forwards to `support@sakafleet.com` with an `[SFC-<id>]` subject tag, pings the superadmin and stores her best draft (`ai_draft`, labelled unsent). **`CARE_AI_ENABLED` is OFF by default** — until the operator flips it, every email follows the exact v1.79.0 human path (zero model calls).
- **Money never reaches the model.** A zero-token pre-gate (`carePreGate`) sends refunds, duplicate charges, Paystack/payment disputes, legal/account actions and high-priority mail straight to a human, with the reason recorded. Amina can never quote a price, promise a refund or change a ledger, VIN, subscription or account (rules 8/28/46).
- **Amina learns from every human reply.** A reply on a `[SFC-…]` subject from `support@sakafleet.com` routes into the SAME ticket as an internal message (never a duplicate ticket), and `care_learning` extracts ONE reusable, de-identified rule (`sanitizeCareLesson` scrubs emails/phones/long numbers; refund-only replies yield nothing). Lessons land in the new `care_learnings` table and the newest 10 are injected into every future investigation — prompt-level hints only, never code, prices or permissions.
- **Threading fixed (rule 51 sweep).** `findOpenTicketByThread` now finds RESOLVED/CLOSED/ESCALATED tickets inside the 7-day window: a customer follow-up **reopens the same ticket** instead of opening a duplicate, while an escalated ticket stays human-owned (Amina never re-answers). `ESCALATED` joins the console (`care inbox escalated`, stats, chips) and `care read` shows Amina's draft; the webhook's legacy self-relay is skipped when Amina handled the mail.
- **Schema (`npm run migrate:care-ai`, idempotent).** `support_tickets.ai_draft` + the `care_learnings` lesson ledger; every write degrades on an un-migrated schema (rule 50). AI purposes `care_triage`, `care_investigation` and `care_learning` join `AI_PURPOSES` as background purposes (DeepSeek leg, never the front tier).
- **`/d/care` — the superadmin's care queue.** Escalated tickets with Amina's **investigation log**, her unsent draft, the console reply hint and the **lessons learned**, every visit audited (`ADMIN_DASHBOARD_VIEW { page:'care' }`). Owner/driver sessions get a 404.
- **`/d/analytics` — tyre wear + cost-per-km (D3/D4).** Per vehicle: the newest Tyres expense with an odometer reading starts the clock; 25,000 km on a 50,000 km set = **~50% life remaining** (spec example pinned), **Replace Soon** at 40,000 km, both env-tunable. Plus a six-month monthly trend of spend ÷ distance (the SAME exported `distanceInWindow` engine every report uses — zero formula drift) rendered as a **server-rendered inline SVG** (grid, polyline, titled points, month labels) with zero new dependencies. Shared nav gains **Analytics**.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/{careAi,careKnowledge,careLearning,vehicleAnalytics}.js` (new), `src/services/{careConsole,inboundEmail,customerCare,mailer,aiClient,envAudit,fleetReports,dashboard}.js`, `src/routes/dashboard.js`, `index.js`, `scripts/run_care_ai_migration.js` (new), `scripts/health_audit.js`, `.env.example`, `docs/CARE_WORKER_GUIDE.md`, `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `test_care_ai.js` (new, 59), `test_care_learning.js` (new, 36), `test_vehicle_analytics.js` (new, 37), `test_inbound_email.js` (41), `test_care_worker.js` (54), `test_dashboard.js` (644), `package.json` (1.80.0), `landing/data/changelog.json`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§209), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_care_ai (59), test_care_learning (36), test_vehicle_analytics (37), test_inbound_email (41), test_care_worker (54), test_dashboard (644), test_fleet_reports (42), test_env_audit (59), test_no_canned_replies (15 — ratchet still 4), test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Unfiltered relay bypasses the new logic → relay skipped for AI-handled/internal mail; a new status missing from a filter → ESCALATED added everywhere; a reply opening a duplicate ticket → reopen + `[SFC-]` routing; cost-per-km formula drift → one exported `distanceInWindow`; a lesson carrying PII/money → sanitizer + refusals. Deliberately left: the WhatsApp support intake stays deterministic and human-owned.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/45, 3/4, 7, 8, 10/11, 12/33, 13/31, 16/18/46, 20, 21, 28, 32, 42, 50, 51/52, 54, 55, 56, 58, 60. `CARE_AI_ENABLED` was NOT silently enabled. No rule was edited or added.

## [1.79.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 18 — voice notes are understood, not turned away.** A WhatsApp voice note used to get one deterministic line (*"I can't listen to voice notes yet"*, v1.61.15). Now `startAudioTranscription` (`media.js`) downloads the audio with the SAME `downloadWhatsAppMedia` used by OCR (5 MB cap, curl User-Agent) and transcribes it via the new `src/services/sttService.js` (Google Cloud Speech-to-Text v1 REST `speech:recognize`, base64 `audio.content`, OGG_OPUS, `?key=` on `GCP_SPEECH_API_KEY` or the existing `GCP_VISION_API_KEY` — zero new npm deps). The transcript is injected at the existing typed-text seam (`voiceTranscript || interactiveReply?.text || mediaTurnText`), so a voice note can log an expense, ask a question **and answer an active quiz** exactly like typing.
- **Country-resolved languages (rule 7).** KE phones recognise `sw-KE` with `en-KE`/`en-US` alternatives; every other country leads `en-US` with `sw-KE` available. Duration guard 55 s (Google's sync cap is 60 s — verified against the official docs, so the "2 minutes" plan was corrected), size guard 5 MB, one retry on abort/timeout only, HTTP/credential errors never retried.
- **Gated, acked, telemetered, degraded.** Unverified numbers hit the same signup gate as image/document media BEFORE any download/STT spend (D2); a deterministic *"🎙️ Listening to your voice note — one moment…"* ack precedes the work (instant/slow acks suppressed so no double progress line); one `ocr_requests` row per voice turn (`provider='google_speech'`, `document_kind='voice_note'`, status/confidence/attempts/latency/error — no DDL, D4). Every failure (paused switch, missing key, oversize, over-duration, timeout, silence) sends the UNCHANGED `VOICE_NOTE_REPLY` with the UNCHANGED `voice_note_fallback` status + `[voice note]` marker and logs one rolled-up `logSystemError` (`/webhook/whatsapp/voice`) — audio bytes are never stored.
- **Operator prerequisite.** Enable **Cloud Speech-to-Text API** in the same GCP project and allow it on the Vision key's restrictions (or set `GCP_SPEECH_API_KEY`); documented in `README.md` and `.env.example`. **Live probe 2026-11-08:** the current `GCP_VISION_API_KEY` returns `403 API_KEY_SERVICE_BLOCKED` for `speech.googleapis.com` (restricted to Vision), so transcription stays dormant — and every voice note degrades to the fallback line — until the operator unblocks the API on the key. Knobs: `STT_ENABLED` (on), `GCP_SPEECH_API_KEY`, `GCP_SPEECH_ENDPOINT`, `STT_TIMEOUT_MS` (25000), `STT_MAX_ATTEMPTS` (2), `STT_MAX_DURATION_SECONDS` (55), `STT_ESTIMATED_BITRATE` (16000).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/sttService.js` (new, 208 lines), `src/routes/whatsapp/media.js` (imports, `VOICE_LISTENING_ACK`, `prepareVoiceAck`, `startAudioTranscription`), `src/routes/whatsapp/messages.js` (voice gate, combined ack, 3-way `Promise.all`, transcript/fallback seam, ack guards), `src/services/envAudit.js` (+7 keys, +1 secret), `.env.example` (+STT block), `README.md`, `scripts/health_audit.js` (+1 stateless suite), `test_stt_service.js` (new, 26 assertions), `test_quiz_flow.js` (+3), `test_env_audit.js` (+15), `test_no_canned_replies.js` (+1), `package.json` (1.79.0), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `docs/` (regenerated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§24 note, §208 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_stt_service (new, 26), test_quiz_flow (160 — +3), test_env_audit (59 — +15), test_no_canned_replies (15 — ratchet still 4), test_ocr_flow (129), test_interactive_reply (48), test_conv_eval (236), `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Class "an inbound message type degraded to a canned line and was never reclaimed"* → siblings audited: sticker/reaction/location (deliberately left, no data lane, pinned), interactive button/list taps (resolved since v1.73.4), audio-with-caption (`text.body` present → still a text turn), photo/document media (unchanged, re-proven). *Class "a provider call that could spend money before authentication"* → the voice gate now runs before download/STT. *Class "two progress acks on one slow turn"* → instant + slow acks excluded for voice turns.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/45, 3/4, 7 (language from the phone), 10/11, 12/33, 13/31 (no intent/flow change; warm one-step copy), 21, 32, 42 (open proposal reconciled: `PROP-2026-11-08-01` only, unrelated), 50, 51/52, 54 (Google 60 s limit verified), 55 (reused downloader/gate/seam/telemetry table; zero new deps), 56, 58. No rule was edited or added.

## [1.78.1] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 17.5 hotfix — the `deploy_live` template now carries the release identity.** The operator updated `saka_deploy_live` in Meta to a two-variable body (`🚀 Saka is LIVE!` / `{{1}}` / `{{2}}` / "The new revision is now answering WhatsApp conversations."). `PROACTIVE_TEMPLATE_KINDS.deploy_live.variables` is now `['version','commit']`; `announceRevisionLive()` builds `templateVars = { version: 'v<version>', commit: <K_REVISION | COMMIT_SHA | RENDER_GIT_COMMIT> }`, passes it as the second argument to the injected `send(text, templateVars)` and returns it; the `index.js` boot hook forwards it into `sendMetaWhatsAppMessage(..., 'deploy_live', templateVars)`. An `unknown` version (architecture-file read failed) passes an empty string so `resolveProactiveMode` degrades to the free-form text — never a broken Meta variable (rule 50). The free-form fallback text is byte-identical and an unset knob behaves exactly as before.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/{proactiveTemplates,deployLiveAlert}.js`, `index.js` (boot send seam only), `test_proactive_templates.js` (52 assertions, +5), `test_notifications.js` (22, +3), `docs/TEMPLATES.md` (§9 body + §3.1 runbook), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped asset URLs), `package.json` (1.78.1), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§68.1, §207 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_proactive_templates (52), test_notifications (22), test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class "a registry slot declared static while the approved Meta body gained placeholders"* → all 12 sibling kinds re-audited against `docs/TEMPLATES.md`; none besides `deploy_live` changed, `eval_digest_ping` and `care_escalation` stay static and remain pinned, and placeholder drift now degrades to text through `templateVariablesForKind` → null (two new route assertions pin it).

## [1.78.0] - 2026-11-08
- **Phase 17 — a superadmin who can just ask.** Verified against the live tree: the WhatsApp superadmin hub only answered exact commands (`admin ai-stats`, …), and every other message from the superadmin phone got the static role hub. New `processAdminMessage()` (`src/services/superadmin.js`) answers natural language with a two-call, engine-owned pattern: `admin_tool_call` (Tier-1 JSON router) picks ONE tool from the new frozen `ADMIN_AGENT_TOOLS` registry — `get_system_health`, `get_ai_usage(hours)`, `get_open_proposals` — the engine executes it through the existing deterministic handlers (`collectHealthFacts` now shared with `check health`, `handleAiStatsCommand`, `handleAdminRemediationsList`), and `admin_synthesis` words the reply from those facts only. "how many tokens did we use today?" → the real ai-stats digest → a plain-English answer. Static commands still win every matching turn with ZERO tokens (pinned); an inactive session never reaches the model; a non-superadmin phone never enters the flow. Every turn audits `ADMIN_AI_TOOL` + `ADMIN_AI_AGENT`; any AI failure degrades to the deterministic tool reply or the existing "did you mean" clarify (the old `ADMIN_CLARIFY` audit is superseded by `ADMIN_AI_AGENT`).
- **Deploy-live runbook (D1).** The push-time "building" alert stays free-form by design. The LIVE alert was already template-wired (`index.js` → `sendMetaWhatsAppMessage(..., 'deploy_live')`), but the live service had NO `META_TPL_*` variable set — verified by listing Cloud Run env names — so it degraded to free-form text and Meta silently dropped it outside the 24h window (the §87.3 failure). `docs/TEMPLATES.md` gains section 3.1: the exact `saka_deploy_live` body (Utility, en, no variables), the submission steps and `gcloud run services update saka-fleet --region=europe-west1 --update-env-vars="META_TPL_DEPLOY_LIVE=saka_deploy_live"`, plus rollback.
- **Legacy profile backfill (D3).** Live `information_schema` + NULL-count audit (3 profiles): `weekly_report_opt_in`, `monthly_report_opt_in`, `launch_opt_in`, `country_source`, `country`, `country_code`, `unit_system`, `preferred_language` are clean; the pre-v1.63 rows still had NULL `timezone`, `currency` and `locale`. New idempotent `npm run reconcile:profiles` (`scripts/reconcile_profiles.js`): COALESCE-only (a stored value always wins), defaults resolved from the SAME `REGIONS`/`COUNTRY_TIMEZONES` data the app uses (an explicit `country_code` — a user choice — wins over phone detection), `timezone_confirmed_at` and `trial_used_at` NEVER touched, every write audited `PROFILE_CONTEXT_RECONCILED` with a masked phone, `--dry-run` writes nothing. The before-snapshot is taken BEFORE the update so the audit can never be contaminated by a mutating client.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/{aiClient,superadmin}.js`, `scripts/{reconcile_profiles (new),health_audit}.js`, `docs/TEMPLATES.md`, `package.json` (1.78.0 + `reconcile:profiles`), `test_admin_agent.js` (new, 40), `test_profile_backfill.js` (new, 26), `test_phase6_purpose_tiers.js` (+7: 50 → 57), `README.md`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§206 + history + version), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_admin_agent (40), test_profile_backfill (26), test_phase6_purpose_tiers (57), test_superadmin, test_update_suggest, test_phase2_analytics, test_turn_latency, test_trial_engine, test_dashboard, test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class "a model asked to answer instead of fetching"* → the router prompt forbids answering and the synthesis prompt forbids invented numbers; the schema + the registry are pinned (a new tool without prompt/executor fails the suite). *Class "a backfill that overwrites good data"* → NULL-only patches pinned for stored/custom timezones, user country choices and the two never-touch columns. *Class "an audit that records post-write values"* → the before-snapshot moved ahead of the write and pinned by the mock run.

## [1.77.0] - 2026-11-08
- **v1.77.0 production-readiness audit (post-release amendment).** An exhaustive read-only audit of 8 domains (webhook/queue/intent, onboarding/passbook/vehicles, ledger/OCR, compliance/reminders/drivers, subscriptions/trials, AI router/Sentinel, care/admin, dashboard/infra) ran against commit `26c19bb`. **Business-logic override:** trial activation is now triggered ONLY by `vehicle_registered` — `expense_logged` and `report_generated` were removed from the registry and from their call sites (`quizEngine.logExpense`, the `commands.js` report lane); `recordActivationEvent()` rejects them as `unknown activation event`. Evidence: `.clinerules`/architecture consulted first; open proposals reconciled (`PROP-2026-11-08-01`, receipt FK); webhook HMAC timing-safe with `META_APP_SECRET` present in Cloud Run; `trg_prevent_vin_mutation` present and enabled; `receipts` bucket private / `fleet-reports` public; 25 expense types → 23 categories with zero unmapped; 30/14/7/24/overdue ladder one-shot; 13/13 calibration expectations hold. **Finding:** 169 orphaned `chat_history` rows from the pre-wipe era (all 2026-11-03→06); no `expense_logs`/`reminders`/vehicle orphans. Full report: `docs/AUDIT_v1.77.0_production_readiness.md`.
- **Frames touched (this amendment).** `src/services/trialEngine.js` (registry + header), `src/services/quizEngine.js` (expense activation removed), `src/routes/whatsapp/commands.js` (report activation removed + import), `test_trial_engine.js` (1-event assertions), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` §39.4/§39.9, `docs/AUDIT_v1.77.0_production_readiness.md` (new), this file.
- **Suites run (this amendment).** test_trial_engine (116), test_launch_award, test_trial_capacity (48), test_subscription_billing (104), test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green.


- **Phase 16 — the monthly PDF summary, and a leaner WhatsApp report lane.** Two goals in one release: an opt-in **Monthly PDF summary** emailed on the 1st of every month at 08:00 EAT (the branded Financial & Cost PDF for the month just ended), and the retirement of the legacy async report flow ("building your report…" → background job → follow-up link → on-demand email).
- **Monthly sweep (`src/workers/monthlyReports.js`).** `isMonthlyReportWindow()` = Nairobi day-of-month 1, 08:00–08:05; claim-first day marker `monthlyreports.last_sent_date` (`CONFIG_KEY_MONTHLY_REPORTS_LAST_SENT`, owner `monthlyReports`); batch 200; per-owner ACTIVE-vehicle check; `previousMonthBounds()` derives the previous Nairobi calendar month; `buildCustomReport` runs with the Phase 14D `bounds` override and the six financial metrics; `renderReportPdfBuffer` produces the Buffer; `sendMonthlyReportEmail` emails it. **No WhatsApp message, no quota, no trial activation** (weekly precedent, rule 26). An un-migrated `monthly_report_opt_in` returns `column-missing` and RELEASES the marker (rule 50 — a missing column never burns a month).
- **New email (`sendMonthlyReportEmail`, `mailer.js`).** Resend attachment (native Buffer), From `reports@reports.sakafleet.com`, filename `saka-monthly-<Month_Year>.pdf`, warm plain copy, never throws (`false` = honest failure counted by the sweep).
- **D1 — the async report flow is gone.** `followUpAckText`, `queueReportFollowUp` (hooks H10), `runFleetReport`, `resolveReportPdfLinkDelayMs`, the `FLEET_REPORT` kind and its recovery copy are deleted. The follow-up LANE survives for `driver_unassigned` only — the table, the 30s worker and `migrate:followups` are untouched, and a stale pre-deploy `fleet_report` row is failed honestly (pinned).
- **D2 — dashboard-first reports with an inline fast PDF.** `handleReportRequestTurn` answers instantly with the dashboard Reports link. Under `REPORT_INLINE_BUILD_MS` (default 4000, clamped 0–8000; 0 = link only) it also builds, renders, uploads and shortens the PDF and appends `📄 *PDF report:*` — and **only then** records quota usage (the trial activation trigger itself is vehicle-registration-only since the v1.77.0 audit override). A budget miss answers the dashboard link and records nothing. Nothing is ever emailed on demand.
- **D3 — vehicle summary keeps its email.** `deliverReportPdf` behaviour is unchanged (it now calls the extracted `renderReportPdfBuffer`); the explicit "vehicle summary pdf" ask still emails.
- **D5/D6 — the opt-in, three ways.** `profiles.monthly_report_opt_in boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false` (`npm run migrate:monthly-reports`, idempotent + staging schema CREATE/ALTER); a dashboard Settings toggle with its own `action=save_monthly` marker (each toggle writes ONLY its own column, and a rule-50 degrade is honest); and the WhatsApp lane `monthly report on|off` + bare `monthly report` state read.

## [1.76.0] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 15 — the dashboard grows driver and superadmin lanes.** `dashboard_sessions.session_role` (`owner` | `driver` | `admin`, additive with an `owner` default) decides which lane a session may open. `dashboard` in WhatsApp now resolves the caller's lane (`admin > owner > driver`, the same owner-first order as `resolveReportAccess`) and the reply names it; `dashboard admin` is a new exact phrase for the superadmin only — anyone else gets the verbatim not-available line without the service ever being called.
- **D1 — a driver needs a live assignment, at mint AND at verify.** `requestDashboardAccess({ role: 'driver' })` skips the plan gate (a driver is an employee, not a payer) and requires a live `vehicles.assigned_driver_id` row; a withdrawn assignment answers *"You need to be assigned to a vehicle to access the driver dashboard. Ask your fleet owner to assign you."* The identical re-check runs at verify, so an already-minted token dies with the assignment.
- **D2 — admin isolation is a 404.** `/d/admin*` answers **404** to any non-admin session and the body never mentions owners; the owner lane answers **403** to driver/admin sessions and the driver lane answers **403** to everyone else. Refused attempts write no audit row; anonymous requests are 401 before any role check.
- **D3 — the driver sees their own numbers.** `buildDriverComplianceCard` gained an additive `mask = true` parameter; only the driver's own home and compliance pages pass `mask: false`. The WhatsApp card, the reports and the owner's driver page keep exactly the behaviour they had.
- **Driver lane.** `/d/driver` (assigned vehicle + own compliance summary + last 7 days), `/d/driver/daybook` (the full `buildDriverDaybook` engine, 14 days) and `/d/driver/compliance` (own documents, unmasked). Every read is scoped by `driverId = session.ownerId`, never by a vehicle id, so another driver's rows are structurally unreachable — pinned.
- **Superadmin lane.** `/d/admin` lists every owner with ≥1 live vehicle (vehicle + active counts, masked phones, name/visible-digit search, deterministic sort) and `/d/admin/owners/:id` reuses `buildOverviewCard` + `buildVehiclesIndex` + `buildComplianceBoard` against the target owner — read-only. **Every** view, including a not-found one, writes `ADMIN_DASHBOARD_VIEW` to `admin_audit_logs` with `page`, `ownerViewed` and `found`.
- **One identity source (rule 55).** `isSuperadminPhone` / `isSuperadminEmail` moved to a dependency-free `src/services/superadminIdentity.js`; `superadmin.js` re-exports both so every existing importer is untouched, and the dashboard verifies the same identity without importing the Sentinel worker graph.
- **Schema (additive, rule 56).** `DASHBOARD_SQL` gains `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS session_role text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'owner'` (so the boot ensure upgrades a pre-1.76.0 table), `scripts/provision_staging_schema.js` carries it in the CREATE + ALTER, and `npm run migrate:dashboard-role` (`scripts/run_dashboard_role_migration.js`) applies it idempotently. Old sessions default to the owner lane; `db_reset_scope` pins all three carriers.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/dashboard.js` (`DASHBOARD_SQL`, `DASHBOARD_ROLES`, `session_role` in mint/verify/session, `resolveDashboardRole`, `hasLiveDriverAssignment`, §10 driver + admin builders, five renderers, navs), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (`requireOwnerSession` → `requireRoleSession(role)`, 15 owner call sites, 5 new routes, `logAdminAudit`), `src/services/driverReports.js` (`mask` parameter), `src/services/readCommands.js` (admin phrase + role resolution + `no_assignment`), `src/services/superadminIdentity.js` (new), `src/services/superadmin.js` (re-export), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `scripts/provision_staging_schema.js`, `scripts/run_dashboard_role_migration.js` (new), `package.json` (1.76.0 + `migrate:dashboard-role`), `test_dashboard.js` (+85: 504 → 589; five new flag-off URLs), `test_read_registry.js` (+11: 201 → 212), `test_db_reset_scope.js` (+4: 90 → 94), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamp), `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§204 + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** Baseline: test_dashboard (504), test_read_registry (201), test_db_reset_scope (90), test_agent_training (200) — green. After: **test_dashboard (589)**, **test_read_registry (212)**, **test_db_reset_scope (94)**, test_agent_training (200), test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green. Zero pre-existing assertions edited.

## [1.75.3] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 14D — reports builder + settings + data export. The Owner Dashboard is complete.** `/d/reports` builds a bespoke PDF on the spot: pick This month / Last 30 days / This year / a custom range, tick the vehicles (or leave all unticked for the whole fleet) and up to 8 metrics grouped by category — the PDF downloads immediately. `/d/settings` gives one real preference (the Monday weekly-report toggle, the SAME column WhatsApp writes), a **Download all my data** CSV and a pointer to *subscription* in chat for plan changes. Nav gains **Reports** and **Settings**.
- **D1 — date ranges are additive.** `dateRangeBounds()` gained `last_30_days` and `buildCustomReport()` gained an optional `bounds` window; the WhatsApp custom-report lane passes neither, so its behaviour is byte-identical (`test_report_metrics` 25, `test_report_builders` 83, `test_fleet_reports` 42 all green with zero edits).
- **The builder IS the WhatsApp engine.** `buildDashboardCustomReport()` wraps `buildCustomReport()` with the owner lane — same metrics, charts, ledger and PDF layout, so the dashboard can never drift from the chat report. `parseReportSpec()` validates one spec (range, ≤366-day custom window, owner-only vehicle ids, ≤8 metrics) and feeds both the form and the PDF; an unknown metric or another owner's vehicle id is a plain 400. **Dashboard reports never touch `usage_counters`** — the builder has no quota path at all.
- **D3 — "Download all data" is ONE CSV.** Six sections (expenses, earnings, reminders, vehicle documents, driver documents, receipt metadata — never image URLs) with `record_type` as the first column, a `# section` comment row before each block (A2), a `[CAP]` row when a table hits the 2,000-row limit (A1) and an `[ERROR]` row when a table cannot be read — never a silent empty section. Owner-fleet scoped by construction.
- **D2 — CSRF is tokenless by design.** The session cookie is HttpOnly + `SameSite=Strict` and `/d` is never CORS-listed; on top of that, `POST /d/settings` refuses any write whose `Origin` is another host with a **403** (checked before the session gate), so a forged cross-site form fails even on an older browser. No new dependency. The form also carries an explicit `action=save_weekly` marker: a stray POST can never silently unsubscribe an owner, and a failed save says "nothing changed", never "Saved".
- **Settings are honest.** Only `weekly_report_opt_in` is writable here; email, units and language stay in WhatsApp by design. The page shows the masked email/phone, language, units, country, plan (from the tier registry) and when this browser's session ends.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/reportMetrics.js` (two additive changes), `src/services/dashboard.js` (+§9: spec, builder, settings, export, 2 renderers, nav, form CSS, `unit_system` in the profile select), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (+5 routes, Origin guard), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_dashboard.js` (+110: 394 → 504), `test_agent_training.js` (+2: 198 → 200), `package.json` (1.75.3), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamp), `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§203 + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** Baseline: test_dashboard (394), test_fleet_reports (42), test_report_builders (83), test_agent_training (198), `npm run health`. After: test_dashboard (504), test_agent_training (200), test_fleet_reports (42 — unchanged), test_report_metrics (25 — unchanged), test_report_builders (83), test_record_answers (80), test_entry_ledger, test_ocr_flow (129), test_read_registry, test_no_canned_replies (14 — ratchet still 4), test_kb_docs, test_landing_static, test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green. Zero pre-existing assertions edited.
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/45, 3/4, 7, 10/11, 12/33, 13/60, 21, 23/24, 31, 42, 50, 51/52, 54, 55, 56, 58. No rule was edited or added. **Phase 14 (Owner Dashboard) is complete; Phase 15 (driver + superadmin views) is next.**

## [1.75.2] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 14C — the compliance surface.** `GET /d/compliance` shows the owner's fleet the way an owner actually checks it: every vehicle and every assigned driver with what Saka tracks, the soonest document and a colour chip (red expired, orange due, green valid, grey no date), filterable by **All / Due soon (30 days) / Expired** with expired items pinned to the top. `GET /d/drivers/:id` opens one driver: name, phone, the assigned vehicle(s) and their whole document ledger (licence, PSV, PDP, medical…) with numbers shown **in full for the owner**. `GET /d/compliance.pdf?view=…` exports exactly the view on screen. Nav gains **Compliance** between Records and Log out.
- **One filter engine, one chip vocabulary.** Both the page and the PDF are narrowed by a single pure `filterComplianceReport`, which re-derives the totals AND the category matrices with the report's own bucket math — so a filtered PDF can never print a KPI that contradicts its table (pinned: the filtered PDF's KPIs equal the filtered totals). `expiryStatus` and `summarizeCategory` are now exported from `fleetReports.js` and reused rather than re-implemented; chips come from one `complianceChipHtml`.
- **Built on the existing compliance engine.** The board reads through `buildComplianceReport` (the same builder behind the WhatsApp/PDF compliance report), and the PDF reuses `complianceReportToPdf` + the Apex engine, streamed as a Buffer — no Storage upload, no email.
- **Ownership is structural.** `/d/drivers/:id` only renders a driver who is the `assigned_driver_id` of one of the owner's live vehicles; anyone else answers **404** (never 403) with **no name, phone or document number in the body** — pinned. Both new read paths are session-gated and flag-gated (generic 404 while `DASHBOARD_ENABLED=off`).
- **Decisions locked.** D1: numbers unmasked to the owner (the driver's own card keeps the mask). D2: coverage lives in `test_dashboard.js` (one suite). D3: nav order Records → Compliance → Log out. The only CSS addition is `.chip.bad` (red `#F85149`).
- **Two-sources honesty.** A driver document can be *on file* (a `driver_compliances` row) yet not *tracked* (no reminder ladder — e.g. saved without a number). The driver page and the board's driver rollup report the ledger truth; the vehicle section reports the unified expiry collector's truth; neither invents a merged total, and the retired `profiles.driver_license_expiry` mirror is not read.
- **Three real defects caught before shipping (rule 52).** (1) the owner-vehicle scope query did not select `assigned_driver_id` — the driver section would have been silently empty; (2) the report's driver section carries no `driver_id`, so a report-only board would have merged every driver's documents; (3) a naive filter would have left the category matrix stale, so a filtered PDF would contradict itself. All fixed and pinned.
- **Skill sync (rule 13/60).** `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK` + the core prompt now state the compliance board and its read-only driver pages (and no longer claim the compliance page is unshipped); `test_agent_training` +2 (196 → 198); `docs/` regenerated.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/dashboard.js` (+§8: filters, board, driver page, filtered PDF, chips, 2 renderers, nav, `.chip.bad`, `loadOwnerVehicles` select), `src/services/fleetReports.js` (two helpers exported), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (+3 routes), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_dashboard.js` (+95: 299 → 394), `test_agent_training.js` (+2), `package.json` (1.75.2), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamp), `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§202 + version/history), this file.

## [1.75.1] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 14B — the records surface.** `GET /d/records` is a searchable, filterable, paginated table of the owner's expenses AND earnings: 20 rows a page, newest first, with a receipt link on a row only when exactly one receipt matches. `GET /d/records.csv` and `GET /d/records.pdf` export the exact same filtered set (CSV: BOM + RFC-4180 quoting + ISO dates; PDF: the Apex ledger, streamed straight from memory — no Storage upload, no email), and `GET /d/receipt/:id` opens the stored image through a 5-minute signed link. Nav gains **Records** on every signed-in page.
- **FIX-1 — the scope that matters.** The WhatsApp records lane (`recordQueryLedger`) filters `driver_id = owner`, which is right for a per-caller chat but would show a fleet owner only the rows they personally typed. The dashboard uses a NEW `queryOwnerRecords()` scoped `vehicle_id IN (the owner's live vehicles)`, so owner-logged AND driver-logged rows both appear; `recordQuery.js` is untouched. Proof: a driver-logged row (`driver_id='d-1'`) is asserted present in the owner's result set.
- **The filter set (verbatim).** `?from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD&type=Fuel,Repairs&vehicle=<id>&label=karen&keyword=shell&page=N` — Nairobi calendar days (default: last 30, cap 366), expense types (≤12), one owner vehicle, ≤40-char label/keyword (wildcard chars stripped), page cap 250. One parser (`parseRecordsFilters`) serves the table and both exports, so an export can never bypass the table's filter. Export links carry the filter and never the page.
- **D2 — receipt links are unique-match only.** Receipts carry no FK to expenses, so a link appears only when exactly one receipt shares the vehicle, the Nairobi day and the amount; two or more show “Multiple match”, none shows “—”. Never a guess. The durable FK is minted as a tracked follow-up proposal (schema change, not part of 14B).
- **D1 — signed URLs, 300 s, per request, scope-checked first.** A miss answers 404 (never 403, so existence is never confirmed) and no signed URL is ever minted for a foreign row. The object path comes from `media_id`, so signing keeps working once the bucket is private. **Operator prerequisite (live-verified 2026-11-08): the `receipts` Storage bucket does not exist yet — create it AS PRIVATE before the production flip** (`fleet-reports` is `public:true`). Until then no receipt image is stored and the receipt link answers an honest 404; a signed URL on a public bucket would add no protection anyway.
- **D3 — vendor is not a records filter** (it lives only on receipts; deferred to a receipts view in Phase 15+). Label + keyword cover the find-that-entry need.
- **D4 — the test double grew up.** `scripts/mockSupabase.js` now supports `.or()` (comma list = disjunction), `.range()` (inclusive, like PostgREST) and `.ilike()` — additive; every pre-existing suite stayed green before the first new assertion.
- **Three real defects caught by the new tests before shipping (rule 52).** (1) `Number(null) === 0` silently pinned every page to offset 0; (2) `.or('a,b')` was first built as one AND-group instead of a disjunction; (3) the earnings date filter used `created_at`, dropping backdated rows whose business date is `logged_at`. All fixed and pinned.
- **Honest failures.** A failed query can never look like an empty export: the table answers 503, both exports answer 500, and the route is logged with no row, note, label or amount (`error_logs`), pinned by test. A capped export (2,000 rows) says “Showing the newest N records — narrow the dates”.
- **Skill sync (rule 13/60).** `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK` + the core prompt now state the records table, the CSV/PDF exports and the unique-match receipt rule; `test_agent_training` +2 (194 → 196). `docs/` regenerated.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/dashboard.js` (+§7: filters, `queryOwnerRecords`, receipt matching, signed URLs, CSV, PDF, 2 renderers, nav, CSS), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (+4 routes), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `scripts/mockSupabase.js`, `test_dashboard.js` (+112: 187 → 299), `test_agent_training.js` (+2), `package.json` (1.75.1), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamp), `docs/`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§201 + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** Baseline: test_dashboard (187), test_record_answers (80), test_entry_ledger, test_fleet_reports (42), test_report_builders (83), test_ocr_flow (129), `npm run health`. After: test_dashboard (299), test_agent_training (196), test_record_answers (80 — unchanged), test_entry_ledger, test_fleet_reports (42), test_report_builders (83), test_ocr_flow (129), test_read_registry, test_no_canned_replies (14 — ratchet still 4), test_kb_docs, test_landing_static, test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green. ## [1.75.0] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 14A — the vehicle pages.** `GET /d/vehicles` lists every vehicle with plate + make/model, an Active/Paused/Inactive chip, the odometer in the owner's unit, the driver (or "No driver assigned"), this month's spend, an `N overdue` chip and the soonest document's due chip, sorted active-first. `GET /d/vehicles/:id` opens one vehicle: plate + status, make/model • driver, three KPI tiles (odometer / spend this month / earnings this month), every document with its due date and chip (expired first, then soonest) and the latest eight merged ledger rows with amount + date. Every signed-in page now carries `Overview · Vehicles · Log out`, and the overview adds one line: "See your vehicles →".
- **D1 — one data path.** Two new exports in `fleetReports.js` (`buildVehicleDirectory`, `buildVehicleDetail`) reuse the same `loadFleetData` + `collectExpiryItems` as the four PDF/WhatsApp reports, and the detail rows are mapped through `reportLedger.expenseToLedgerRow` / `earningToLedgerRow`, so a dashboard row and a PDF row can never disagree. `dashboard.js` only adds the injectable page wrappers and the renderers — zero new dependencies, inline HTML from the shared shell.
- **D2 — ownership is structural.** The detail builder looks the vehicle up inside the OWNER-scoped dataset, so another owner's id simply is not there (`not_found` → a warm 404 page), never a filter someone can forget and never a 403 that confirms existence.
- **D4 — the 13C country regression, fixed.** Phase 13C called `buildOverviewCard` without `countryInfo`, so every owner's live card defaulted to USD/en-US (a Kenyan fleet read "USD" while every WhatsApp report read KES). The routes now resolve the country from the profile's phone number (`detectCountryFromPhone`, rule 7) and pass it to all three builders; pinned by a KE route test (KES on the list and the overview) and an imperial test (miles).
- **D5 — routes + one session gate.** Both pages are flag-gated first (generic 404 while `DASHBOARD_ENABLED=off`) and registered before `/d/:token`; the cookie → session → profile sequence moved into one `requireOwnerSession()` helper that GET `/d` shares. Rule 50: a failed profile degrades to the slim columns, a failed page renders ONE honest 503 explanation, and no `undefined` can leak.
- **No VIN anywhere on the dashboard** (rule 8), no charts, search, exports or settings — those stay in later phases. `DASHBOARD_ENABLED` remains **OFF** in `.env` and the template; no migration and no schema change ships with this release.
- **Skill sync (rule 13/60).** `SAKA_WEB_DASHBOARD_PLAYBOOK` + the core prompt now state the shipped scope (overview + vehicle list + one page per vehicle) and name the unshipped pages as off-limits; `test_agent_training` +3 assertions (191 → 194).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/fleetReports.js` (+`monthTotalsFor`, `driverNameFor`, `vehicleSummary`, `buildVehicleDirectory`, `buildVehicleDetail`), `src/services/dashboard.js` (+2 builders, +2 renderers, nav, chips, CSS), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (`countryForProfile`, `requireOwnerSession`, +2 routes), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `test_dashboard.js` (+50), `test_agent_training.js` (+3), `package.json` (1.75.0), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§200 + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_dashboard (187), test_fleet_reports (42), test_report_builders (83), test_read_registry (201), test_agent_training (194), test_no_canned_replies (14 — ratchet still 4), test_kb_docs (36), test_landing_static, test_doc_sync (30), `npm run health` — green. Zero behavioural assertions edited.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *A page builder called without country context silently defaults to GLOBAL* → every sibling audited and pinned (D4). *A route can shadow another by registration order* → the vehicle routes sit before `/d/:token`, and the flag-OFF sweep now covers both new URLs.

## [1.74.2] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 13C — the dashboard pages.** `GET /d/<token>` renders the OTP entry page (masked destination, hidden token, 6-digit input, never-share warning); `POST /d/verify` issues the 30-day `saka_dash` cookie and 303s to `GET /d`, which now renders the **overview card**: Spend / Earnings / Net / Active vehicles KPIs, up to six upcoming expiries (soonest first, `due` chips inside 7 days) and up to five recent activities — the first real dashboard page, proving trigger → email → code → session → render end to end. `GET /d/logout` deletes the session row and clears the cookie; a missing/expired cookie gets an honest 401 page with the WhatsApp instruction. Every page is inline HTML from the shared `renderPageShell()` (D1) — no static server, no new dependency.
- **D3 — `upcomingExpiryItems(supabase, ownerId, limit = 6)`** exported from `fleetReports.js`: the unified expiry collector filtered to `daysLeft >= 0`, sorted soonest-first, capped, returning exactly `{ plate, category, dueDate, daysLeft, status }`. `buildOverviewCard()` composes it with `buildFleetReport({ type: 'fleet' })` — money in the owner currency (no hard-coded KES), honest zeros for an empty fleet, never invented figures.

## [1.74.1] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 13B — the dashboard WhatsApp lane.** Replying *dashboard* (or *my dashboard* / *open my dashboard* / *web dashboard*) now triggers the dashboard service: one email carries a 15-minute private link AND a 10-minute 6-digit code, and the chat replies with the LINK only. **The code NEVER appears in WhatsApp and is never accepted in chat** — the two-channel split is the whole point (D2). The lane sits in the read registry after `my_ticket`, parks any quiz, stays out of PIN/OTP phases, and is exact-phrase only (`dashboard ideas` / `i can't see the dashboard` still reach the AI).

## [1.74.0] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 13A — dashboard foundation: magic link + email OTP + a hashed HttpOnly session, all behind `DASHBOARD_ENABLED` (OFF).** The dashboard program (Phases 13–16) makes the web dashboard the PRIMARY data surface and keeps the PDF as an export; Phase 13 ships only the auth foundation and one page (13C). New `src/services/dashboard.js` + `src/routes/dashboard.js` + `dashboard_sessions` (`npm run migrate:dashboard`, idempotent, boot-ensured, in the slate-wipe scope and the staging DDL). WhatsApp "dashboard" → one email carrying a 15-minute magic link AND a 10-minute 6-digit code (the code NEVER rides WhatsApp); `GET /d/<token>` → OTP page; `POST /d/verify` → 30-day `saka_dash` cookie (`HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/d; Secure`); `GET /d` → the session-gated page; `GET /d/logout` clears it.
- **Security primitives (A3).** The link token, the OTP and the session token are stored as sha256 hashes only — raw values live exclusively in the email and the cookie; OTP comparison is `crypto.timingSafeEqual` with a length check first; codes come from `crypto.randomInt`; links are single-use (verify burns the OTP hash and mints exactly one session); 5 wrong codes lock the link (audited `DASHBOARD_OTP_LOCKED`); a 60-second resend cooldown prevents mail spam; a failed Resend send deletes the row so no orphaned live token exists.
- **A1 — invisible while off.** With `DASHBOARD_ENABLED=off` every `/d` route answers Fastify's own 404 payload (indistinguishable from an unregistered route), and no database write happens. Eligibility is `profiles.email_verified` AND (`entitlements.isTrial` OR `isPaid`) — the welcome trial counts, a free/lapsed account never does.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/dashboard.js` (new), `src/routes/dashboard.js` (new), `scripts/run_dashboard_migration.js` (new), `test_dashboard.js` (new, stateless), `index.js` (import + register + boot ensure + 15-min prune), `src/services/mailer.js` (`sendDashboardMagicLinkEmail`), `src/services/envAudit.js` (SERVER + `DASHBOARD_ENABLED`), `.env.example` + `.env`, `scripts/provision_staging_schema.js`, `scripts/reset-db.js`, `test_db_reset_scope.js`, `scripts/health_audit.js`, `package.json` (1.74.0 + `migrate:dashboard`), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§199A + version/history), this file.

## [1.73.4] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 12B — template button replies: defensive plumbing + interactive diagnostics.** Meta may attach quick-reply buttons to the proactive templates, so a tap is now handled before the WABA button registration is even confirmed. New pure leaf `src/routes/whatsapp/interactive.js` (`parseInteractiveReply`) turns `interactive.type='button_reply'` (`{id,title}`), `list_reply` (`{id,title,description}`) and legacy `button {text,payload}` into the typed text the user would otherwise have sent — resolved BEFORE the media envelope, so voice notes, images, stickers and reactions are never touched. The tap then rides the live grammar: no new intents, no prompt changes.
- **D1 registry (locked).** `BUTTON_PAYLOAD_PHRASES` maps `remind_me → reminders` (the due LIST the template body promises — never `remind me`, which starts the set_reminder quiz), `reminders → reminders` (idempotent), `subscription`, `keep_all → keep all`, `help`. The registered phrase wins over the button title; an unregistered payload falls back to the title. `docs/TEMPLATES.md` §5 is the approval contract the code mirrors.
- **Quick-edit ids stay safe.** Existing v1.51.10 button ids resolve to their live operators (`my entries`, `edit vehicle`, `you read it wrong`) with `intent='quick_edit'`; locked ids (`*:vin`, `entry_date`) fall through to the title — the VIN can never be produced or resolved (rule 8).
- **Unrecognised shapes are diagnosed, never guessed (A1).** If an `interactive`/`button` object exists but the parse returns null (for example `product` or `nfm_reply`), ONE `logSystemError` row goes to `error_logs` under `/webhook/whatsapp/interactive`; the stable message lets the 24-h `ERROR_ROLLUP_HOURS` rollup fold repeats into one row per shape/phone incident — never per turn — and the stack captures ONLY `{ interactive, button }` (≤4000 chars): no message body, no media ids, no phone, no timestamps.
- **Frames touched.** `src/routes/whatsapp/interactive.js` (new), `src/routes/whatsapp/messages.js` (import + parse/diagnostic block + `messageText` injection seam), `src/routes/whatsapp/index.js` (barrel +1 export), `test_interactive_reply.js` (new, 48 assertions), `test_no_canned_replies.js` (11th scan file + 2 leaf-boundary pins), `test_ocr_flow.js` (+2 source pins), `scripts/health_audit.js` (stateless suite registered), `docs/TEMPLATES.md` (§5 registry), `package.json` (1.73.4), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `docs/` (generated), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§198B + version/history), this file.

## [1.73.3] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 12C — quiet evaluation notifications, exhaustive digest.** The eval loop no longer interrupts the superadmin with an immediate both-channel alert when the judge cannot score a case. Every judge error now writes one bounded `CONV_EVAL_JUDGE_ERROR` audit row (case id + reason, max 10 per tick) and lands in the **6-hourly digest** as its own "Judge errors" section — kept separate from the heal-refusal section so judge noise can never pollute the "needs a code fix" candidates. Heals, proposals and heal failures already rode the digest; the digest is now exhaustive.
- **Nothing is lost when the email cannot send.** The v1.61.13 retry floor is unchanged (only a SENT email consumes the 6-hour window; a failure retries after 15 minutes), and a delayed digest now sends ONE free-form WhatsApp line — "⏳ Digest email delayed — retrying in 15 min. Judge errors and case details will land in the next successful digest." — instead of staying silent.
- **Retired dead config.** `lastErrorAlertAt`, `resetConvEvalAlertThrottle()` and `CONV_EVAL_ALERT_COOLDOWN_HOURS` had exactly one consumer (the removed alert); the knob is gone from `getConvEvalConfig()`, the env registry and `.env.example` together (parity pinned). The daily eval call-budget heads-up is deliberately kept (spend telemetry, not an error).

## [1.73.2] - 2026-11-08

- **Phase 12A — driver compliance reminders are now complete AND quiet.** Every driver document with an expiry now gets its own reminder — driving licence, PSV badge, PDP, CPC, operator card, medical certificate (before, only the licence was guaranteed). Each document keeps its own ladder: a PSV badge and a PDP both count as "permit" internally but are tracked separately, so neither can hide the other's expiry.
- **The duplicate-alert bug (found by the deep audit).** The compliance sync runs every 30 minutes and re-saved every linked reminder with its notification flags wiped — so the same tier (30-day, 14-day, 7-day, 24-hour, overdue) re-fired on every sweep for driver documents, and a freshly refreshed licence could hold TWO live ladders against the same date. Now a re-sync of an UNCHANGED date keeps the already-sent tiers, only a real date change resets the ladder, and the canonical `driver_compliances` licence row retires the old profile-mirror duplicate. **Behaviour change:** drivers stop receiving duplicate reminders every 30 minutes — the first sweep after deploy is quieter, and a renewed document alerts once per tier, exactly as designed.
- **Edge cases handled.** Lifetime documents never get a reminder; a row without a number is skipped; and a document that is already expired is never promoted into a NEW reminder (an EXISTING active reminder still fires its single overdue alert, so a genuinely missed window is never lost). Side-by-side assertions pin all of it (+14 in test_expiry_reminders, 48 → 62).
- **Same rules when a driver submits a document in chat.** `submitDriverCompliance` now retires the legacy profile-mirror reminder the moment a licence is refreshed (rule 51 sibling).
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/maintenanceReminders.js` (createExpiryReminder date-change guard + `expiryRowWithoutTierFlags`; merged `syncAllDriverComplianceReminders`; new `retireLegacyDriverLicenceReminder`; runReminderSweep counters; exports), `src/services/driverAssignments.js` (submission sibling), `test_expiry_reminders.js` (renamed import/call + 14 new assertions), `package.json`, `docs/` (generated), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§28.4/§28.5 + §198A + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_expiry_reminders (62), test_maintenance_reminders (50), test_driver_assignment_gate (54), test_user_context_cache (136), `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defect classes *an idempotent re-upsert that mutates notification state* and *two write paths owning the same document* -> siblings audited: every vehicle-linked upsert shares the same `createExpiryReminder` guard (fixed globally), `submitDriverCompliance` fixed, `createThresholdReminder` deliberately left (insert-only, never resets flags). Rule 13: no intent/flow/copy change — `flowKnowledge.js` / `sakaAi.js` untouched.

## [1.73.1] - 2026-11-07

- **Phase 11 (final cleanup) — the legacy onboarding lane gets its own module.** `src/routes/whatsapp/onboarding.js` (new, 229 lines) now owns the two remaining legacy onboarding blocks from messages.js: **branch A** (the two-stage progressive onboarding fallback, section 191, 178 source lines) as `handleLegacyOnboardingTurn(...)` and **branch B's registration starter** (51 source lines) as `startLegacyRegistrationFlow(...)`. Both are pure moves: every moved statement is byte-identical after the uniform re-indent (fingerprint proof: 137/137 exact in branch A, 21/21 exact in the starter; the ONLY deltas are the approved D2 seed dedupe), and the processor keeps its positional precedence — the branch-A gate expression and the `if/else-if` chain to branch B are untouched at the call site. messages.js: **2,236 -> 2,021 content lines** (-215); the processor still owns the session state machine, the store and every send.
- **The `onboardingStore` boundary — passed as an argument, no cycle.** The mutable store stays in messages.js (snapshot hydration at L176/L290, the early state gate, the email-gate delete and the `finalizeQuizOutcome` argument all depend on it; suites and `src/services/onboarding.js` read it through the barrel). The new leaf receives it as a parameter, exactly like quiz.js does — the module imports `src/services/*` + `./helpers.js` only and never `./messages.js` (pinned).
- **D2 seed dedupe (`buildOnboardingSeed`).** The three near-identical legacy store seeds (reset, default, starter) collapse into one private factory — field-for-field identical (`stage`, `step` derived from the plate, the full `core`/`optional` shapes, `role: 'Owner'`), behaviour-neutral.
- **Two pre-existing latent ReferenceErrors repaired (found by the rule-52 audit).** The legacy branch called `checkIsConversationalFeedback(...)` and `formatUsdInLocalCurrency(...)` but messages.js imported NEITHER — both paths would have thrown (masked by the branch being a near-dead fallback behind the modern quiz and by the processor's catch). The extracted module declares both imports, messages.js gains the paystack import for its remaining branch-B capacity block, and `test_no_canned_replies` pins the repair.
- **Rule 51 sweep — the ratchet follows the code.** `test_no_canned_replies` now scans 10 route files (`onboarding.js` added) plus two new boundary assertions (never sends, never imports messages.js, both helper imports present). The canned-literal count is unchanged at **4** (the moved literals are template strings with interpolation, already outside the ratchet's plain-literal patterns) — 12 assertions green.


- **The `trial-end-downsize-choice` eval flake — fixed with facts, not bar manipulation.** Diagnosis: the case's per-criterion bar is **0.7** (there was never a 0.85 bar; the "0.83" in earlier release notes is the CLI-printed AGGREGATE with `boundary_safety` listed as the failing criterion — it dipped below 0.7). Root cause: the stored reply is real engine output (pending.js `keep <plate>` lane) that asserts engine-owned facts (the fitted plan `Solo Pro`, the pause-never-delete contract) which appeared in NEITHER the transcript NOR `engine_facts`, so the judge's "absent from both = invented" law made `boundary_safety` flap. Fix: `engine_facts` added to the case (mirroring the sibling `trial-end-fit-offer`) + one `test_conv_eval` assertion pinning them. The reply and the criteria were NOT touched. Judged result: **0.93 PASS** (was 0.83 FAIL / 0.88 PASS).
- **Frames touched.** `src/routes/whatsapp/onboarding.js` (new, 229), `src/routes/whatsapp/messages.js` (2,236 -> 2,021; branch A call + starter call + dead import swapped for the paystack import + leaf import), `src/routes/whatsapp/index.js` (+1 barrel export), `test_no_canned_replies.js` (10th scan file + 2 pins), `test_conv_eval.js` (+1 engine_facts assertion), `evals/cases/conversation_cases.json` (the case's engine_facts + note), `package.json`, `docs/` (regenerated), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§197 + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_first_message_flow (57), test_quiz_flow, test_pending_state (434), test_first_time_flow, test_greetings (110), test_web_onboarding, test_no_canned_replies (12 assertions — 4 canned literals, ratchet held), test_reliability (Test 4 fails identically on the untouched baseline: DEEPSEEK_API_KEY absent in the test shell — pre-existing, outside `npm run health`), test_deep_audit (28), test_conv_eval (218), `npm run health` — green; `npm run eval:conv -- --exclude-calibration`: signal **23/23 (100%)**, calibration **12/12 held**, gate PASS (1 judge provider hiccup demoted to the exit-2 warning).
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class — a split can strand a user-facing literal outside the ratchet* (§191 F1): fixed by adding `onboarding.js` to the scan list before the move was run; verified count unchanged (4). *Defect class — a branch can call helpers it never imported* (latent ReferenceError): both identifiers audited across messages.js — `checkIsConversationalFeedback` had zero other consumers (left out of messages.js), `formatUsdInLocalCurrency` is still called by the branch-B capacity block (import added to messages.js); the new module declares both; pinned. *Defect class — a judge flake pinned to missing engine facts, not a real deviation*: fixed with `engine_facts` (the v1.61.2 mechanism), never by lowering the safety bar or rewriting the engine-owned fixture; the sibling trial-end case audited for the same shape (already carries facts).
- **Rules shaped this work (rule 58).** 1/39/45 (consult-first), 3/4 (targeted reads, batching), 10/11 (targeted suites + cleanup), 12/33 (commit/push, version + history + CHANGELOG same commit), 13 (docs sync — no flowKnowledge/sakaAi change: pure extraction + corpus metadata), 31 (unchanged copy), 44/51/52 (audit-before-acting, recurrence autopsy, deep audit + verbatim proof), 50 (no schema touched), 54 (live checks: eval gate + health run for real), 58 (this report). No rule was edited or added.

## [1.73.0] - 2026-11-07

- **Pagination on `my entries` and `receipts` (Phase 10, P3-1).** Page 1 is unchanged (6 entries / 5 receipts); a new `pagination_more` lane serves the next page when the user replies `more` (or `next`, `next page`, `older`, `show me more`), driven by a 15-minute `session.paginationCursor` — session MEMORY, never the Phase 4 pending slot (decision D1: a cursor is not an interaction, and the pending machine's eviction + fresh-log rules must not apply). Page 2+ is **view-only** (D2): `edit N` / `delete N` / `delete receipt N` always target the newest list, so paged pages never invite them. The list is capped at 5 pages (30 entries / 25 receipts, D3), then an honest redirect to `search <keyword>` or a report. `listLedgerEntries` gained an `offset` param (merged two-table paging is deterministic), receipts gained `pageReceipts` (DB-side `range()` + vehicle `.in` filter + limit+1 `hasMore`). **A1:** a cursorless/expired `more` is NEVER claimed (the lane is last in the registry and gate-checked; pinned in the frozen corpus) — and `show me more` no longer falls into `records_query`'s degenerate ledger read (`inspectRecordQuestion` short-circuits every list-continuation phrase).

- **User-side ticket status (Phase 10, P3-3).** `my ticket` / `my tickets` / `ticket status` now read the caller's OWN last 3 support tickets (short reference, subject, status chip, priority, last update) — zero AI tokens, `parksQuiz`, gated during PIN/OTP phases. OWN DATA only: the query filters `support_tickets.whatsapp_number = callerPhone` (the exact value the intake writes; email-source tickets can never match). **A2:** a user with zero tickets always gets the same honest empty state — never the support-intake prompt, even on repeated calls. Reply/edit/close stay staff-console-only (rule 49). A pre-care-console table degrades to the slim columns in one retry (rule 50).

- **Weekly report opt-in + Monday sweep (Phase 10, P3-4).** `weekly report on` / `weekly report off` flips the new `profiles.weekly_report_opt_in` (migration `npm run migrate:weekly-report-optin`, idempotent; opt-in default false). A bare `weekly report` answers its state deterministically — the quota-hijack guard (D4): without it the phrase could be classified as a report request and burn the monthly quota. The toggle is a pre-read write lane beside the country command (the read registry keeps writes out). New `src/workers/weeklyReports.js` sends every opted-in owner with ≥1 ACTIVE vehicle a ONE-MESSAGE fleet-status text summary on Monday 08:00 EAT (`buildFleetReport({ type: 'fleet' })` text only — no PDF), deduped by the `weeklyreports.last_sent_date` system_config marker with **claim-first + retry-on-failure** (D5: at-least-once, never a silent miss). **Invariants pinned by tests:** zero `usage_counters` writes, zero trial-activation events, no PDF path (§39.5 R5). `SYSTEM_CONFIG_KEYS` 9 → 10.

- **Frames touched.** `src/services/{entryAnswers,entryLedger,receipts,readCommands,customerCare,systemConfigKeys,flowKnowledge}.js`, `src/workers/weeklyReports.js` (new), `src/routes/whatsapp/{commands,messages}.js`, `index.js`, `sakaAi.js`, `scripts/{run_weekly_report_optin_migration(new),provision_staging_schema}.js`, `package.json`, `test_{read_registry,entry_ledger,ocr_flow,customer_care,system_config_keys,agent_training,weekly_reports(new)}.js`, `docs/` (generated), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§196 + version/history), this file.

- **Suites run.** test_read_registry (138 → 181), test_entry_ledger (+4 offset assertions), test_ocr_flow (122 → 127), test_customer_care (18 → 28), test_system_config_keys (37 → 38), test_agent_training (180 → 187), test_weekly_reports (new, 46), test_v1583 (10), test_pending_state (434), test_subscription_billing (104), test_trial_engine (114), test_product_qa (152), test_driver_compliance_answers (64), test_labels_everywhere (22), test_first_message_flow (57), test_no_canned_replies (10), test_conversation_compliance (29), test_feature_notice (25), test_user_context_cache (136), test_doc_sync, `npm run health` — green; `npm run eval:conv -- --exclude-calibration` signal 23/23, calibration 13/13 (Phase 10 touches no AI routing).

- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class — a phrase claimed by an earlier deterministic lane:* `show me more` was answered by `records_query` as a degenerate ledger list; the fix lives in the ONE shared parser (`inspectRecordQuestion`) and the pagination lane gate, with the cursorless case pinned in the frozen corpus. Siblings audited: ledger/receipts/QA/odometer/driver-profile detectors — no match, left untouched and fixture-pinned. *Class — a new config key violating the registry law:* the underscores in `weekly_reports.last_sent_date` failed the `domain.namespaced` test; renamed `weeklyreports.last_sent_date` and the count moved 9 → 10. *Class — quota/activation creep into a new sender:* source pins prove the weekly worker imports and calls neither `subscriptions` nor `trialEngine`.

- **Design decisions (locked).** D1 session `paginationCursor` (not `session.pending`, not the pending registry); D2 view-only paged pages (edit/delete anchored to the newest list); D3 5-page cap with search/report redirect; D4 pre-read write lane + bare state-read anti-hijack; D5 claim-first marker with retry. Additions: A1 cursorless `more` never claimed (frozen fixture); A2 `my ticket` empty-state idempotency (never the intake prompt).

## [1.72.2] - 2026-11-07

- **"Trial status" now answers YOUR trial, not the pricing card (Phase 9, P3-6).** A new read-registry lane (`subscription_status`, ordered before `product_qa` so it shadows the marketing QA card) routes the exact phrases `trial`, `my trial` and `trial status` to the existing engine-owned summary (`formatSubscriptionSummary` → "🎁 Free trial — N day(s) left (ends …)") with zero AI tokens. The same exact-phrase alternative is mirrored in `classifyIntentWithRegex` (quiz fast path + provider-outage fallback), `maybeHandleSubscriptionTurn`'s `wantsView`, and the processor's subscription gate. The `$` anchor is the safety law: "my trial ended…", "extend my trial" and "trial extension" keep their own flows (the trial-end decision window and EXTEND_TRIAL never get hijacked). Ownerless sessions keep today's marketing card (never a null reply).

- **The eval gate can now catch earnings invention (Phase 9, P2-7).** New calibration case `earning-invented-amount-calibration` (`expected: "fail"`, tags `log_earnings, earnings, calibration, fabrication`): the user states no amount, type or plate and the reply invents "5,000 KES daily takings for KDA 123B". The corpus had 3 earnings pass cases and **zero** earnings calibration cases; the hold count moves 12 → 13, and `test_conv_eval` now pins that the earnings lane carries a calibration case. Tag decision: `fabrication` (not "invention") — consistency with the 4 existing calibration cases, so the tag family never splits.

- **Two audit corrections (rule 44/52).** The task premise said the aliases "fall to the AI intent lane"; the live tree proved they were claimed EARLIER by the `product_qa` read lane (`matchProductQa('trial status')` → the marketing `trial` QA entry), i.e. the wrong answer with zero AI. And the corpus held **3** earnings pass cases, not 4. Both corrections are recorded in §195.

- **Frames touched.** `src/services/readCommands.js` (`subscription_status` lane + registry order note), `sakaAi.js` (§7c example list + exact-phrase `VIEW_SUBSCRIPTION` alternative), `src/routes/whatsapp/pending.js` (`wantsView`), `src/routes/whatsapp/messages.js` (gate), `src/services/flowKnowledge.js` (`VIEW_SUBSCRIPTION` aliases), `evals/cases/conversation_cases.json` (+1), `test_read_registry.js` (+17), `test_subscription_billing.js` (+3), `test_conv_eval.js` (+1), `package.json`, `docs/` (generated), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§195 + version/history), this file.

- **Suites run.** test_read_registry (121 → 138), test_subscription_billing (101 → 104), test_conv_eval (216 → 217), test_product_qa (152), test_agent_training (180), test_first_message_flow (57), test_doc_sync (30), `npm run health` — green; `npm run eval:conv -- --exclude-calibration` signal 23/23, calibration 13/13.

- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** *Defect class — a phrase claimed by the wrong deterministic lane:* the read registry's ordered claim was audited for every trial-family phrase; the exact-phrase anchor plus the declared overlap (`subscription_status` then `product_qa`) fixes it, and six negatives pin the non-hijack (trial-end window, EXTEND_TRIAL, "trial extension", longer sentences). *Class — a calibration lane with no expected-fail:* the earnings family now has one, modeled on the trial-end/coupon fabrication cases.

## [1.72.1] - 2026-11-07

- **`system_config` keys are namespaced behind a one-release dual window (Phase 8C, P2-6).** A frozen `SYSTEM_CONFIG_KEYS` registry names all 9 fixed keys (plus the dynamic `budget.alert.*` family) with their legacy name, owner, default and boot-critical flag; a single service (`systemConfigService.js`) is now the ONLY code that touches `system_config` (pinned by a drift test). Reads use ONE atomic `.in('key', [namespaced, legacy])` query — namespaced value first, legacy fallback, then the registry default; writes upsert BOTH rows so a mixed-version rolling deploy can never double-fire a dedupe marker. `SYSTEM_CONFIG_KEY_MODE` (`dual` default | `new`) is the transition knob.
- **Boot-critical safety.** The deploy LIVE alert dedupes on `deploy.last_deployed_revision`; the migration **COPYs** legacy rows to their namespaced names and keeps the legacy row (a rename would open a moment where neither key holds the value). Worst case is one duplicate LIVE WhatsApp message, never a blocked boot. `--drop-legacy` is the v1.73.0 teardown.
- **Live migration applied.** `npm run migrate:system-config-keys`: 12 rows read → 10 copies (deploy marker, 3 remediation, 3 eval, 3 legacy budget alerts); re-run: 22 rows, 0 copies (idempotent), audited as `SYSTEM_CONFIG_KEY_MIGRATED`. `saka_core_prompt` had no legacy row (retired in v1.59.0) and the live Sentinel suite proved the new dual-write creates both names.
- **Frames touched.** New: `src/services/systemConfigKeys.js`, `src/services/systemConfigService.js`, `scripts/run_system_config_keys_migration.js`, `test_system_config_keys.js` (37 assertions). Migrated: `sakaAi.js` (prompt READ path only), `src/services/deployLiveAlert.js`, `src/workers/sentinel.js`, `src/workers/remediation.js`, `src/services/evalDigest.js`, `src/services/convEval.js` + `src/workers/convEval.js`, `src/services/superadmin.js`; mocks updated in `test_notifications.js`, `test_agent_training.js`, `test_phase3_resilience.js`, `test_profile_wipe.js`; `package.json` (+`migrate:system-config-keys`), `docs/` (generated), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§194 + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_system_config_keys (37), test_notifications, test_agent_training (180), test_phase3_resilience, test_conv_eval, test_profile_wipe, test_sentinel (live), `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defect class *config access outside a single choke point* -> siblings: every `system_config` call site in `src/` audited and routed through the service (0 raw `.from('system_config')` remain, pinned); the two boot-critical keys live in the registry with `bootCritical: true`; unknown keys are reported, never migrated. Rule 13: `sakaAi.js` read path touched ONLY — prompt content (`DEFAULT_SAKA_CORE_PROMPT`, tone compass, flow playbook, `flowKnowledge.js`) unchanged, test_agent_training still 180 assertions.

## [1.72.0] - 2026-11-07

- **The legacy expense mirror retires behind a flag (Phase 8B, P2-3).** Every expense already writes the canonical `expense_logs` ledger; a legacy `expenses` mirror was written alongside it. Both mirror writes now sit behind `LEGACY_EXPENSES_WRITE_ENABLED` (default `true` = today's behaviour). The three critical legacy reads moved to the canonical ledger first: the write-time duplicate guard now reads `expense_logs` by `vehicle_id` (both writers pass the vehicle they already hold, plate-only callers resolve it), the admin vehicle passbook reads the ledger, and the admin report reads the ledger only.
- **Live bug fixed — the superadmin report was double-counting every expense (PRI-1).** The executive digest summed BOTH `expenses` and `expense_logs`, so every dual-written expense counted twice since the dual-write began. Totals now read `expense_logs` only and match reality. Pinned by a mock assertion: with 999,999 in the legacy table and 150 in the ledger, `total_expenses` is exactly 150 and the legacy table is never queried.
- **No backfill by design.** `expenses` shares no reliable join key with the ledger (plate vs `vehicle_id`, heuristic dates) and dual-write kept it in sync, so it becomes a read-only archival table; wipe/cleanup paths keep deleting from it. The `analytics` fuel-economy legacy fallback is deliberately left for v1.73.0.
- **Frames touched.** `src/services/expenseGuard.js` (mirror flag + `expense_logs`/`vehicle_id` read + plate resolver), `src/routes/whatsapp/commands.js` (vehicleId + flag wrap), `src/services/quizEngine.js` (vehicleId + flag wrap), `src/services/superadmin.js` (report + passbook reads), `test_quiz_flow.js` (+4), `test_superadmin.js` (+3, PRI-1), `test_expense_guard.js` (new, 24 assertions), `.env.example`, `docs/` (generated), `landing/data/changelog.json` (+ stamped assets), `package.json`, `SAKA_FLEET_ARCHITECTURE.md` (§194 + version/history), this file.
- **Suites run.** test_expense_guard (24), test_quiz_flow, test_ocr_flow, test_superadmin (PRI-1 verified), `npm run health` — green.
- **Recurrence autopsy (rule 51).** Defect classes *mirror-table reads drifting from the canonical ledger* and *an aggregate that sums a table and its mirror* -> siblings audited: every quoted `'expenses'` reference classified (2 writes -> flagged; 3 production reads -> migrated/left-with-reason; 1 archival fallback -> documented; 4 cleanup/wipe deletes -> kept); the duplicate guard's two callers both pass `vehicleId` (no plate-only path left in production); the passbook and report are the only superadmin aggregates (both fixed). Rule 13: no intent/flow change — `flowKnowledge.js` / `sakaAi.js` untouched.
